PROVED BY MUTATION IN THREE DIRECTIONS: A (_render_timer discards alpha) caught by arm 1, 4 fails; B (calculate_position floors) caught by arm 3, 0/1653; C (extrapolate ahead) caught by arm 2, 16 fails. B WAS NOT CAUGHT FIRST TIME and that is the most useful line in the ADR -- the arm sampled mid and mid-0.5, which STRADDLE an integer, so floorf(8.0) vs floorf(7.5) still differed and it passed 1644/1644 while its own diagnosis line read 'movers redrew 0 of 240 (0.0%)' beside it; it now samples floor(mid)+0.25 and +0.75, both inside ONE tick. Seed A doubles as a positive control on the diagnosis (real zero-tick frames went to 0 of 191 redrawn). REJECTED: extrapolate ahead (dec. 2); double-buffering the previous tick and lerping between snapshots (unnecessary -- position is an ANALYTIC function of the timer, so evaluating at a fraction IS the interpolation); raising the tick rate to match the display (breaks ROM parity and only moves the beat -- no integer rate is phase-locked to an arbitrary refresh); making frames cheaper (backwards); resetting the accumulator after the loop (destroys ADR-0239's resume); a new test scene; gating PASS on observed zero-tick frames. SOFT SPOTS: S1 AnimationClock still steps on the integer tick. S2 the 144 Hz share is ARITHMETIC from the panel's mode, not a measurement on that panel -- the correlation is measured, the share derived. S3 arm 3 has no positive control for the cliff/pass-through branch, skipped by construction. S4 the one-tick latency is asserted as a BOUND, never measured as a perception. |
| [0293](0293-the-ability-data-is-the-permission-to-land-on-a-corpse-and-an-empty-slot-is-not-one.md) The ability data is the permission to land on a corpse, and an empty slot is not one | `engine` | · | · | 2 | — |
| | | | | | THE ABILITY DATA IS THE PERMISSION TO LAND ON A CORPSE, AND AN EMPTY SLOT IS NOT ONE (#1113, build map #1101). #1102 opened the target POOL so a KO'd? slot could SEE a corpse; the cast then committed, spent the MP, played the animation and no-opped at impact. SIX is_unit_dead gates sit between commit and HP write, not the three the ticket named: the pool (already open), handle_moving_to_cast_state (stage_pathfind), run_cinematic_orchestrator's per-target walk (stage_spell), apply_attack_damage (stage_compute), cast_cinematic_spell's AoE stamp walk and stage_damage Phase 1's AoE walk. THE LAST TWO ARE LEFT STRICT AND THAT IS THE DISCRIMINATING COUNT: 0 of the 5 cancel-Dead abilities carries effect_area > 0, so opening them is a change nothing in the shipped database can exercise. A SEVENTH site is not a filter at all -- cast_instant_spell has no dead gate; what stopped a revive there was its own break/heal/damage fork routing the two revivers that are NOT ABFLAG_HEALING into the damage arm. THE PERMISSION IS ROM DATA ALREADY IN THE SSBO: inflict_mode == CANCEL && inflict_mask bit 0 (Dead), i.e. AB_INFLICT_MODE / AB_INFLICT_MASK, which GPUAbilityLoader already substitutes from the chemist item's own block for an ITEM ability. That names FIVE and only five: Raise (5, ct=4, formula 0x0D y=50), Raise2 (6, ct=10, 0x0D y=100), Revive (107, ct=0, 0x35 y=20, taking_damage), Oink (312, ct=0, 0x35 y=100, taking_damage), PhoenixDown (381, ITEM, 0x4B). dec. 1: no ABFLAG_ was invented, so ADR-0013's deferred-flag guard has nothing new to police. dec. 2: the mask bit is the ABILITY's, not the unit's -- STATUS_DEAD stays hollow and #1105 keeps its call. dec. 3: revive_unit is THE ONE PLACE FLAG_DEAD IS CLEARED, and RERAISE's Stage B was re-routed through it so the count goes one -> one, not one -> two. dec. 4: the HP is the ability's own formula, which required IMPLEMENTING 0x35 -- it fell to the damage=formula_y default, so a Revive restored 20 flat HP instead of 20% of maximum; 0x0D and 0x35 now share an arm because what differs between them is the hit roll and this function returns the amount. RERAISE keeps max_hp/RERAISE_HP_DIVISOR (a lethal-damage capture has no ability to read a formula from); revive_unit clamps into [1, max_hp]. dec. 5: the revive is taken BEFORE the break/heal/damage fork at every landing site, or it would silently cover 3 of 5. dec. 6: four filters open, two stay strict. dec. 7: the VICTORY TALLY NEEDS NO CHANGE and the reason is structural -- check_victory ends a battle only on 0-alive + all-survivors-CELEBRATING, and the reviver is alive, on the corpse's own team (include_ko on an enemy pool is UNSUPPORTED) and in SPELL_CHARGING/ACTING for the whole window, so it adds +1 alive and -1 celebrating to exactly the team whose wipe would end it. dec. 8: AN UNFILLED UNIT SLOT IS NOT A CORPSE -- set_battle_units marks every slot past the roster FLAG_DEAD + HP 0 with the record otherwise ZEROED, U_TEAM included, so a 2v1 in an 8-slot battle carries five phantom team-0 corpses at tile (0,0), and #1102's KO-inclusive pool is the first pool that admits them; is_unit_slot_filled reads U_MAX_HP > 0 and gates both pool walks, paid only on the include_ko path. MEASURED BOTH WAYS from tile (1,0): without the clause the Cleric casts Raise at Unit3 at tick 10, before anything has died, and the real corpse is never revived; with it the slot cannot fire until tick 23. Anywhere mid-map the real corpse wins on path cost and the defect is invisible -- the ADR's own first draft shipped the clause unexercised until the roster was relocated to the corner. dec. 9: a corpse still does not occupy its tile (get_tile_occupant skips FLAG_DEAD). dec. 10: the witness asserts the FLAG, not the hp_event -- a heal at a corpse already writes HP and leaves the unit dead, and the predicate fails loud in BOTH arms when the target never died. REJECTED: a new ABFLAG_MAY_TARGET_KO (the data answers it); routing through apply_inflict_all's CANCEL arm (clears the hollow bit, has no heal amount); reusing the RERAISE cinematic wholesale (timer-driven off a capture, hard-codes divisor and effect id); opening the two AoE walks; a U_REVIVE_PENDING field for the tally; making get_tile_occupant count the dead (more ROM-faithful but moves pathing under the whole suite); U_TEAM = -1 for padding (an enemy pool tests team != my_team, so a sentinel reads as ENEMY and every enemy-side search would admit all five). SOFT SPOTS: S1 PhoenixDown's arm is opened and UNWITNESSED -- it is the only ability reaching filter 3 and Gambit.ActionKind has no ITEM verb. S2 formula 0x4B (Heal_(Rdm(1..9))) is still unimplemented. S3 a one-tick victory-tally window survives on the projectile-deferred path, unreachable per S1. S4 a reviver may stand in the tile it is about to refill. S5 Revive and Oink are admitted on the ability table alone, with no ROM check. S6 a cancel-Dead ability at a LIVING target is unchanged. S7 the revived predicate samples once per host frame while the GPU runs ~25 ticks, so the fixtures carry hp:20/max_hp:2000 to make a percentage revive outlive a frame. |
| [0298](0298-the-rom-says-how-long-a-status-lasts-and-it-gives-sixteen-of-them-a-byte-to-count-it-in.md) The ROM says how long a status lasts, and it gives sixteen of them a byte to count it in | `engine` | · | · | 0 | `0013`, `0236`, `0251` |
| | | | | | THE ROM SAYS HOW LONG A STATUS LASTS, AND IT GIVES SIXTEEN OF THEM A BYTE TO COUNT IT IN (#1116). #1105 measured a complete decay CONSUMER and no PRODUCER: `set_status_with_timer` had zero call sites, so every status inflicted in a battle was permanent and GPUStatusDecayTest was green because it SEEDED a countdown and never touched the producer. The ticket offered a per-status table, a per-ability ROM field if one exists, or a global scalar; one exists. dec. 1: the duration is the ROM's -- status-attribute table, 40 records x 0x10 at SCUS 0x80065DE7, byte 0, in CT units, extracted by tools/extract_status_attributes.py and mirrored in StatusRegistry.DEFAULT_CT + combat_common's `status_default_ct`, held equal BOTH WAYS by StatusRegistryTest::_test_duration_parity (the existing bit-parity arm regexes `const int STATUS_*` and cannot see a switch arm). THREE SITES FIX THE SHAPE AND IT TAKES ALL THREE: Initialize_Status_Check_Data @0x80059854 fixes count 40 + stride 0x10; Status_CT_Set @0x8005DB70 (`sll 4` then `lbu 0x5DE7(at)`) fixes the record BASE one byte below the flag bytes -- align to ...DE8 instead and the column shifts one status off its owner, reading Sleep=24/DontMove=60/DeathSentence=0xF2; the same function's `sb 0x5D(unit + status - 0x18)` behind `sltiu 0x10` fixes WHO CAN BE TIMED. dec. 2: ZERO IS A ROM ANSWER -- 16 of 40 statuses have a countdown byte and 24 do not, so Darkness/Silence/Oil/Frog/Confusion/Berserk/Petrify/BloodSuck/Float/Reraise/Transparent are permanent until cancelled; 11 of the 16 have a bit here, Wall/Innocent/Charm/Reflect/DeathSentence are #99's and their slots are reserved. dec. 3: one CT is TURN_METER_FULL/100 = 36 ticks, DERIVED from ADR-0236's meter and not the 30 an ability's charge CT uses (different quantity, and the two being close is an accident of 3600). dec. 4: the countdowns are STATUS-ADDRESSED -- 16 16-bit counters in the same 8 U_STATUS_TIMER ints, slot = ROM's `status - 24`; the old first-come pool had a ninth case the ROM does not (a flag set with no slot left, permanent and silent), so the ticket's 'what happens when the 8 slots are full' is DELETED rather than answered. dec. 5: the producer is apply_inflict_all's ALL branch -- ONE write of the whole mask then one armed countdown per bit, because set_status reads CURRENT and writes NEXT so per-bit sets would keep only the last. dec. 6: read/write_status_timer use the NEXT buffer -- two counters share an int (poison+regen in TIMER_0), so a CURRENT-buffer RMW undoes its neighbour; same reasoning stage_compute's TRANSPARENT consume already writes out. dec. 7: set_status_with_timer is DELETED, not replaced by a second never-called helper. dec. 8: a cinematic pause FREEZES a countdown (U_PAUSED early-returns before tick_status_timers) and that is faithful -- FFT's clock stops for the animation; measured ~107 ticks on the witness. dec. 9: the witness is GPUStatusDecayTest, UN-SKIPPED -- it was red in skip_tests.tsv since #542 ('HASTE clears 8 ticks off a +/-3 tolerance') and THE INSTRUMENT WAS WRONG, NOT THE RULE: the bit is sampled per FRAME and a frame banks as much wall clock as it took, so the window measured the box. One-sided now, plus the arm the suite never had -- a status INFLICTED in battle whose countdown is read out of the buffer and must be 864 = 24 CT x 36, clearing on its own -- plus an untimed control that must stay set. dec. 10: duration is NOT a named lever yet (#1106) because the table is in the shader; what changed is that the baseline a lever would scale is the ROM's. EVIDENCE: static met and mechanistically complete (three sites + the extractor's own assertion that the 16 with a CT are exactly the 16 with a slot + the values match FFT's documented durations); dynamic met for THIS KERNEL (the GPU witness) and NOT met for the ROM -- no PCSX run watches Haste's 32 count down, and the decrementer's caller (BATTLE.BIN 0x80182CBC) is identified and unwalked. REJECTED: a per-status table of our own numbers (invention, and invisible as such); a single global scalar (cannot express Sleep 60 vs Stop 20 vs DeathSentence 3 -- it would have flattened a shape the ROM has); a per-ability duration field (effects.json has none and the ROM's is per-STATUS); keeping the 8-slot pool with an overflow policy (the shape was wrong, and every policy adjudicates a case FFT cannot reach); converting with the ability-CT 30; a new test scene (the charter prices processes); widening the unit block. SOFT SPOTS: S1 poison is still lethal and the INTERVAL is why (36 CT = 43 bleed ticks here vs ~3 of the unit's turns in FFT); S2 two CT conversions now live in the kernel; S3 the armed value is witnessed for ONE status (Slow) and the other ten rows rest on parity; S4 the pause freeze is unbounded; S5 five timed statuses have no bit; S6 Death Sentence's no-re-arm carve-out is not ported; S7 a cross-unit arming race inside stage_compute (pre-existing for the flags write at the same site); S8 byte +0x0F of the ROM record is an order column this decode does not explain (39 of 40 distinct in 0..39, the 40th reads 0xF7). |
| [0299](0299-the-rom-prices-a-separate-inflict-at-a-flat-24-percent-because-its-inflict-record-has-nowhere-to-put-a-probability.md) The ROM prices a separate inflict at a flat 24%, because its inflict record has nowhere to put a probability | `engine` | · | · | 0 | `0013`, `0049`, `0278`, `0298` |
| | | | | | THE ROM PRICES A SEPARATE INFLICT AT A FLAT 24%, BECAUSE ITS INFLICT RECORD HAS NOWHERE TO PUT A PROBABILITY (#1117). `apply_inflict_all` implemented ALL and CANCEL and fell off the end for RANDOM and SEPARATE while StatusEncoder translated all four faithfully, so 27 of the 155 status-bearing abilities inflicted nothing -- MimicDaravon, GalaxyStop, the Bio family, every separate-mode monster ability -- with no warning at encode time and no branch at apply time. The ticket asked for the per-status probability; there isn't one. dec. 1: SEPARATE'S CHANCE IS A CONSTANT AND IT IS 24, cited to `pass_fail_roll(100, 0x18)` at BATTLE.BIN 0x801880E8 whose PASSING branch XORs the status back out -- `InflictStatusList` (SCUS 0x80063FC4) is 128 entries of SIX bytes, one mode flag plus a five-byte status set, so there is no probability column to read and the ability's own hit rate is spent upstream deciding whether the inflict site is reached at all. A nine-status set lands 2.16 statuses on average. dec. 2: RANDOM IS UNIFORM OVER THE BITS PRESENT, so a mask short of the ROM's set skews the pick rather than losing a status; measured, Muramasa and Nightmare are the two affected records (both list Death Sentence, which has no bit) and their pick of two is currently a pick of one -- #1119's debt, named so it does not read as a bug here. dec. 3: THE MODE RESOLVES TO A MASK AND THEN THERE IS ONE APPLY -- `resolve_inflict_mask` narrows, `apply_inflict_all` writes, mirroring FUN_80187F24 which filters its scratch copy in place before ORing it onto the unit. That is what keeps #1116's producer correct: countdowns are armed off the RESOLVED mask, so a status a SEPARATE roll dropped cannot get a countdown for a flag it never set. dec. 4: THE RNG IS SALTED AND THE SALT IS LOAD-BEARING -- `rand_int` folds (battle_seed, slot, tick) and the status hit roll at the same (caster, target, tick) already seeds on caster*100+target, so an unsalted inflict roll would be that same draw re-read at a new modulus; INFLICT_RNG_SALT = 7919 and SEPARATE walks it per bit. dec. 5: `apply_inflict_all` KEEPS ITS NAME even though `_all` is now wrong three ways -- renaming orphans citations in three ADRs, five docs and a test for no behavioural gain. dec. 6: THE CONTRADICTION IS CAUGHT AT ENCODE TIME AND THE PAIR IS THE UNIT -- `StatusEncoder.inflict_for_record` resolves mask and mode together and push_errors on an unknown mode or a mask with no mode; both encode sites route through it. A ratchet on a clean tree: zero violations across every ability and both item blocks, and the ROM's own 128 entries have none either (no entry sets statuses with a zero mode byte, none sets two mode bits, none uses the low nibble). dec. 7: WHAT THIS DOES NOT FIX IS NAMED, NOT COUNTED -- Blaster (f61), CrushPunch (f45) and BloodSuck (f71) carry a mask and still reach no inflict site, and SplitPunch encodes to an empty mask. dec. 3b: BOTH RESOLVE LOOPS WALK THE SET BITS AND THAT IS A COMPILE-TIME DECISION -- `resolve_inflict_mask` inlines at nine sites, so a 0..31 loop is a 32x unrolled body at every one; the first version cost +2.5 s on a COLD pipeline build (11.1 s vs 8.8 s for a comment-only control at the same commit) and 11 s of async warm-up outlives a short test's quit(), which scored GambitDeploymentPickerTest THREW in both repeats on a freed-autoload read AFTER its own [PASS]. Walking set bits (`m &= m - 1` / findLSB) makes the trip count the statuses LISTED (1-9, never 32): 10.1-10.2 s and 103/103 across two repeats. The teardown race is latent in ANY shader edit and is NOT fixed here. MEASURED: the 128-entry table is 75 all / 18 cancel / 15 separate / 10 random / 10 reserved; effects.json is 114 / 14 / 16 / 11. WITNESS `tests/GPUInflictModeTest.gd`, four arms in one battle -- LookofDevil set exactly one of five every run; GrandCross over three targets in ONE cast gave 2/3/4, 2/0/0 and 3/0/0 of nine. Both seeds verified: RANDOM-as-ALL reds arm 1 only, SEPARATE-as-ALL reds arm 3 only -- and the second seed is why the test has a settle window, because the first version snapshotted at the first bit and the ALL seed PASSED it (cast_cinematic_spell gives each in-radius target its own fire frame, so the third had not been hit yet). REJECTED: deriving the per-status chance from the ability's hit rate (the intuitive reading of 'separate', and it would make GrandCross inflict all nine every time since f56 rolls nothing); quartering the hit chance as well as rolling 24% (the literal transcription of `[action+0x2A] >>= 2`, which double-counts -- that halfword is a display/AI accumulator never rolled against anywhere in BATTLE.BIN, and the quarter exists BECAUSE of the 24%); a per-ability probability field (six bytes, one of them the mode); failing loud at APPLY time (a shader cannot raise, and after this the unhandled-mode set is empty); a rate band measuring the 24% directly (six tests are already skipped for that shape, #539). SOFT SPOTS: S1 the 24 is STATIC-ONLY -- a PCSX arm breaking on 0x801880E8 would settle it and has not been run. S2 arms 3 and 4 of the witness are probabilistic, P(false red) = 0.76^27 = 8.2e-4, and that number assumes all three targets are in the blast -- CONTROLLED by three ALL-seeded runs, not assumed, because placement is not pinned. S3 RANDOM's uniformity is asserted structurally: a pick that always returned the lowest set bit would pass. S4 dec. 2's skew is live and nothing fails when #1119 removes it. |
| [0301](0301-retreat-is-one-tile-directly-away-and-then-a-fresh-decision.md) Retreat is one tile directly away, and then a fresh decision | `engine` | · | · | 5 | `0023`, `0024`, `0048`, `0062`, `0224`, `0275` |
| | | | | | RETREAT IS ONE TILE DIRECTLY AWAY, AND THEN A FRESH DECISION (#1103). ADR-0062 deleted RETREAT from Gambit.ActionKind because "retreat" named no single referent -- a corner, a near ally, away from the nearest enemy were all candidates -- and it rejected the farthest-from-enemy tile selector as needing "a battlefield/distance-field scan that does not exist." Both halves have expired: #1103 is a wayfinder:grilling ticket and the user answered it verbatim in two sittings ("find the nearest enemy / pick the direction opposite the vector to them / move there / reevaluate gambits like normal", then "retreat is one tile then reevaluate"), and DistanceFieldGenerator now precomputes an ALL-PAIRS path field that get_distance reads in one indexed load. dec. 1: RETREAT IS A VERB AGAIN and APPROACH stays deleted -- an approach IS MOVE aimed at the thing approached, but every target MOVE can take is somewhere to move TOWARD, so the sign is the verb and a target cannot carry it. dec. 2: IT IS A DESTINATION, NOT A TARGET TYPE -- no FURTHEST resolution is added; the action_target names the unit to move away from and routes through the same _target_selector_to_gpu block every other action uses, so RETREAT inherits the supported/UNSUPPORTED target set for free. #1103's Q3 (whether FURTHEST earns its place independently) is untouched. dec. 3: ACTION_RETREAT_STEP IS A NEW GPU PRIMITIVE, not ACTION_MOVE_TO with a near destination and not ACTION_MOVE_TO_UNIT with the field inverted -- both enter a walking state that OUTLIVES the step, and handle_moving_state opens with "is anyone attackable? then stop and swing", so a retreat in LOGICAL_ACTIVITY_WALKING breaks off into an attack on the unit it is fleeing. MEASURED as the witness's seeded break: the WALKING substitution reds four of five arms, one of them by walking the fleeing unit back toward the threat. dec. 4: LOGICAL_ACTIVITY_RETREATING (value 10, APPENDED so no committed U_STATE value moves), Display WALKING, routing visualizer -- the split is about what the SIMULATION may do next, the same reason APPROACHING exists beside WALKING. dec. 5: THE RULE -- preferred direction is the dominant component of (me - them), ties to x; the cell must be traversable, within jump, unoccupied and STRICTLY further by path distance; otherwise a four-neighbour scan takes the cell that opens the most, still strictly; otherwise there is no retreat. The preferred direction wins even when a perpendicular step would open MORE -- the answer says "opposite the vector", not "maximise the field", and maximising is what FURTHEST would do. "Strictly greater" is also the anti-oscillation rule, which is why a retreat needs no U_PREV_MOVE_POS memory: a monotone sequence cannot step back onto a cell it left. dec. 6: A CELL THE THREAT CANNOT PATH TO AT ALL IS EXCLUDED, not treated as infinitely far -- the field cannot tell perfect cover from an isolated ledge, and stranding a unit is the worse error. dec. 7: THE CELL IS PICKED IN THE DECIDE STAGE SO A CORNERED RETREAT FALLS THROUGH -- execute_gambit_action runs retreat_step_cell before committing the slot and returns VERDICT_NO_RETREAT (12, taking GambitVerdictReader.EXPECTED_CODE_COUNT to 13) when there is no cell, so the walk reaches the slots below and a cornered unit still gets its Attack row. Deferring the pick to stage_pathfind like every other movement action would leave it committed to a retreat it cannot take, re-deciding into the same wall every TICKS_GAMBIT_REEVAL forever. dec. 8: A SELF-AIMED RETREAT IS REFUSED IN THE KERNEL, not only on the surface -- get_distance from a cell to itself is 0, every neighbour beats 0, and the strictness test would wave through an arbitrary direction forever; that is a random walk, not a no-op, and the rollout fleet and the raw command path both bypass GambitOptions. dec. 9: THE PROSE SAYS "Retreat from" -- every other verb takes its target as the thing acted ON, so "Retreat Nearest Foe" reads as retreating TOWARD them. dec. 10: #1103's Q4 ("what stops a retreat loop?", the ticket's own "probably the hardest one here") is DISSOLVED, not answered: there is no committed multi-tile flee to bound, so the gambit's own condition holds or releases it, one tile at a time. READ RATHER THAN ASSUMED: U_MOVE is declared in combat_common.glslinc and READ BY NO SHADER, so #1103's other open question (whole move budget or one tile of it) has no budget to spend -- movement is priced per step by get_move_ticks. WITNESS tests/GPURetreatStepTest.gd, five arms in one battle, two team-0 units that are each other's control: distance never falls; the unit opens >= 2 tiles (so the step REPEATS); it enters neither ACTING nor WALKING; a self-aimed retreat falls through to slot 1 and is seen APPROACHING; and the unit settles to IDLE once its condition goes false. Both seeds verified. REJECTED: composing retreat from MOVE plus a repulsive target (no such target exists -- the sign would be a flag, which is a verb in a costume); inverting the sign inside scan_adjacent_moves (shared by every move in the game, and the GambitBattle map's bar forbids changing how ordinary movement scores tiles); a multi-tile flee with a bound (rejected by the answer, and it is what made Q4 hard); letting a cornered retreat fail in the pathfind body like a failed path (a unit backed into a corner has to be able to fight). SOFT SPOTS: S1 the terrain FALLBACK branch is covered by reading, not running -- MapComposer's open procedural map always offers the preferred cell, and MEASURED, dropping the strictness test leaves all five arms green; reaching it needs authored terrain, which is #795's fixture work. S2 the settle brake, the turn brake and settle_awaited_state all gained RETREATING; omitting it from the last would let a battle report a win while a retreating sprite teleports the rest of its step, and nothing tests that directly. FOUND IN PASSING, NOT FIXED: a unit spawned on cell (0,0) loses exactly 5 HP on tick 3 -- measured with all three units holding Wait gambits, flat 5 whatever the PA/WP/max HP, no ACTING state and no damage carrier anywhere in the battle, gone the moment the unit moves off that cell. GPUMoveToUnitTest has carried it since ADR-0062 because the unit it puts there is the one whose HP it never asserts. |

Two tests read the ENTD directly so no filter can hide a slot again. |
| [0284](0284-an-entd-equipment-byte-is-a-sentinel-three-times-in-four-and-the-job-default-is-the-resolver.md) An ENTD equipment byte is a sentinel three times in four, and the job default is the resolver | `engine` | · | · | 1 | — |
| | | | | | AN ENTD EQUIPMENT BYTE IS A SENTINEL THREE TIMES IN FOUR, AND THE JOB DEFAULT IS THE RESOLVER (#1121, build map #1101). The item table is 0..0xFD (items.json holds 254 records, ids 0-253) and three non-item values dominate the five ENTD equipment bytes. Over the 847 slots EntdBattle.combatant_slots actually deploys -- NOT the raw job!=0 count of 5,657, which includes empty-slot padding and the event-join units the port already drops -- right_hand is 0xFE on 529, 0xFF on 117, 0x00 on 101 and an explicit item on only 100; of the 720 HUMAN slots, 506 are 0xFE. 0xFE = 'the game fills this in', the same sentinel level/month/day/bravery/faith/experience use (410 of those 720 slots carry level==0xFE too, 311 of them the same slots). 0xFF = empty slot. 0x00 = the literal <Nothing> record, what monsters carry (2,746 of 3,104 monster slots), passed through. DYNAMICALLY CLOSED OFFLINE from a PCSX savestate, no emulator launch: battle unit table at 0x801908CC stride 0x1C0, 21 slots; join key unit+0x161 = the ENTD unit_id (🔴 CORRECTED by ADR-0289: this row and the ADR originally said unit+0x02, which is the player-ROSTER index, so the original join read party members as the record's enemies -- re-censused on the right key, 64 distinct generated units, every explicit id verbatim, 0xFE drawing a SPREAD of real ids per slot and resolving to EMPTY in 10 of 20 accessory observations, so the 'Battle Boots' row was a player unit's); equipment at +0x1A head / +0x1B body / +0x1C accessory / +0x1D right hand / +0x20 left hand with 0xFF = empty, that column map established by item-id RANGE legality over 571 live-unit observations with zero violations. Aligning just_before_last_enemy_magic_city.sstate to ENTD record 258 (all five bytes 0xFE) the units hold Leather Hat, Clothes, Battle Boots and a Dagger or Broad Sword -- all enemy_level 1 on level-1 units, and two same-job slots drew DIFFERENT weapons, so the ROM's pick is a RANDOM DRAW from the qualifying set, not the deterministic 'highest' the research doc asserted. 🔴 CORRECTS ITEM_AVAILABILITY_TIMELINE.md AND THE TICKET THAT QUOTED IT: 0x801950CC is NOT the equipment resolver, it is the AI's THROW/ITEM usability gate -- its loop runs ability ids 0x17F-0x188 = Knife, Sword, Hammer, Katana, NinjaSword, Axe, Spear, Stick, KnightSword, Dictionary (the ten Throw classes, bracketed by 0x17E Shuriken and 0x189 Ball), and the branch selector unit+0x1BA&0x30 routes a player unit to the party item ledger at 0x800596E0 instead. No BATTLE.BIN code resolves equipment from rec[2]; the resolver's address is STILL UNLOCATED. THE PORT'S DEFECT: _seed_equipment_from_slot skipped 0xFF and 0 and wrote everything else through, so 0xFE put ITEM ID 254 in the slot -- get_item(254) returns {} and is_weapon(254) is false, so the unit reached the kernel at WP 0 / range 1 / weapon_type 0 / weapon_id -1, SILENTLY, and it OVERWROTE the job default create_default had already seeded, making the majority sentinel the only one that destroyed the fallback: 123 of 720 deployed human slots held a weapon record, 597 did not. dec. 1: all three of 0, 0xFE and 0xFF are sentinels and all three are skipped (123 -> 318 of 720 armed). dec. 2: 0xFE is spelled ENTD_RANDOMISE, the constant Character already had for the stat bytes. dec. 3: Character.get_starting_weapon IS the port's enemy-equipment resolver and the ROM picker is NOT PORTED -- it is a random draw and it needs a level 410 of 720 slots do not carry; a fixed job default is a deterministic sample of that draw and matched the ROM's tier on every generic slot of the battle closed dynamically (exact on two of four). dec. 4: every id in that table is a weapon id, verified by NAME against items.json -- four were wrong, each comment naming the right item and the number naming another: Archer 81 is Hunting Bow not Long Bow (83), Lancer 97 is Papyrus Plate not Javelin (99), Samurai 113 is Octagon Rod not Asura Knife (38), and Ninja 129 is BUCKLER, A SHIELD, which is_weapon rejects outright so a Ninja fought bare-handed. dec. 5: a rig seated on real rosters RECORDS which policy armed each unit, because the substitute is not the ROM. dec. 6: the table covers 12 jobs and the gap is measured not silent -- 303 of the 506 0xFE human slots carry an uncovered story job and stay bare-handed. REJECTED: porting the picker faithfully (random draw over a level-gated set whose level is itself a sentinel -- a rig whose rosters change run to run cannot referee a lever); treating 0xFE as 'no weapon' (the opposite of what the ROM does); leaving the write-through and guarding downstream (every consumer would need the same guard and each would fail silently the same way). SOFT SPOTS: S1 the real resolver was not located, only its effect. S2 0xFF vs 0xFE is closed statically only -- no savestate maps to a record carrying 0xFF on a HUMAN slot. S3 unit+0x1E and +0x1F are unexplained, yet the left hand is at +0x20. S4 the job table's fidelity is a sample of one level-1 battle. S5 level 0xFE resolves to 1 in the port where the ROM scales it to the party, so 410 slots fight the rig at level 1. |
| [0289](0289-the-entd-level-byte-is-not-a-level-and-resolving-it-is-only-half-the-fix.md) The ENTD level byte is not a level, and resolving it is only half the fix | `engine` | · | · | 5 | `0284` |
| | | | | | THE ENTD LEVEL BYTE IS NOT A LEVEL, AND RESOLVING IT IS ONLY HALF THE FIX (#1179, build map #1101). Over the 847 slots EntdBattle.combatant_slots deploys the level byte is 0xFE on 506, an explicit 1-99 on 336 and >=100 on 5 (101,105,105,110,115); the port resolved 0xFE to 1, so the majority of the deployed cast fought at the bottom of the curve. THE RESOLVER IS IN SCUS, NOT BATTLE.BIN -- the ENTD record is consumed before the battle loop, which is why #1121 could not find it: BATTLE 0x8017f8a0 entd_to_roster_loader_16 walks the 16 40-byte slots and hands each to SCUS 0x8005a9b4. SCUS 0x8005cbd0 computes the ceiling P = max level over the player's TWENTY-slot roster at 0x80057F74 stride 0x100 (byte +0x16 is the level, +0x01 == 0xFF marks an empty slot) clamped to 99, stored at DAT_80066308 and recomputed at every battle load -- the WHOLE BARRACKS, not the deployed party. SCUS 0x8005add4 then resolves the byte: 0 or 0xFE -> uniform random over the P/8+1 levels in [P - P/8, P] via BIOS A(2Fh) rand(); 1..99 -> verbatim; >=100 -> P + (byte - 100); then 0 -> 1 and >=100 -> 99, written to unit+0x22. SCUS 0x8005b880 then GROWS the raw stats -- for L = 2..level, raw += raw/(C + L - 1) over the same x16384 fixed point -- gated on unit+0x02 == 0xFE, the 'generated, not save-loaded' discriminator. That walk is byte-for-byte UnitProgression.level_up() repeated. DYNAMICALLY CLOSED OFFLINE over 94 savestates: the roster max equals the stored DAT_80066308 in every battle state, and 31 distinct sentinel observations land 28 inside [P - P/8, P] -- before_bridge gives 87/90/91/92/94/96 at P=99, the spread being the draw showing itself; all 336 explicit bytes reproduce verbatim; the 3 outliers are unit_ids 4 and 7 (Ramza, Agrias) at level 1-2, which the ROM loads from the save roster instead of generating. 🔴 CORRECTS ADR-0284's JOIN KEY: unit+0x02 is NOT an enemy's ENTD slot index, it is the player-ROSTER index for a save-loaded unit and 0xFF for a generated one; the ENTD unit_id is at unit+0x161, as fft-ghidra's own label for entd_to_roster_loader_16 already said. ADR-0284's conclusions survive and are better supported on the corrected key (64 distinct generated units, zero cross-column violations, 0xFE drawing a spread of real items), but its 'Battle Boots' accessory row came from a PLAYER unit -- on generated units accessory 0xFE resolved to EMPTY in 10 of 20 observations. AND THE PORT'S LEVEL WAS DECORATIVE ANYWAY: create_default seeds raw stats at the level-1 base and only level_up() grows them, so all 847 deployed slots fought at base stats, not just the 506 sentinel ones. dec. 1: the byte is a three-branch field and Character.resolve_entd_level implements all three plus the clamps; 0 is the SAME sentinel as 0xFE. dec. 2: the sentinel resolves to the window's MIDPOINT, not a draw -- ADR-0284 dec. 3's reproducibility argument on the same sentinel. dec. 3: the ceiling is the RECORD's own max authored level (only 75 of the 174 records with deployed slots author one), else Character.entd_level_ceiling, a static var defaulting to 23, the ROM's own median authored deployed level (mean 22.2). dec. 4: from_entd_slot GROWS to the resolved level via _grow_progression_to, else the decision moves nothing a shader can see. dec. 5: the ceiling a corpus ran under is part of the corpus (ADR-0284 dec. 5's sibling). dec. 6: a caller holding a whole cast passes the record ceiling; -1 means the corpus parameter. CONSEQUENCES, measured in three arms over all 847 slots: level mean 10.04 -> 23.47, median 1 -> 22, slots at level 1 560 -> 54; sum effective HP 30,857 base -> 56,482 (x1.83) growing the OLD levels -> 109,523 (x3.55) resolved and grown; sum effective PA 4,258 -> 5,045 (x1.18) -> 6,375 (x1.50). Roughly half the move is the missing growth rather than the sentinel, and #1110 and #1111 must both run post-fix. REJECTED: porting the random draw (reproducibility); deriving the ceiling from the opposing side's mean (circular -- 99 of 174 records author no level at all); keeping 0xFE -> 1 (the one value the ROM produces only when the player's roster is empty); resolving the level and leaving the stats at base; threading a battle seed down to from_entd_slot (rejected for cost, not principle). SOFT SPOTS: S1 the corpus ceiling is a parameter, not a derivation -- there is no roster in the rig for the ROM's rule to read. S2 the >=100 branch has ZERO dynamic observations. S3 the midpoint erases variance the ROM has, so the rig's fights are more uniform than FFT's by construction. S4 the port has no save-roster override, so it scales every ENTD slot alike. S5 the growth walk matches the ROM's divisors statically but was not compared against a high-level savestate. |

| [0189](0189-the-unit-sprite-is-a-module-and-a-consumer-asks-for-a-variant.md) The unit sprite is a module, and a consumer asks for a variant | `engine` | interface | · | 10 | `0129`, `0131`, `0139`, `0141`, `0146` |
| | | | | | BUILT 2026-08-26 test-first on refactor/render-system -- decisions 1-7 landed in five commits; decision 8 BUILT 2026-08-27 as tools/check_unit_shader_paths.py -- the rule as WRITTEN could not be built: its `unit*.gdshader` glob also matches unit_portrait_3d.gdshader, which is booked UI and correctly named by UIPortrait.gd, so the guard names the three variant paths explicitly; comments are stripped because ten .gd files cross-reference a path in prose; and ScenarioDeadUnitFadeTest is ALLOWED because it is the independent oracle for the additive swap and routing it through the module would make it compare the module to itself. UI and Cutscene do not need a different sprite compositor -- SpriteLayerManager.initialize(anim_set, shader_material) takes the material from the CALLER (5 production sites), so the only way to change how a unit sprite blends is to reach in and replace .shader, which FormationScene.gd:2996-2998 and ScenarioVM.gd:3038 each do in one line. The 1,018-line src/ui3/shaders/formation_unit.gdshader is what that hole LEAKS, not the defect: 19 of its 21 function bodies are byte-identical to unit_sprite_body.gdshaderinc (558 of 619 code lines), only vertex and fragment differ, and its own header asks a human to re-sync the fork. Sprite Rig publishes UnitMaterial.for_variant(OPAQUE|ADDITIVE|FLAT); no file outside it names a unit shader path. THE #define WAS REFUSED ON A GUARD, NOT ON TASTE: formation_unit writes POSITION with no PAR helper and is 1 of 11 BURN_DOWN entries in check_par_shaders.py, so under #define UNIT_FLAT_SCREEN the un-PAR'd write relocates INTO the shared include (battle path, already holds the PAR'd write -> two writes, one file, no preprocessor -> red) while formation_unit, now writing no POSITION, reads as a STALE BURN_DOWN entry -- also red. The debt would be laundered, not paid. So the include stops owning vertex/fragment and publishes four names (billboard, unit_paint, unit_colour, unit_light); UNIT_ADDITIVE and its #ifdef are deleted. The body stays Sprite Rig's in assets/shaders/ -- a third #include is not an admission argument (ADR-0139 dec. 2) and CONTEXT.md's Shader library entry already books unit_sprite_body Tier 2; the kernel refuses it on ADR-0146 dec. 3 (implemented once, in GLSL, no CPU counterpart -- shared implementation, not a codec). ONE FILE MOVES: src/ui3/shaders/formation_unit.gdshader -> assets/shaders/unit_flat.gdshader with its .uid; unit.gdshader is NOT renamed (path+uid refs in unit.tres, Unit.tscn, SequenceViewer.tscn, ScenarioVM.gd and 2 tests, for symmetry alone). Staying in src/ui3/shaders/ was refused because that rule is a PREFIX and a Sprite Rig file there needs a per-file exception ahead of it -- ADR-0141's src/data/ defect. SPRITE RIG GAINS A PART, modulation: cj_* belongs to NEITHER system's Parts as written (not a pose/facing/quadrant/equipment/position, so BLUEPRINT §4 misses it; and §6 gives UI the commit cadence, so it reads as UI's). Booked to Sprite Rig on ORDERING: the gouraud corner tint must land between psx_color_apply and ambient_brightness, and only the pipeline's owner can guarantee that. UI owns WHEN and WITH WHAT CORNERS. The colour-stack route is BLOCKED not unattractive -- ColorRecipe's affine is uniform over the surface, this is bilinear over four corners, a third recipe SHAPE, and the discriminator is a two-way rgb1[i].w == 0.0 test with no spare encoding (9-11 files for one caller). The nine cj_* uniforms live in unit_flat.gdshader, NOT the shared body: Godot surfaces every declared uniform on the ShaderMaterial parameter list, so the shared body would hang nine dead params off unit.tres and every battle unit material. psx_par.gdshaderinc moves out of the shared body into the two entry shaders that call psx_par_anchor. GUARDS: check_par_shaders BURN_DOWN entry is REPATHED not converted -- an exemption is arguable on the guard's own docstring but 10 of 11 entries are the other formation shaders and re-judging one leaves the group incoherent (recorded as an observation, not acted on). check_color_shaders.py:66 REQUIRED_CONSUMERS names 3 files but FIVE entry .gdshader reach psx_color_apply (unit, unit_additive, indexed_color, screen_background, formation_unit) -- TWO are unnamed, not one, and formation_unit is additionally unaddressable because the positive loop resolves under assets/shaders/ only; the move fixes both. classify_blueprint gains unit_flat.gdshader -> Sprite Rig and re-books unit_additive.gdshader Cutscene -> Sprite Rig. OPEN (dec. 8): the ~40-line anti-regrowth guard (no .gd outside Sprite Rig names res://assets/shaders/unit*.gdshader) is NOT decided. REOPENS ADR-0129 dec. 4 for this case only -- 'a producer keeps its own shader' was written about fold producers taking Render's generic library, and this is a system taking another system's WHOLE compositor; dec. 4's standing for fold producers is untouched. CENSUS WARNING: 1,018 lines leave UI and ~560 cease to exist, so per ADR-0131 the measurement must not ride with a commit quoting the old numbers. NOT CLAIMED: the three entry shaders do NOT merge (render_mode must sit in the entry .gdshader; Godot has no runtime blend switch) -- the deliverable is that FormationScene and ScenarioVM stop containing a shader PATH. |

| [0286](0286-extraction-7-is-the-effects-runtime-and-the-studio-that-is-seventy-percent-of-the-bucket-is-a-root-set-that-stays.md) Extraction #7 is the `Effects` runtime, and the studio that is seventy percent of the bucket is a root set that stays | `method` | · | · | 0 | `0035`, `0110`, `0112`, `0115`, `0126`, `0131`, `0134`, `0140`, `0141`, `0148`, `0157`, … |
| | | | | | EXTRACTION #7 IS THE EFFECTS RUNTIME, AND THE STUDIO THAT IS SEVENTY PERCENT OF THE BUCKET IS A ROOT SET THAT STAYS (ADR-0126 pass 1; runs parallel with extraction #6, #1025). The selection instrument reports Effects at 191 files / 58,124 lines, arm 7 = 3 names / 21 lines -- and BOTH HALVES OF THAT NUMBER ARE SOMEBODY ELSE'S. 18 of the 21 arm-7 lines sit in src/debug/{EffectViewerPanel,TrapViewerPanel,FireCastReproPanel}.gd naming TuneField (15) and BaseDebugPanel (3): the host debug window reaching its own base class, booked to Effects because classify() books a file to its CONSUMER (ADR-0243 dec. 3). That is ADR-0257's artefact firing a SECOND time, on a system it was not derived from, unamended -- the control arm ADR-0257 could not run on itself. And 69.9% of the bucket is an authoring tool: src/effects/studio/ is 116 files / 40,623 lines, EffectStudioPage.gd alone is 7,946 lines in 186 of the last 495 commits. dec. 1: #7 is Effects and every number is RE-TAKEN at 2b55e3f81 (the handoff's blocker #1059 phases 1-2 already merged, PRs #1088/#1095; arm 7 is subject-keyed so the tier fix could not move it). dec. 2: THE MEMBERSHIP IS THE RUNTIME -- 69 files / 16,505 lines: src/effects/*.gd (45), src/effects/callbacks/ (12), assets/shaders/{effect_*,trap_charge_line} (12). Its outbound debt into an unextracted system is THREE LINES -- caster:Unit,target:Unit in EffectManager's three spawn_* signatures -- at 0.018%, against Sprite Rig's 6 lines / 0.112% (#4) and Battlefield's 10 / 0.121% (#3). The best reading the method has taken. dec. 3: the six src/debug/ panels are NOT members (ADR-0257, ADR-0035). dec. 4: src/effects/studio/ is not in this extraction and ADR-0134 dec. 4 is UPHELD, not narrowed -- classification and membership are DIFFERENT QUESTIONS; the studio stays classified Effects, is NOT re-parked in assembler (ADR-0134 dec. 2: anything there is permanently exempt from extraction), and the partial extraction is the Audio shape at six times the scale. dec. 5: 'Effect Studio last' is upheld and made precise -- the runtime extracts at #7, the studio stays, and its 39 code lines + 32 res:// paths into the runtime become an ordinary inbound edge, SCHEDULED rather than discovered. dec. 6: src/effects/EffectScoreModel.gd names EmitterFieldRelevance (3) and EmitterChannel (2) -- a BACKWARDS EDGE, the runtime naming its editor, and the one finding that BLOCKS the move; invisible to all_shapes, found only because arm7() tests set membership. dec. 7: DebugConfig on 61 lines across 19 of 69 files is NAMED not absorbed (ADR-0141 makes Debug a non-counting sink; ADR-0175 dec. 2 / ADR-0187 is the port that answers it). dec. 8: the inbound seam is 44 lines over ~12 addresses once studio and panels are set aside -- PSXCameraConvert 16, PsxUnits 5, CameraCalib 4, three trap classes 5, plus 8 autoload reaches; the four autoloads ship by ADR-0262 dec. 6 (precedent five). dec. 9: gl-ADR 0286-0295 RESERVED for this track (#1031 -- nothing allocates numbers across 53 worktrees / 309 branches; both scans agree the high-water was 0285). dec. 10: PASS 9 WILL NOT READ 'Effects is gone' -- the studio's 40,623 lines stay booked Effects in the host, so classify_blueprint will report ~70% of the present size after a SUCCESSFUL extraction; stated now rather than explained then. dec. 11: the pass-6 move is GATED on the studio quiescing or an agreed flag-day against #262. MEASURED: four candidate memberships on one instrument (as-classified 191/58,124/21 lines; panels-out 185/57,128/3; runtime 69/16,505/8; studio 116/40,623/8). The inbound surface was taken over the WHOLE TREE (2,545 files) because classify() cannot see tests/: 1,207 references, of which 1,117 (92.5%) are in tests/ or tools/ -- 4x Battlefield's 284, the largest previously recorded (ADR-0157 S3). GROWTH, like-for-like against ADR-0134 dec. 5's 2026-08-20 split: studio 21,986 -> 40,623 (+84.8%), the rest of the bucket 16,176 -> 17,501 (+8.2%) -- in three weeks the studio nearly doubled and the runtime moved 8%. TWO INSTRUMENT BLIND SPOTS, both the same defect in all_shapes' inside_prefixes being a PREFIX test: src/debug/ swallowed 54 DebugConfig lines (arm 2 reads 9 with the panels in and 63 with them out, with no code change), and no prefix can spell 'src/effects/ but not src/effects/studio/'. Neither fixed here -- a pass 1 that repairs its own instrument mid-reading cannot say whether the reading moved (ADR-0148). REJECTED: taking the bucket as classified (70% authoring tool, 1.7% debug window); taking panels-out-studio-in, which publishes the best arm-7 ratio in the history of the method at 0.005% AND IS THE TRAP -- three absolute lines over a 57,128-line denominator is a smaller FRACTION than three over 16,505 and nothing about the seam changed (ADR-0131's drift used as an argument); keeping the six panels (declines ADR-0257 six weeks after adopting it, on no new evidence); selecting Debug as #7 (cheapest seam in the corpus at 1 name / 2 lines, ADR-0140 permits it anywhere, declined on MEASURED collision -- four src/debug/ files name the catalogue, two live worktrees are editing the directory, #1025 item 4 open; it is #8); deferring Effects until the studio quiesces (treats 495 commits as one number when 370 are the studio's); splitting the studio out of Effects in the blueprint (already decided against by ADR-0134 dec. 4 on ADR-0115 dec. 3's asymmetry test). SOFT SPOTS: S1 the instrument was not repaired and the reading was taken anyway, by hand, which is what arm7_membership.py was built to end (ADR-0241 S1 / ADR-0243 dec. 11) -- pass 2 should fix inside_prefixes and re-take the runtime row. S2 the 44-line seam counts LINES not CALLS (ADR-0141 dec. 2 has no term separating a name that is called from one merely named; ADR-0213 dec. 4 recorded the gap). S3 the 61 DebugConfig lines were counted and bucketed and NOTHING WAS READ. S4 the studio stays booked Effects and nobody has decided whether it should be -- the question is now asked by the measurement, not by preference. S5 the 14-day churn split (19 runtime / 8 studio) is ONE FORTNIGHT of a series that read 209/370 over 60 days. |
| [0287](0287-the-backwards-edge-is-one-misfiled-file-and-the-arm-that-fails-is-the-one-no-selection-ever-ran.md) The backwards edge is one misfiled file, and the arm that FAILS is the one no selection ever ran | `method` | · | · | 1 | `0112`, `0124`, `0126`, `0129`, `0131`, `0134`, `0135`, `0139`, `0157`, `0184`, `0190`, … |
| | | | | | THE BACKWARDS EDGE IS ONE MISFILED FILE, AND THE ARM THAT FAILS IS THE ONE NO SELECTION EVER RAN (ADR-0126 pass 2 for extraction #7, #1184). ADR-0286 selected on arm 7 = 1 name / 3 lines and warned, citing ADR-0262 dec. 2/3, that the other eight arms would find what the selection instrument is blind to. THEY DID, THREE TIMES. (1) ADR-0286 dec. 6 priced the backwards edge at FIVE lines; it is TWENTY-FIVE -- five class_name (all arm 7 can see) plus TWENTY preload(res://src/effects/studio/...) which is arm 6. src/effects/EffectScoreModel.gd's header is an 18-line block of studio preloads and its only consumers outside studio/ and tests/ are EffectViewerScene.gd (THE STUDIO'S OWN ASSEMBLER, ADR-0134 dec. 3) and three tools/ probes; two src/effects/ mentions are ## DOC COMMENTS. It is a studio file in the runtime's directory. (2) ARM 4b DOES NOT REPORT DEBT, IT FAILS -- ADR-0190 dec. 3 enforces that only a non-system addon may DECLARE a global uniform, and assets/shaders/effect_particle_stp.gdshaderinc declares psx_gamma (:56) and psx_fx_stretch (:66). NO SELECTION PASS IN SIX EXTRACTIONS HAS RUN THIS ARM. (3) 396 lines of the proposed membership are reached by nothing: src/effects/PSXDitherCurves.gd (159) is named in the WHOLE TREE only by its own class_name line, and src/debug/EffectTimelineView.gd (237) by three doc comments; closure.py reports both UNREACHED independently. dec. 1: the audited membership is 67 files / 14,441 lines -- a directory rule MINUS A NAMED EXCEPTION LIST, which is what check_move_manifest needs anyway (ADR-0184). dec. 2: EffectScoreModel.gd moves to src/effects/studio/ and is the first build ticket; arm 6 falls 22 -> 2 and arm 7 3 names/8 lines -> 1 name/3 lines, the selection's own figure RECOVERED rather than claimed. dec. 3: arm 4b's two names have TWO DIFFERENT FIXES -- psx_fx_stretch is MECHANICAL (the declaration seam exists at addons/exmateria_platform/display_port/psx_sprite_stretch.gdshaderinc:41 and the shader ALREADY #includes the platform; this is exmateria_battlefield's precedent verbatim, ADR-0220 dec. 2), while psx_gamma is DECLARED BY NO ADDON FILE ANYWHERE and the platform deliberately does not provide it (plugin.gd:37) -- a decision, not a move, and the first time ADR-0190 dec. 4's residual block has reached a system trying to leave. dec. 4: CompositorAutopilot.gd is not a member (classify books it assembler; ADR-0135 dec. 9a's one recorded exception). dec. 5: PSXDitherCurves.gd is dead and is NOT MOVED -- an extraction must not carry dead code into an addon where a later reader finds it inside a published package and assumes it is interface; removal from the membership costs nothing, deletion is delete_dead_code.py's own ticket (ADR-0112 makes UNREACHED a ceiling, not a verdict). dec. 6: A DEBUG PANEL'S PRIVATE MODEL IS NOT A BaseDebugPanel SUBCLASS -- panel_subclasses() catches 3 of ADR-0286 dec. 3's six files; the other three are excluded by the DIRECTORY rule, so dec. 3 rests on dec. 1 and not on ADR-0257's detector. Recorded, not fixed (widening it re-prices three shipped extractions). dec. 7: arm 5's debt is TEN lines and all four symbols are named -- ExMateriaBattlefield.MapIlluminationDDA x4 / .Lattice x2, ExMateriaRender.FoldSurface x3, ExMateriaSound.FedsBank x1 -- against ADR-0262 dec. 5's FIFTY-FIVE accepted for #6. The other 53 are free BY DECLARED TIER (schema/kernel 46, platform/port 3, almanac/rules 4 where AbilityDatabase is declared "table" at exmateria_almanac.gd:451, ADR-0273): that is #1059's mechanism doing the job it was built for, and the same reading before #1059 would have counted the almanac's four against a system that does not exist. dec. 8: arm 2's 72 lines split 61 Debug / 11 AUDIO -- ExMateriaEffectSfx on 11 lines of EffectInstance.gd. AN EXTRACTED PACKAGE'S AUTOLOAD IS ARM-2 DEBT FOR EVERY SUBSEQUENT EXTRACTION, free spelled get_node_or_null(/root/X) and debt spelled X.foo(); arm 2b reads 0. Pass 3 should design it as ADR-0124's crossing (Effects tells Audio a code and a time), not as 11 autoload reaches. dec. 9: VERDICT -- the membership is audited and #7 is viable; three items land BEFORE pass 6 in order (the file move, the psx_fx_stretch include, the psx_gamma decision). MEASURED, all nine arms, 70-file vs audited 67-file: arm1 92->67, arm2 72->72, arm2b 0->0, arm3 7->7 (all free: platform 4, schema 3), arm4 0->0, arm4b 2->2 RED, arm5 63/19 rows unchanged (shape scan reads 18 -- it under-reads by 45), arm6 22->2, arm7 3 names/8 lines -> 1 name/3 lines. Four autoloads declared INSIDE reached on 15 member lines, free on the move by ADR-0262 dec. 6. INBOUND re-taken over the whole 2,545-file tree: 1,132 references, 88 production, 1,044 (92.2%) in tests/ or tools/ -- still ~4x Battlefield's 284. ADR-0286 SOFT SPOT S1 DID NOT BITE: membership_arms.py takes an EXPLICIT file list and all_shapes tests `dst in members` before any prefix, so passing no --inside= makes the membership exact and every number here is one command; the inside_prefixes defect is a USAGE HAZARD on selection_sweep.py, not a hole in this reading, and stays a ticket. REJECTED: leaving EffectScoreModel and inverting the two class_name refs as ADR-0286 dec. 6 proposed (would make the runtime addon publish or duplicate TWENTY editor types); splitting the file (a pass-3 design decision -- moving it whole is reversible, splitting is not); per-member freeing for arm 2 (arm 2 and 2b report the same dependency differently ON PURPOSE); declaring psx_gamma in exmateria_platform (the platform's ADR to write); deleting PSXDitherCurves here (UNREACHED is a ceiling; closure.py cannot see 225 non-literal load() sites); widening panel_subclasses() (ADR-0262's reason verbatim). SOFT SPOTS: S1 the 61 DebugConfig lines are STILL unexamined -- ADR-0286 S3 said so and this pass reproduced the count without reading one; oldest unpaid item in the extraction. S2 NO GODOT PROCESS WAS STARTED -- dec. 2 rests on a name-and-path scan plus two doc comments, and a duck-typed reach or non-literal load() is invisible (ADR-0131 dec. 6); verify the move by LOADING and ASSERTING, per ADR-0157 Spike A. S3 psx_gamma has no owner and this ADR does not give it one -- if still open at pass 6 the extraction STOPS, because arm 4b enforces. S4 arm 5's free set is now read off plugin.cfg, so a wrong tier= would make the headline number wrong SILENTLY and nothing here re-derives them. S5 the 1,044 test-and-tool references were counted, never opened. |
| [0288](0288-the-sixty-one-debug-lines-are-four-booleans-and-the-seam-is-three-addresses-once-the-psx-trio-goes-home.md) The sixty-one `Debug` lines are four booleans, and the seam is three addresses once the PSX trio goes home | `method` | · | · | 6 | `0074`, `0091`, `0112`, `0114`, `0119`, `0124`, `0126`, `0128`, `0131`, `0134`, `0135`, … |
| | | | | | THE SIXTY-ONE DEBUG LINES ARE FOUR BOOLEANS, AND THE SEAM IS THREE ADDRESSES ONCE THE PSX TRIO GOES HOME (ADR-0126 pass 3 for extraction #7, #1184). ADR-0286 S3 and ADR-0287 S1 both deferred the same item -- are the 61 DebugConfig lines one port's two signatures or 61 independent decisions? NEITHER. They are FOUR BOOLEANS -- particle_debug_enabled x26, iteration_debug_enabled x24, timeline_debug_enabled x9, camera_debug_enabled x2 -- READ, NEVER WRITTEN, and every one gates a print. 35 of the 61 read flags with NO PRODUCTION READER OUTSIDE THE MEMBERSHIP (particle, timeline): Effects' own flags wearing a host autoload's name. And DebugConfig.<flag> is a facade over Tune (_dbg_get = Tune.is_registered + bind + get_value), so the runtime reaches a host autoload to read a registry THE PORT ALREADY EXPOSES -- verbatim the sentence addons/exmateria_sprite_rig/install/RigDebug.gd:14-19 wrote for Sprite Rig at #744. dec. 1: add EffectsDebug.gd (statics, TunePort, the same debug.* slugs) -- a TRANSCRIPTION of RigDebug, including _static_init() binding at class load (without it every get_value asserts, because the slugs used to be bound lazily by DebugConfig._dbg_get and so only if the HOST read first) and the shared slug literals (two binds of one slug is supported; two slugs for one flag is the defect). THE SEVERANCE IS TEST-TRANSPARENT: five tests write DebugConfig.particle_debug_enabled and _dbg_set routes to Tune.set_value, which TunePort.get_value reads. dec. 2: particle/timeline become the ADDON'S and the host property becomes a re-export for LoggingDebugPanel. dec. 3: ADR-0286 dec. 8's role binding is THREE TYPE ANNOTATIONS -- caster: Unit, target: Unit at EffectManager.gd:48/:132/:258. Every actual use of a caster or target is global_position / add_child / get_instance_id() / is_instance_valid() / .name-in-a-print; attach_anchors_to_units is ALREADY typed Node3D and UnitTintOverlay NEVER DEREFERENCES the id it is keyed by (Unit.gd:730 passes the material). ADR-0126 cited PaletteSubsystem's WeakRef as a coupling the scan cannot see; re-read, THE DUCK-TYPED REACH IS CLEAN AND THE THREE THINGS THE SCAN CAN SEE ARE THE WHOLE DEFECT. dec. 4: PsxUnits.gd (121) + CameraCalib.gd (35) + PSXCameraConvert.gd (60) MOVE TO addons/exmateria_platform/ -- a closed 216-line triangle whose only outside dependency is ExMateriaPlatform.PsxNum, whose own docstrings say ADR-0091 and never say 'effect', and which is 18 of the 32 game-side inbound lines. THE THIRD KIND OF MISFILED FILE IN src/effects/ (after a studio file and a dead file, a PORT-TIER one). Cost named: arm 5 rises 63 -> ~93, ALL of the increase FREE by declared tier. dec. 5: THE AUDIO CROSSING IS FIFTEEN LINES, NOT ELEVEN -- EffectInstance.gd:17/20/21 preload three scripts BY PATH out of addons/exmateria_sound/runtime/, all three published on the ExMateriaSound facade, and ALL THREE ARE INVISIBLE TO EVERY ONE OF THE NINE ARMS because the target is inside an addon root. Re-spell all fourteen through the facade; the verb surface is four (begin_effect / play_pair / end_effect / orphan_effect), which is ADR-0124's code-and-a-time. The trade is ADR-0175 dec. 2's, repeated deliberately: arm 2 -> 0, arm 5 debt up by the same amount, converting a project.godot dependency into an addon-presence one. dec. 6: SIX OF THE ELEVEN ExMateriaEffectSfx LINES ARE THE STUDIO'S -- audition_container/audition_sound have no caller outside src/effects/studio/ + EffectViewerScene.gd's has_method route. Booked to the studio, NOT MOVED. dec. 7: ADR-0126 check 5 re-tested -- MapTintOverlay IS UnitTintOverlay WITH THE SURFACE KEY ERASED; publish one TintedSurfaces registry (the blueprint's COLOUR OP as payload, blueprint:349 / ADR-0128 -- spelled ExMateriaSchema.ColorRecipe in code; 'colour recipe' was this ADR's own rival coinage, caught in draft) and keep ScreenEffectOverlay's gradient half separate. Four host [autoload] lines become two. dec. 8: arm 5's ten debt lines are FOUR DIFFERENT ANSWERS -- MapIlluminationDDA x4 is PRODUCTION-DEAD (build_illumination's only callers are two tests; ADR-0287 dec. 5's rule, its own ticket), FoldSurface x3 is HONEST AND STAYS (ADR-0074), Lattice x2 is INVERTIBLE AND NOT OURS TO INVERT (a Battlefield sightline query), FedsBank x1 is CORRECT (the blueprint's Format owner rule). dec. 9: the game-side seam is TWELVE CODE LINES over three addresses -- spawn 3, tinted-surface registry 5, screen backdrop 4 -- correcting ADR-0286 dec. 8's '44 lines over ~12 addresses'; TWO of the 14 the scan reported are GDScript DOCSTRINGS (CombatLoop.gd:1858, Unit.gd:1090), ADR-0126 check 1's prose warning arriving inside this pass's own instrument. dec. 10: THE PREDICTED METRIC -- arms 1 67->3 (2 if dec. 8's dead DDA goes), 2 72->0, 3 7->8, 4b 2->0, 5 63/10 debt -> ~93/~10 debt, 6 2->2 NOT PAYABLE (TrapEffect.gd:257-258 name ROM-derived content under the symlinked gitignored project-assets tree -- a content-pack dependency, ADR-0142, not portability debt), 7 3->0; touch_matrix Effects row 87->23 and column 25->6, total cross-system 675->~592 (-12.3%). classify_blueprint: EFFECTS DOES NOT SHRINK -- ADD addons/exmateria_effects/ TO THE WALK ROOTS IN THE SAME COMMIT AS THE MOVE, because ADR-0286 dec. 10's 'roughly 70%' holds only if it is omitted, and a 30% improvement that is a missing walk root is the artifact ADR-0114 dec. 4 forbids. dec. 11: build order extends ADR-0287 dec. 9. INSTRUMENT FINDINGS: touch_matrix cannot see Effects -> Audio AT ALL (classify_blueprint.py:13 -- exmateria_sound is not walked), so an extraction shrinks the progress bar partly by leaving the instrument's field of view (extraction #2 measured this edge at 24 lines; it reads 0 today with the coupling present); Effects -> Debug = 81 is 61 the membership's and 20 the three src/debug/ panels naming BaseDebugPanel/TuneField, booked Effects by classify_blueprint.py:627/638's basename-prefix rules; the inbound COLUMN is blind to the assembler, where 6 of the 12 real inbound lines live (BattlefieldWiring.gd). | [0290](0290-an-addon-was-never-a-system-and-the-arm-4b-blocker-is-four-throwaway-probe-shaders.md) An addon was never a system, and the arm-4b blocker is four throwaway probe shaders | `method` | · | · | 4 | `0074`, `0091`, `0115`, `0121`, `0126`, `0128`, `0134`, `0148`, `0149`, `0150`, `0153`, … |
| | | | | | AN ADDON WAS NEVER A SYSTEM, AND THE ARM-4B BLOCKER IS FOUR THROWAWAY PROBE SHADERS (ADR-0126 pass 4 -- grill and name -- for extraction #7, #1184/#1191). Two questions ADR-0288 left, both with a premise that does not survive measurement. Q1 'what is the addon CALLED, and can it be?' assumed an addon holding PART of a system would be new here. IT IS NOT: classify()'s own walk, split by addons/ vs host, reads Sprite Rig 100% in-addon, Render 100%, Battlefield 99.8%, CHARACTER CATALOGUE 76.9% (582 lines / 3 src/debug/ panels left in the host), and exmateria_almanac holds 795 lines booked UI and 2,745 booked Battle while being named for NEITHER -- ADR-0262 dec. 4 already ruled it ('exmateria_almanac is not a system'). Audio ships as TWO addons in a package outside the walk, so ADR-0121 dec. 1's own exemplar has split. dec. 1: AN ADDON IS A SHIPPING UNIT, A SYSTEM IS A CAPABILITY, and ADR-0134 dec. 4 STANDS UNAMENDED -- dec. 4 rules on the SYSTEM (ADR-0115 dec. 3 asymmetry), ADR-0286 dec. 4 rules on the ADDON; ADR-0121 dec. 1 is an ADDRESS rule, not an identity. What is new is the FRACTION: 24.8% (14,441 of 58,124) against the catalogue's 76.9%. dec. 2: THE ADDON IS addons/exmateria_effects/ -- the blueprint's Effects entry names only the timeline, the particle sim and the channel vocabulary (the membership is exactly those three, so the addon holds 100% of the CAPABILITY and 24.8% of the CONSUMER CENSUS); the _Avoid_ list bars VFX and 'effect system', not Effects; Render's entry licenses a shipped addon naming itself and exmateria_sound is already a name on its own system's Avoid list. A QUALIFIED NAME (exmateria_effect_runtime) IS REJECTED on ADR-0121 dec. 4 -- it bakes a temporary fact into a permanent name. dec. 3/4: #1187 DOES NOT BLOCK EXTRACTION #7. check_addon_portability.py:136 says '22 shader files read' psx_gamma; measured at this commit the name has SEVEN code readers tree-wide -- 3 in UI's shaders, 4 in tools/probe_shaders/ -- and ZERO in the 67-file membership. The membership's only involvement is the DECLARATION at effect_particle_stp.gdshaderinc:56, which exists so four throwaway probes compile; its sibling psx_brightness was deleted from that same include in the ADR-0074 fold endgame and lives as a local const in each probe THREE LINES ABOVE. Delete-and-localise and arm 4b closes INSIDE tools/, with ADR-0287 dec. 3's psx_fx_stretch taking arm 4b 2 -> 0 with NO cross-package dependency. #1187's 'circularity' is also false -- nothing prevents an addons/exmateria_platform/display_port/ .gdshaderinc declaring it (psx_sprite_stretch.gdshaderinc:41 + plugin.gd:116 do exactly that for psx_fx_stretch); plugin.gd:37 DESCRIBES the tree, it does not refuse on principle, and ADR-0220 dec. 4 leaves the question OPEN, which is not blocked. #1187 is re-scoped to UI, not closed. dec. 5: ADR-0288 dec. 4's PSX-trio move CARRIES A RENAME or it launders jargon into a tier nothing scores -- score_goals.py's own lexicon over the 67 files reads 111 JARGON CODE LINES (psx x109, fft x2) across 25 files, 38 of them in or naming the trio; PLATFORM_EXEMPT is {'Render'} and goal #7 is scored PER SYSTEM over GOALS.tsv, so 'platform' is a tier no goal row reads. CameraCalib -> CameraCalibration (beside #583's DisplayCalibration), PSXCameraConvert -> that facade, PsxUnits LEFT OPEN (Units=measurement collides with Unit=combatant). pitch_psx/yaw_psx/base_yaw_psx -> _12bit, ADR-0234's EXECUTED spelling, not a new word. dec. 6: 'BOOKED BUT NOT MOVED' BECOMES CHECKABLE BY ONE RENAME -- classify books FILES and ROOT_SET books ROOTS, neither can book a METHOD, so ADR-0288 dec. 6's booking has no carrier; rename EffectInstance.audition_container/_sound to studio_audition_* matching the host wrappers at EffectViewerScene.gd:768/777 THAT ALREADY CARRY THE PREFIX, and one grep returns the whole four-hop chain. dec. 7: ADR-0288 dec. 7 SPLITS -- the rename (UnitTintOverlay -> TintedSurfaces, register_unit -> register_surface) is free and lands regardless; the SURFACE_MAP merge that deletes MapTintOverlay is the new code and takes its own ticket. Measured blast radius: 34 + 40 namers, and the only two references from inside SHIPPED addons (exmateria_battlefield DynamicGeometryBuilder.gd:33/561, exmateria_schema ColorStack.gd:325) ARE COMMENTS -- the live reach was inverted at #589. Payload keeps the blueprint's word, COLOUR OP (ADR-0128). dec. 8: the translation table lands in docs/context/15-effect-orchestration.md, re-measured at pass 9 (extraction #4's precedent). dec. 9: the blueprint's three ADR-0126 check-2 verdicts are RECORDED in BLUEPRINT.md, not rewritten -- ADR-0288 dec. 3 ruled it and did not do it. dec. 10: GOAL #2'S ARTIFACT HAS LAPSED TWICE -- extraction #5 (almanac, ADR-0251) and #6 (catalogue, ADR-0267) both landed with NO translation table and NO GOALS.tsv rows (its only subjects are Render, Audio, Battlefield, Sprite Rig); ticketed, not silently back-filled. GENERAL LESSON: a portability arm answers 'is this name declared here', NEVER 'does anything here read it' -- so a declaration with no local reader is invisible to the arm that reds it, and the fix looks like another package's work when it is a delete. S1: three passes with no Godot process; pass 4 changed no code, and the tree fast-forwarded 11 commits, which is the known post-merge .godot cache hang -- budget a cache warm. S2: the psx_gamma census is a static scan over four shader suffixes and cannot see a string-built shader. S3: PsxUnits is left open, which is the binary-in-a-soft-spot shape ADR-0286 dec. 7 warned about. S4: 24.8% is against a bucket whose studio half is still moving (~495 commits in 60 days). S5: dec. 6's rename is held by two has_method STRING literals -- nothing reds if one is missed. |
| [0295](0295-the-forty-five-class-names-collapse-to-twenty-one-and-forty-eight-vault-notes-are-held-by-two-anchors.md) The forty-five class_names collapse to twenty-one, and forty-eight vault notes are held by two anchors | `method` | · | · | 4 | `0052`, `0057`, `0091`, `0111`, `0114`, `0126`, `0128`, `0148`, `0149`, `0153`, `0157`, … |
| | | | | | THE FORTY-FIVE CLASS_NAMES COLLAPSE TO TWENTY-ONE, AND FORTY-EIGHT VAULT NOTES ARE HELD BY TWO ANCHORS (loop pass 5 -- plan -- for extraction #7, #1184/#1191). Passes 1-4 settled WHAT moves and WHERE; neither they nor any instrument they ran priced what the DESTINATION already obliges, and ADR-0212 is cited by none of 0286/0287/0288/0290. dec. 1: THE ADDON PUBLISHES 21 NAMES. Every one of the nine addons declares exactly ONE class_name, the folder-named facade (ADR-0212 dec. 1); the membership declares 45. Derived rather than assumed: 24 members are reached from PRODUCTION outside the addon, 3 of those leave to ExMateriaPlatform, so 21 are required on ExMateriaEffects; 15 more are reached ONLY from tests/tools and are NOT published (a global name lands in a consumer who will never run our tests); 28 are reached from nowhere outside (12 shaders, 4 autoloads, 12 internal .gd). Consequence stated up front: check_lattice_scene criterion 4 is NON-ZERO for this addon by decision, not by slip. dec. 2: FOUR INSTRUMENTS NEED TEN REGISTER SEEDS, ALL IN THE MOVE COMMIT -- and WALK_ROOTS feeds check_addon_portability's _walk_roots.addon_roots(), so ONE LINE ARMS TWO INSTRUMENTS and ADR-0288 dec. 10's predicted table becomes a pass-6 acceptance criterion rather than a pass-9 prediction. The FACADES row cannot precede the addon folder (extraction #4's finding, unchanged). dec. 3: PsxUnits -> PsxMagnitude, not coined -- ADR-0091's own filename is psx-MAGNITUDES-convert-to-game-units and PsxUnits.gd:3 calls itself the ONE home for PSX continuous-magnitude conversions; Magnitude appears as an identifier in 3 lines tree-wide and as a class_name in none. dec. 4: PSXCameraConvert -> PsxChirality, CORRECTING ADR-0290 dec. 5's row. Dec. 5 folded it into CameraCalibration on the ground that it 'publishes no arithmetic of its own'; measured, its 70 call sites are 21 pure delegation to PsxUnits + 21 pure delegation to CameraCalib + 38 ITS OWN -- the Y-negate and the -pitch. Both siblings refuse the chirality axis IN THEIR OWN DOCSTRINGS (PsxUnits.gd:19, CameraCalib.gd:5, PSXCameraConvert.gd:10). The 42 pass-throughs re-point directly; the trio stays three names and completes ADR-0091 section 2's split. Fallback if the grill disagrees: PsxCameraConvert, the spelling fix alone. dec. 5: EIGHT FACADE CITE LINES IN THREE OTHER ADDONS name a src/effects/ path (exmateria_schema.gd:71/72/80/91/92/99, exmateria_battlefield.gd:133, exmateria_render.gd:60) and all eight go stale on the git mv, in addons this extraction does not otherwise open. dec. 6: THE STUDIO GATE IS MEASURABLY OPEN and the hot half is the OTHER one -- src/effects/studio/ ran 43/135/162/15/0/0 commits in W32-W37 and last moved 2026-08-30, while the 67-file membership ran 8/21/15/26/7/0 and last moved 2026-09-06. dec. 7: the Godot debt is THREE items, each naming its assertion (ADR-0157 Spike A), and the cache warm is MEASURED at 22 s with zero parse errors -- not a hang. dec. 8: extraction #7 writes docs/EXTRACTION-7-MOVE-MANIFEST.tsv, 67 rows, AT THIS PASS -- check_move_manifest.py:4 says the manifest is written at pass 5 (ADR-0168) and extractions #4/#5/#6 wrote none. dec. 9: THE 48 UNANCHORED VAULT NOTES BLOCK PASS 6 and are the only item here that cannot be deferred -- check_vault_anchors reports Effects at 2 anchors naming 1 note against Sprite Rig 40, Battlefield 34, Audio 168, while 48 vault notes cite a src/effects/ path; the anchors must be written while the files are at their OLD address because the R: path is the only note->file map and the move destroys it. GENERAL LESSON: a guard that REPORTS coverage and never asserts it makes two anchors and forty look identical in a green pre-flight. THE 1,044 IS OPENED: it is a SHAPE count, and the move's real bill is 234 DISTINCT CODE FILES (183 tests, 35 production, 16 tools) because the two channels are paid in different units -- 414 res:// path binds per LINE, 671 bare class_name reaches per FILE (ADR-0211 dec. 4), 48 autoload reaches FREE. Stripping STRING LITERALS before the type scan is what separates them; without it every preload of a member is double-counted as a bare reach. S1: 234 is a static floor (225 non-literal load() sites tree-wide). S2: a name reached only DYNAMICALLY reads as internal here and would fail at runtime, not at the guard -- a fourth Godot item is owed and not specified. S3: dec. 4 re-opens a row ADR-0290 dec. 11 listed as settled, on a measurement, with a fallback. S4: quiescence is a commit-rate reading and a flag day is about conflicts. S5: NO NUMBER HERE WAS TAKEN WITH THE PRE-FLIGHT GREEN -- main is red on check_adr_shape (ADR-0275 carries THREE Amendment sections, #1207, worse than the two that ticket records), so every instrument was run directly. S6: '48 notes' is a path-literal grep -l and nobody has read them. |

Supersedes ADR-0161 SS2 and refutes all three constants it shipped - RAMP_TICKS 60->16, STEP_TICKS 2->1 (the tick increments by one; the 2-step is {3E}'s FUN_801467dc, a different overlay), START_VALUE 255-linear->0xC0 with an offset and saturation at frame 12 of 16. SS4's refusal to share a kernel is VINDICATED: measured, the two curves differ. Bit 1 of kind is direction and the &2 guard makes a same-direction fire a NO-OP, so the map's entry cue does not unconditionally fade in. Root cause was instrument SCOPE: world_disassembly.txt holds only 0x800E0000..0x801CD957 (WORLD.BIN), so every WLDCORE address reads as absent; WLDCORE.BIN + mipsdis.py at base 0x80067000 was the whole measurement.
