| [0230](0230-the-reveal-drain-is-an-ordered-pass-and-the-animation-writes-the-bit.md) The reveal drain is an ordered pass, and the animation writes the bit | `engine` | · | · | 5 | `0117`, `0161`, `0172`, `0174`, `0176`, `0179`, `0188` |
| | | | | | THE REVEAL DRAIN IS AN ORDERED PASS, AND THE ANIMATION WRITES THE BIT. Campaign.MASK_REVEAL had zero callers and set_node_known/set_route_drawn had none outside tests, so no reveal emit had ever become a known node and the spine stalled at story counter 4 (scenario 29 writes var[110]=4; the single live enter is node 26 Sweegy Woods; node 26 is unknown so the cursor skips it and travel drops every route with an unknown endpoint). The proposed fix -- loop query(marker, MASK_REVEAL) until it returns {} -- DOES NOT TERMINATE: mask 0xF80 selects 107 of the 182 scripts (reveal_route 43, pair100 4, here200 17, reveal_node 41, unary800 2), and nine nodes open their beat with a here200 guarded on a bit only a later reveal_node sets, while query() is first-match. The table is a CHOREOGRAPHY, not a set: every beat is triplets of here200(from); reveal_route(from,to); reveal_node(to). FUN_8006C894 @ 0x8006C894 was read (90 instructions, 4 callees): it does ONE emit per invocation, dispatching the result word 0x800D4644 -- &0x180 -> FUN_8006DA88(OUT0,OUT1,(r&0x100)!=0), &0xC00 -> FUN_8006DE50(OUT0,(r&0x800)!=0), &0x200 -> FUN_8006D7F4(OUT0==0xFF?marker:OUT0). Three measured facts follow. pair100/unary800 are NOT separate kinds, they are the ERASE DIRECTION of reveal_route/reveal_node (same call, flag set; and each of the six is guarded on the bit it must clear). FUN_80091238 really is first-match from script 0 every call (s1=0 at 0x80091294, no resume cursor in the ROM). And THE EMIT DOES NOT WRITE THE VARIABLE -- op 0x22 handler 0x80091D48 only ORs 0x401 into the result word; the write is FUN_800EF25C(node+512,1) at 0x8006DFA0 inside mode 0x36 tick, the node-reveal ANIMATION page, paired with sound 0x77 (erase arm branches past it, sound 2). The runner is itself page mode 0x39 (FUN_8006C7AC calls it at 0x8006C82C), so nothing gates it -- being that page is the gate. dec. 1: CampaignRevealPass holds a node index and a script CURSOR; step() scans forward for the first 0xF80 script whose conditions pass against the LIVE store, applies it, returns a descriptor; {} ends the pass. Conditions re-evaluate live as the ROM does, but a script is visited at most once, so termination is BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by a property of the data -- and that property is FALSE, 17 of 107 scripts never clear their own guard. Deliberately NOT called a transcription. dec. 2: Campaign owns the table and the store, the map owns the presentation; Campaign.reveal_pass(node_index) is the whole crossing (port-list C3). The doc conflict was a false dilemma -- the map decides WHEN and paces the steps, Campaign decides WHAT and writes the bits; the map never reads events.json and Campaign never animates. dec. 3: a step applies the bit, because on the console the bit and the animation are the same event. dec. 4: the pass is STEPPED not run -- WorldMapScene loops step() today, and pacing those calls is the only change the animation needs. dec. 5: reveal/unreveal are one family with a direction; pair100 and unary800 are retired as names; the erase arms ship now because they first fire at counter 18 where Zirekile removes what Dorter drew at counter 16, silently. dec. 6: here200 is LOOK_AT (handler 0x80091DC8 writes selected-place word 0x800D0BB4 and hands the node 0x34 descriptor to FUN_8008ED00; 0xFF means the marker own node) and is returned as a step that does nothing yet; NOT named focus -- check_context_index refused it, the vocabulary already spends that word on the input-focus stack. dec. 7: reveal_route(a,b) resolves by UNORDERED ENDPOINT PAIR and that is total -- all 48 routes have a unique pair and all 47 route emits guard on exactly 556+the resolved index, zero mismatches, asserted rather than trusted. dec. 8: the trigger is map-open and arrival, nothing else; arrival is NOT optional because counter 31 reveals live on Dorter with no hand-off at counter 30, and two beats (Zeltennia var[170], Warjilis var[165]) are not counter-gated at all. dec. 9: the sound cues 0x77 and 2 are recorded and DECLINED on ADR-0174 grounds. dec. 10: _conditions_pass unchanged -- ZERO of the 107 reveal-mask scripts is roster-gated, all 214 conditions are opcode 0x01. Prediction 3 FALSIFIED usefully: 17 of 19 beats are hosted on the node whose hand-off fired at counter k-1 so the marker is there by construction, but counter 1 and counter 31 are not, which is what forced the arrival trigger; had it held, open-only would have shipped and Chapter 3 would have stalled. Residual unread and stated as such: here200 writes no variable anywhere read, so on a strict reading the console would re-fire it forever too. Seven counters (14,29,30,34,40,46,49) still bind no enter -- five bind a multi (0x004), the ERRANDS list of FUN_8008D3C0, not a hand-off -- filed as #849 rather than folded in. |
| [0231](0231-the-reveal-animation-is-three-pages-on-the-vsync-clock.md) The reveal animation is three pages on the vsync clock | `engine` | · | · | 2 | `0161`, `0177`, `0230` |
| | | | | | THE REVEAL ANIMATION IS THREE PAGES ON THE VSYNC CLOCK. ADR-0230 dec. 4 left the pacing owed: WorldMapScene drained a reveal pass with a while loop and the whole beat landed in one frame. The three WLDCORE animation pages under FUN_8006C894's dispatch were read (tick table 0x8009E690 names each): look_at pushes FUN_8006D7F4 -> page 0x32 -> tick FUN_8006D928, a CAMERA pan; reveal_route pushes FUN_8006DA88 -> page 0x35 -> tick FUN_8006DBB8, the ribbon; reveal_node pushes FUN_8006DE50 -> page 0x36 -> tick FUN_8006DF4C, sixteen vsyncs (ori v1,zero,0x10 at 0x8006DE58) whose whole body is the counter and the rebuild bit. FUN_8008D9A0 arms the ribbon: resolves the route by unordered endpoint pair, copies the polyline to 0x800D3BE8, and computes per-quad holds isqrt(dx2+dy2)>>5 into 0x800D3BC4 (sra v0,v0,5 at 0x8008DBC0) -- on the shipped model every hold is ZERO because the longest segment is 31.1 px and the shift needs 32, so a road draws one quad per vsync in 3 to 10 frames. THE BIT IS NOT ALWAYS WRITTEN ON FRAME 1: reveal_node 0x8006DFA0 frame 1, unreveal_route 0x8006DC10 frame 1, reveal_route 0x8006DD04 LAST frame, unreveal_node 0x8006E048 LAST frame -- one rule, the bit changes on the frame the thing appears or disappears, which corrects 11-campaign-spine.md's Reveal step entry. dec. 1: WorldMapRevealAnimation is a stepper on WorldMapScene.advance beside _screen_in and _travel, never a Tween (ADR-0161; the panel here measures 143.9 Hz). dec. 2: the holds are console constants, and the >>5 formula is ported rather than the 1 it evaluates to. dec. 3: the animation DRAWS and never writes a bit -- the write stays on ADR-0230 dec. 3's seam, and for the two directions where the console writes at the far end of the page the FRAME is overridden instead (a revealing route is hidden from path_quads and drawn as a partial ribbon; an erasing node is drawn as a ghost the store no longer knows). dec. 4: a look-at's picture is the LOCATION NAME over the node it names -- the emit handler writes DAT_800D0BB4 and 19.1 draws that node's name cel at that node's point -- and NOT the player's cursor, which FUN_8006C844 STILLS (it zeroes the four ramp words 0x8009EF6C/0x8009EF7C/0x8009F184/0x8009F194) before every push. dec. 5: the pen outlives its one-vsync page, because on the console it is a word. dec. 6: the arrival's ending waits (_arrival_pending) -- the town-page return and node_entered both hand the screen away and would land over a running reveal. dec. 7: input is refused for the length of the pass as a GAME-STATE gate, the shape _travel already has, not a sixth answer to who has input (ADR-0177); the console agrees structurally because the animation page sits above mode 0 at 0x800BB4F0. THE 0x800D3C8C LATCH IS NOT THE REVEAL RUNNER'S: every access in the overlay is one write at 0x80067EF0 (init, zero), a pointer form at 0x8006CD0C and three reads; the write (v^2)|1 is at 0x8006CDA0 inside FUN_8006C9FC's own cursor auto-move block, so WORLD_MAP_SCREEN.md line 306 and 29.2 misattribute it -- the sentence moved with the gate when 32.1 corrected the entry point and the writer did not. dec. 8: the map opens and THEN reveals, after the screen-in lands. dec. 9: settle() is the pre-0231 behaviour kept as a verb, and _capture uses it. dec. 10: the erase ships with a picture -- its PAGE was read even though its draw side was not. dec. 11: the cues stay declined (ADR-0230 dec. 9) and a third is recorded -- the ribbon page plays 0x76 in BOTH directions at 0x8006DC50/0x8006DC54. Also read: 0x800D3BBC is the ribbon animation's state block, not WorldMapTravel's 'selected route ... not read'. Prediction 3 FALSIFIED usefully -- three of the four inputs are guarded twice over, but X is guarded by nothing else, so the arm that scores the _unhandled_input gate had to be the X one. here200's console termination is still OPEN and the read narrows it: with no pan armed page 0x32 pops on its own first tick. |
| [0264](0264-gambit-battle-is-a-seek-into-the-navigator-not-a-second-combat-host.md) Gambit battle is a seek into the navigator, not a second combat host | `engine` | · | · | 7 | `0037`, `0051`, `0056`, `0065`, `0082`, `0083`, `0137`, `0219`, `0242`, `0258` |
| | | | | | GAMBIT BATTLE IS A SEEK INTO THE NAVIGATOR, NOT A SECOND COMBAT HOST. Three defects in GambitBattle -- camera framed on the map corner, every unit frozen on IDLE frame 0 through deployment, authored cast facing the wrong way -- were reported as one cause (gambit skips the event script). Only the camera is that. dec. 1: FRAMING IS AN EVENT-SCRIPT DEFECT and there is no scriptless battle -- every battle entry pose is the terminal {19} of its group opener (Gariland scn 10 PC 35 = Angle 302 / Map Rotation 5632 / Zoom 4096, where 302 and 4096 are camera_init FUN_8008e468 constants and the rotation is the map-dependent yaw); all 72 battle groups have an opener; random encounters use the shared Random Battle Template (scn 400/401/402), whose 401 carries five {19}s including the same terminal settle. GambitBattle sets NO pose: its only camera write is cursor_rig.seed_from_map(map, Vector2i(0,0)), and CursorController.seed_from_map hard-cuts the camera to that cell. dec. 2: THE FREEZE IS A CLOCK DEFECT AND {80} MARCH DOES NOT FIX IT -- ScenarioCast stamps clock_owner = COMBAT (Unit._process no-ops), TurnDirector.open_deployment sets combat_active = false, no GPU simulator exists until commit, so NOTHING TICKS during deployment; {80} supplies a pose, not a tick. NavigatorMain rides ScenarioVM.idle_only_units on the one 60 Hz tick (ADR-0065). dec. 3: THE FACING IS A DATA DEFECT NO OPCODE CAN REACH -- ScenarioCast hardcodes SOUTH for enemies / NORTH for the player and never reads the ENTD facing_raw we parse and already lift correctly (ScenarioPlayerScene.initial_spawn_facing_12bit, ROM angle = Facing << 10 at 0x80087c1c); for the squad it invents _face_toward_enemies over a unit_facing nibble the deployment-zone table authored and DeploymentZoneDatabase already serves. THE GARILAND OPENER CONTAINS ZERO FACING INSTRUCTIONS -- no {2D}, {53}, {2C}, {69} or {8C} -- so running it would have corrected the facing of nobody while appearing to. Two of the three defects would have survived the fix proposed for them. dec. 4: PLAY-ONE-BATTLE BECOMES A SEEK INTO NavigatorMain via the existing NavigatorRunner.start_at(index), defaulting to the pre_battle beat (opener fast-forwards at 30x, ~1 s) with a flag to seek the opener and watch it; GambitBattle stops being a CombatHost. All three defects are already solved in the spine ONCE -- _settle_world_via_opener, _register_deployed_idle_pump + _hand_off_clocks_to_combat (ADR-0083), _deploy_owned_units zone-facing lift -- so delegation DELETES ScenarioCasts hardcoded facing and _face_toward_enemies rather than fixing them, and inherits the deploy-before-opener invariant (28 of 72 openers Erase/Draw the squad then {1F} Focus on it; NavigatorMain.play_beat carries the scar). dec. 5: addressing stays scenario-id-shaped -- a groups root IS its setup records id, so --scenario=9 keeps meaning what it means. dec. 6: THE DEPLOYMENT PICKER IS PROMOTED INTO THE SPINE and replaces its DeploymentPlan.assign call, because DeploymentAssignment.auto_fill already delegates to that same function -- the spine does not lose auto-deploy, it gains a picker with auto-deploy inside it. dec. 7: {1B} Map Light gets a documented _skip, not an implementation -- verified:false, un-RE-d, twice in all 72 openers (both group 291 / scn 292); the last unverified lighting opcode implemented on inference, {1A}, was a phantom that changed zero pixels and had to be removed (ADR-0051), and the overexposed-map bug it is confused with was ADR-0056, a map-data export defect. REJECTED: patching GambitBattle in place (a second implementation of battle entry, and the three invariants are exactly what drifts apart in two copies); extracting a shared battle-entry component (it converges on being most of NavigatorMain); engine defaults instead of the opener -- the apparent control, 170 byte-identical 3-instruction event stubs, turned out to be SETUP RECORDS, 155 of which are immediately followed by their groups non-stub opener, so the stub is half of a battle, not a battle. CONSEQUENCES: the fast-forward becomes load-bearing for 72 openers of 24-679 instructions (mean 144) against ScenarioPathApplier._MAX_FF_FRAMES = 2100 at _FF_SPEED = 30, never exercised at this range, and a truncated fast-forward fails SILENTLY into the bad framing this ADR is about -- the 72-opener sweep is in scope, not a follow-up; the host-vs-host differential test EXPIRES ON SUCCESS and is written to be deleted with GambitBattle; parse_placement.py-s open zone_facing fold question gets its second live oracle free; GambitBattle is NOT deleted in the change that replaces it, because its seven tests are the pickers only behavioural coverage. |

| | | | | | Extraction #1 follow-up. Resolves #575, which ADR-0169 dec. 7 filed OFF map #560. The addon is TWO modules with opposite shapes and #575's disagreement belongs to one of them: PSXDisplay.gd (260 lines, 19 production files across 8 buckets) is a PORT and goes to the platform BUCKET; the fold bracket (447 lines, ONE production consumer, setup(cam) + quantize_levels) has a system's shape and stays Render's, one of the eleven. 🔴 THE ARGUMENT IS NOT REACH: #575 and ADR-0169 dec. 7 both argue 'eight buckets reach a peer', and ADR-0139 dec. 2 already refuted reach as an admission test (rank by it and Debug 160 goes first, Tune second -- the reductio). This decides on SHAPE, per ADR-0139 dec. 12: an autoload with I/O and mutable state is a port, which is Tune's own shape. Corroboration was already in classify_blueprint.py, whose comment books psx_par.gdshaderinc platform BECAUSE 'its CPU side is one global uniform pushed through a port (PSXDisplay._apply_par)' -- so the GPU half was booked on the port's existence and the port was not booked with it. TWO VOCABULARY TRAPS pinned: 'platform' names an ADR TIER (over decisions, and it is the tier that is 'not a place for code', ADR-0129 dec. 9) and a CODE BUCKET (Tune.gd, PsxNum.gd, two .gdshaderinc); and the bucket does not mean generic (BLUEPRINT.md: PsxUnits is platform-tier and PSX-specific). NO FILE MOVES HERE: the relocation is pass 6's FOURTH item beside ADR-0169 dec. 2's three, with the RULES line as one exact rule ahead of the addons/exmateria_render/ prefix (ADR-0146 dec. 2's plugin.gd mechanism). Deciding here and building there avoids two branches creating one address. What must land before pass 6 is the DECISION, because #563 is open and scores PSXDisplay [Render] as outbound debt while Tune [platform], reached 30x harder, is not -- a difference that is entirely the tier. 🔴 THE PORT'S SURFACE IS MOSTLY UNREAD: 3 of 5 signals have ZERO subscribers anywhere (src, tests, tools), live_par_changed has zero in production, shader_global_default() is public for one test; and 4 of 6 knobs ship at IDENTITY with zero committed render.* overrides for any of its slugs, so every boot _apply_* re-pushes the value project.godot already declares. The load-bearing member is live_ui_par (7 readers, no other source). RENAMED to DisplayCalibration -- PSXDisplay names a system the file is leaving AND promises a display abstraction it lacks (it owns par, three sprite stretches, gamma = calibration). This REVIVES ADR-0129 dec. 10, whose trigger ('the rename happens when Render extracts') fired at extraction #1 and DID NOT EXECUTE: the kernel still ships psx_ot_depth.gdshaderinc, prefixed, inside the addon where dec. 10 says the prefix says nothing. Follow-up ticket, not pass 6's commit: 27 files / 90 lines + project.godot:44, none of it work pass 6 was doing. ARM 4 HOLE: ADR-0169 dec. 5 scopes arm 4 to 'any global uniform reachable from an addon root', but PSXDisplay DECLARES none -- it PUSHES four by name, and psx_gamma (22 shader readers) is declared by no addon file at all, so a declaration-only arm is silent on the push side. GOALS re-evidenced: goal #7's exemption is 26 of Render's 29 platform-jargon lines and they are ALL PSXDisplay.gd's (score_goals.py), and ADR-0150 restricts the exemption to Render -- so the file's departure drops Render to 3 and the rename is what stops the 26 being jargon in a bucket with no exemption. Goal #5 unaffected in substance (0 outbound either way) over an addon that falls 707 -> 447 lines. Amends ADR-0150: its subject argument stands, but subject alone no longer settles membership. |

A forwarder cannot close the door by construction: six assembler scene roots hold the map as `@onready var map: Node3D = $ProceduralMap`, a NodePath fetch infers Node, and criterion 1 (annotations) passes a forwarder while criterion 2 catches it. ADR-0166 dec. 4 criterion 3 forbids it outright (2 of its 8 rows). The port needs a FOURTH member, all_cells() -> Array[TerrainCell]: dec. 2 published three, dec. 1 measured door 1's used surface as two methods, and get_all_tiles has four external consumers reading only TerrainCell fields. 🔴 The register's src/ population is 15 LINES OVER 6 FILES, not 12: 12 .get_tile + 1 .get_all_tiles (ScenarioUnitAlignmentDebugPanel.gd:137, which classify() books CUTSCENE, making its share TEN not nine) + 2 has_method guards. One of the 12 DISSOLVES rather than re-points -- MovementComponent.gd:28 derives both args from the unit's own global_position, so ADR-0166 dec. 2's Vector2i deletes the line and kills initialize_logical_position's map parameter across 3 callers. 🔴 Re-pointing leaves the handle as untyped as it is today: through map_composer, src/ reaches get_tile x9 but ALSO is_animation_active x3, play_texture_animation, commit_field_tint, set_field_color_stack, change_map, rebuild_map and 5 more has_method guards -- the port covers 10 of ~24. The handle SPLITS (typed `lattice` + retained untyped composer) and the ~14 residual field-effects reaches are NAMED AS DEBT, so pass 9 reads Cutscene -> Battlefield 4 -> ~13 as PARTIAL; 24 would mean over-scope, 4 would mean nothing shipped. Unit.get_current_tile() is NOT a door (dec. 3's row 3 is a mis-classification): it reads a STORED FIELD and queries nothing. Its circular dependency is real and is EXACTLY the occupancy members -- Tile.gd names Unit on four lines (70, 201, 213, 234) and nothing else -- so ADR-0166 dec. 1 dissolves it, and dec. 2 dissolves it again by returning Vector2i. MovementComponent.gd:9 already types the field because Tile.gd doesn't name MovementComponent; only Unit.gd closes the loop, so the comment was CORRECT. 🔴 The register gets TWO ARMS because classify() returns None for EVERY tests/ file -- ADR-0167 dec. 6's assembler hole and #551's unbucketed-asset hole a third time, now as the MAJORITY of a door's population: 13 consumer sites plus 7 files that DEFINE func get_tile, i.e. PRODUCERS outside the addon that ADR-0166 dec. 4's producer-side choice cannot reach. Arm 1 ENFORCES over src/ (15 -> 0); arm 2 REPORTS over tests/ (20, no target) because a threshold there would be guesswork and a silent cap reads as coverage. GDScript has no interfaces, so arm 1 must admit both `extends Lattice` and a seeded real Lattice. 🔴 THE DUCK-TYPING IS A TEST SEAM as much as a type refusal: 4 of the 7 mocks return a bare Node3D, an ad-hoc MockTile, or FakeTile extends RefCounted, and ScenarioVM.gd:3788 probes `"impassable" in tile` under the comment 'Mock maps without an impassable field count as walkable'. SOURCE-COPY, AND THE RIG PRINTS WHICH CLAIM IT CHECKS (dec. 5): stranger_sound stages the PUBLISHED tree because its published shape differs materially (fft_smd.gdextension is withheld so the GDScript driver runs downstream); no such asymmetry exists for the four in-walk addons, whose manifest would exclude .godot/ and nothing else and would assert a public REPO none of them has. An earlier draft cited `compositor_fold` in FoldSurface.gd; that string is in a COMMENT recording a migration OFF it -- this repo's assertion-matches-its-own-comment defect, committed while writing the file that names it. ADR-0164 dec. 4's three criteria all measure host->addon; 'drop the addon into an empty project and it works' is the OPPOSITE axis, and ADR-0196's Consequences raised it as 'raised and unowned' -- two passes printed that sentence and moved on. This ADR owns it. dec. 2: the install target is a bare Godot 4.8-COMPOSITOR-FORK project containing exmateria_schema, exmateria_platform and exmateria_battlefield and nothing else -- NOT bare-alone, on 9 distinct sibling class_names over 23 rows, 16 shader #include lines covering every shader the addon has, and one preload by path (MapGridOverlay.gd:19); severing it is not a portability fix, it is undoing ADR-0139 dec. 9/12, which PERMIT naming the kernel and the port. dec. 3: the fork is part of the TARGET, not debt -- 6 files carry compositor_fold/compositor_layer/render_layer, fork-only, and under stock 4.7 the compositor self-disables with NO ERROR while Fold.add throws (ADR-0186 Amdt 4 3); REPORTED with no target, because a register that scored its own target could never reach 0. dec. 5 REFUSES the blanket 'declared asset contract' reading this pass inherited, on a measurement that contradicts it: only assets/maps/ is gitignored and the RANGETILE trio sits behind a symlink into the asset hub -- the other FIVE of nine distinct targets are TRACKED and ship with a clone. So the 11 sites split three ways. Class A (3 sites, TRACKED and addon-owned: tile_cursor_opaque.tres, tile_overlay.tres, tile_knife.json) MOVE into the addon, no contract -- and the test is TRACKED-NESS not authorship, a wording this ADR first got wrong about one of its own three: tile_knife.json is ROM-DERIVED (parse_cursor_bob.py extracts it from BATTLE.BIN at FUN_8007e304) and is still Class A because the repo has already COMMITTED its 426 bytes, while Class B's members are un-shippable for being gitignored rather than for having a ROM upstream; TileCursor.gd:150 is a preload and therefore the one PARSE-TIME site. Class B (7 sites, ROM-derived, un-shippable: assets/maps/, assets/doodads/, the RANGETILE trio) can never be bundled -- the HARDCODING is the defect and the dependency is a contract, closed by a settable search root, and the register scores the LITERAL not the dependency. Class C (1 site) is dec. 6. dec. 6: PlayerCamera.tscn:5 declares and :38 instances the HOST's CombatUI under FocusPoint/Camera -- the addon's camera scene owns the host's entire combat UI; re-pathing it turns arm 1 green and makes the tree WORSE, so the fix is a mount point the host fills, and this is the case proving a register can be satisfied in the wrong direction. dec. 7: a SOFT null-guarded reach to a host autoload is conformant (TunePort.gd:78 root.get_node_or_null('Tune')) and the consequence is stated not discovered -- in a bare project every tunable override is INERT and compiled-in defaults apply, which is a working install. dec. 8: shader globals are a bare-project COMPILE failure (strictly worse than a parse error) and an ENABLE-TIME ProjectSettings WRITE from plugin.gd is PERMITTED -- a runtime READ means the addon depends on host config and stays forbidden (count 0, must stay 0), a write means the addon PROVIDES its own config, the opposite coupling. dec. 9 gives the input actions the same answer, and the test is 'not a ui_* builtin' rather than 'the host declares it' (a guard keyed on the host goes blind the day the host stops). REGISTER BUILT 2026-08-28 (tools/check_addon_install.py, 17 seeded tests, wired into pre-flight); FIRST RUN 49 sites over 29 rows -- 11 asset / 12 action rows over 8 distinct actions / 6 globals -- zero unlisted, zero stale. CLASS A CLOSED the same day (three commits) and the register graded the move IN BOTH DIRECTIONS AT ONCE: run with the burn-down untouched it returned rc 1 with the three Class A rows STALE and TWO ROWS UNLISTED, which is the strongest evidence available that the scanner is satisfied rather than blind -- a blind one reports the same 0 before and after. 🔴 THE MOVE SUBTRACTED THREE AND ADDED TWO: tile_cursor_opaque.tres carries its own two RANGETILE ext_resources, always there one indirection away and invisible to EVERY instrument while the file sat outside the addon root, so arm 1 went 11->10 not 11->8 and the two are booked Class B beside the Tile.gd rows naming the same textures; a fix booked into the bucket it drains is how a delta lies, so classify the new file before believing one. The count is also the wrong measure of the move: TileCursor.gd:150 was the register's ONE PARSE-TIME site (a preload of a file a bare project does not have, which stops the addon LOADING) and it now preloads a file the addon ships, so what remains fails at runtime -- the register counts sites and cannot show a severity change, which is why dec. 5 splits by class rather than counting. Verified in the ENGINE not inferred: TileCursor.tscn boots clean on the 4.8 fork and a control breaking the same preload path produces 'Parse Error: Preload file ... does not exist', and Godot exits 0 either way so the log is the signal. A GENERATOR'S OUTPUT PATH IS PART OF THE MOVE: parse_cursor_bob.py wrote tile_knife.json to assets/sprites/ and repointing only the consumer would have left a stale copy at the old path and a never-refreshed one at the new with nothing failing to say so (the glove half stays put -- UI reads it). Register now 48 sites over 28 rows: B 9, C 1, D 12, E 6. Predictions 5 of 6 held, 5 GRADED HELD by the above; PREDICTION 2 MISSED BY FOUR: this ADR said 2 input actions and the tree holds 8, because six of them travel as StringName consts (CURSOR_ACTIONS, PAN_ACTIONS, CURSOR_CONFIRM_ACTION, CURSOR_INSPECT_ACTION) and reach is_action_pressed through a LOOP VARIABLE, so no is_action_pressed('literal') grep can see them. Had the register been built to agree with its own ADR it would have read 2, matched, and been blind to three quarters of the arm -- dec. 10's thesis arriving one arm early and about the ADR rather than a later pass. The register also found a defect in itself: '^\s*global uniform' under re.M matched from the BLANK LINE ABOVE a declaration because \s matches a newline, reading psx_dither.gdshaderinc:6 for a declaration on 7; fixed to [ \t]* and pinned. 🔴 THIS REGISTER NEEDS THE OPPOSITE STRIPPER FROM ITS THREE SIBLINGS: they scan for SYMBOLS via touch_matrix.strip_noncode, which BLANKS string literals, and every site here lives inside one -- reusing it would have reported a clean tree over eleven live edges. And res:// CONTAINS //, so a shader comment stripper run first eats every #include. _literals walks characters tracking quote state so both are safe at once, and two tests assert it against the tree rather than in prose. dec. 10: the register goes BEFORE the moves (ADR-0192 dec. 1 via ADR-0196 dec. 1) because Class A erases its own baseline -- once tile_overlay.tres lives in the addon, a scanner that never learned to read res:// out of a .gd reports the same 0 as a correct one; named burn-down never a filter (#424), both ratchet arms seeded, every seed constructs BOTH site and row (a control has expired on success four times in this family). dec. 11: criterion 1 is NOT touched -- different axis, open under ADR-0196 dec. 8. THREE RECEIVED COUNTS WERE WRONG AND ARE CORRECTED ON THE RECORD: shader globals 12->6 (the guard's 12 is the whole walk and six of those lines are PSXDisplay.gd PUSHES, which are GDScript sets and cannot fail a compile; 4 are declared in the addon, 2 arrive via platform #includes), cross-addon 23 rows -> 9 distinct SYMBOLS (23 is the printed row count; the guard's headline 119 is the walk), and 'ROM-derived and gitignored' -> 5 of 9 targets tracked. The third changed the design; the other two would have set the register's expected population wrong, the one input dec. 10 cannot get wrong and still grade anything. Also measured: Tune. appears 12 times in the addon and ALL TWELVE are comments/docstrings -- the addon reaches the store only through TunePort, ADR-0139 dec. 12 working as ruled. Consequences name the reconciliation rather than doing it: check_addon_portability.py still reports OK over 11 asset sites, two instruments answering one goal and disagreeing, owned by the pass that closes Class B. |

The runner-up (keep both names, ask hosts to prefer the rig) is REJECTED for ADR-0192 dec. 4's reason: 'published' becomes a NAMING CONVENTION, the exact thing criterion 1 replaces. 🔴 THE MEMBER SURFACE WAS MEASURED, NOT ASSUMED, AND ONE MEMBER WAS NOT REAL: GPUArena.gd:559's `tile_cursor.active_tile()` is INSIDE A COMMENT and was on the port's first draft -- a grep for `handle.member` that does not strip comments invents surface. The real union across all of src/ is 4 signals + grid_pos + SEMI_MODE_LABELS + 2 construction-only exports; CursorController's host surface is THREE methods. dec. 2: TWO entry points, bind() for a scene-authored $TileCursor (GPUArena, EffectViewerScene, FireCastReproScene) and mount() for a host with no cursor node (NavigatorMain) -- NO SCENE STRUCTURE CHANGES, ADR-0204's lesson; mount() sets the exported NodePaths BEFORE add_child because _ready resolves them relative to the parent, and records ownership so dispose() frees the cursor there and not at a bind() site. dec. 3: exactly ONE emit site per relayed signal. dec. 4: every host handle is CursorRig BY TYPE (wire_cursor, bind_map, mount_over_map, CursorDebugPanel.setup), so this is NOT the forwarder ADR-0170 dec. 1 refused -- that refusal was about the RECEIVER being untypeable, and delegation was never the objection. dec. 6: FORBIDDEN KEEPS THE TWO DEAD NAMES. Four of its six now have no class_name at all and score 0 by construction; that is why they stay -- re-adding `class_name TileCursor` must red, and REMOVING AN ENTRY BECAUSE IT READS 0 RETIRES THE GUARD ON THE DAY IT STARTS WORKING. CursorRig joins DECLARED_PUBLISHED. 🔴 dec. 7: THE HANDOFF PREDICTED THE SEED TESTS WOULD EXPIRE ON SUCCESS AND THEY DID NOT. All 30 assertions were audited BEFORE any row was deleted; every one reads seeded output and test_a_LISTED_row_prints_above_the_verdict CONSTRUCTS both site and row. 15/15 pass with PUBLISH_BURN_DOWN empty -- the first control in this family to survive its own success, because a previous pass built it to. Side effects: criterion 4 138 -> 137 (NavigatorMain's TILE_CURSOR_SCENE_PATH const is gone, mount() owns the instantiation); 5 tests/ annotations became Node3D; TileCursorIntegrationTest now builds a real CursorRig and exercises the SHIPPED seam. 🔴 THE README'S class_name COUNT WAS MADE CORRECT BY ACCIDENT: it said 30 while the tree held 31, and CursorRig in / TileCursor + CursorController out is net -1, so it is now right for a reason nobody chose. An earlier draft asserted 'now 29 and stale' -- arithmetic on a number the README had already got wrong. Checked, not assumed; the README now carries the drift history and a re-count command. Alternatives rejected: hand out TileCursor.tscn (answers construction, leaves 13 annotations); expose rig.cursor for the handoff sites (reintroduces the duck-typed receiver ADR-0170 dec. 1 objected to); move SEMI_MODE_LABELS to the schema addon (it is a debug label table, not schema). |

THE CROSS-ADDON EDGE IS THE DOMINANT CASE, NOT A CORNER: exmateria_battlefield names NINE of the ten globals (Fold in 9 of its files, CellMarking 6, TunePort 6, DepthMode 4, TerrainCell 4, ColorStack 3, ColorRecipe 3, DisplayPort 2, PsxNum 1) and render names Fold at 5 sites -- already DECLARED via plugin.cfg deps= (ADR-0194 dec. 7), already staged by rig.sh, and check_addon_portability passes it rc=0 today under 'the kernel and the port'. dec. 1: one global per addon, ExMateriaX, in addons/exmateria_x/exmateria_x.gd -- not a count and not merely a prefix; arm_rot already asserts the folder-named form, so render's fix is a FACADE (4 lines), not the cheaper-looking rename. dec. 2: complete-close is a DESCRIPTION OF SCOPE, never a precondition. dec. 3: ADR-0211 dec. 8 OVERTURNED. dec. 4: symbol axis alone (ADR-0205 criterion 4 and check_addon_install unchanged). dec. 5: the shader #include channel is OUT OF SCOPE and SAID SO -- ~20 includes reach platform and ~20 schema from assets/shaders/ and battlefield; a .gdshaderinc declares no class_name so it carries none of the hazard, and no GDScript const routes a preprocessor include. dec. 6: check_addon_globals.py WIDENS to a FACADES dict over four addons -- RESTORING the shape of its own ancestor exmateria-sound/tools/check_globals.py, which it was lifted from and NARROWED; burn-downs battlefield 0 / platform 3 / schema 6 / render 1, seeded in the SAME commit because run_tests_parallel preflight calls it and an early-aborting preflight hides every guard behind it. The >40-file vacuity control becomes PER-ADDON: addons/exmateria_render holds TWO .gd files. dec. 7: arm 3 covers all four facades and a SIBLING-ADDON citation is valid provided the entry says 'sibling namer' (schema's heaviest consumers are inside battlefield, so a src/-only rule leaves Fold's load-bearing use uncitable). dec. 8: the schema facade costs 3-of-4 stock loading -- Fold.gd:36's const FOLD_LAYER preload of a CompositorRenderLayer is a stock parse error that cascades to the facade file; ACCEPTED, with plugin.cfg's now-false comment corrected in the same commit and the lazy-load repair filed as #721. dec. 9: check_addon_portability's cross-addon report goes COARSER (21 rows naming a symbol -> ~19 naming a folder) -- the REVERSE of ADR-0211 dec. 4's 'aliasing makes coupling more visible', which is true for the HOST and false for a guard that already resolved bare types; recorded, repair filed as #722. dec. 10: three PRs ascending -- register+render, platform, schema -- and PR 3's full run DISCHARGES ADR-0211 dec. 9's owed suite, since it executes on a tree carrying the merged battlefield migration. dec. 11: rules the FOUR godot-learning addons only; sound/spu stay ruled by the repo-root ADR-0003 and its own check_globals.py, and the two corpora share one number space so a bare ADR-0003 here addresses unit-encode instead. Footprint across six addons 13 -> 6, one per addon, every one brand-prefixed and folder-named. REJECTED: rename FoldSurface instead of a facade (cheaper by nothing; buys a name that cannot grow). REJECTED: close schema's 16 path reaches in a preparatory pass first (same edit). REJECTED: rule only platform and render (leaves the six worst-colliding names). REJECTED: clone the guard three times. REJECTED: fix Fold.FOLD_LAYER inside the migration. REJECTED: rule all six addons in one document. REJECTED: fold this into ADR-0211 as an amendment (check_adr_shape caps one dated section; dec. 8 is folded IN PLACE instead, adding none). |

Composed with ADR-0190 / arm 4b (only the kernel and the port may DECLARE one) this yields ONLY THE PORT PROVIDES, which closes the name for every consumer present and future instead of re-opening it once per addon. dec. 2: exmateria_platform provides all six (psx_par, psx_dither_enabled, psx_fx_stretch, psx_cursor_stretch, psx_unit_stretch, psx_camera_angle); exmateria_battlefield's PROVIDED_GLOBALS becomes [] and it keeps PROVIDED_ACTIONS, on ADR-0147/0148's diff-don't-duplicate rule -- NOT ADR-0146's, which does not contain that sentence (ADR-0149 records the misattribution). dec. 3: check_addon_portability arm 4c ENFORCES it, keyed on `!= the declaring addon` and NOT on `is None` -- an is-None predicate reads a name provided for years by the wrong addon as green, which is exactly the state that let the rig be uninstallable while every guard passed. dec. 4: `psx_gamma` is excluded and left OPEN -- its declaration is the host's (3 sites under src/ and assets/), so under dec. 1 it is nobody's to provide, and its absence is REPORTED not silent since ADR-0203 dec. 7. dec. 5: the arm does not ship alone -- its predicate is A STRING APPEARING IN plugin.gd, so PlatformProvidesTest.gd calls provide_into against a double and ships in the same commit (ADR-0203 dec. 4); its 7th arm reads the `global uniform` lines out of the addon's own .gdshaderinc at RUNTIME and diffs both directions against provided_keys(). dec. 6: the paste block in the port's README is SIX entries, not the five ADR-0190 dec. 4 wrote before #744. Condition 2 is NECESSARY NOT SUFFICIENT and the ADR says so: 593 of 789 tests/*.gd name a host path, so it is a VETO not an election. dec. 3: both numbers print as `n (+m oracle)` and are NEVER summed; a third verdict sentence covers criterion 4 at 0 over a non-empty oracle channel. dec. 4: unit.tres STAYS on the production channel and is not paid here -- draining it means the rig ships its own base material and the host injects only textures, a design question ADR-0217 does not answer. MEASURED: exmateria_sprite_rig criterion 4 7 -> 1 (+6 oracle) with NOT ONE LINE OF THE TREE MOVED, so the drop is a fact about the SCANNER (ADR-0205 dec. 7) and every report says so in its own text; exmateria_battlefield unmoved at 0 (+0 oracle); suite 50 -> 69. A REACH HAS A BUCKET AND AN ADDRESS AND GOAL #5 ONLY EVER READ THE BUCKET. score_goals.outbound_reaches returned (rel,kind,target,dst,[lines]) where dst is classify()'s BUCKET: record() computed the resolved dst_path, keyed the row on the bucket and THREW THE PATH AWAY, then dropped every row whose bucket was not one of the eleven SYSTEMS. So the BUDGET question (is the target's system one of the eleven) was askable and the BOUNDARY question (does the target resolve outside every addon root) was not, and exmateria_sprite_rig printed `0 cross-system reach lines leave the addon`, met, while FIVE lines named a class_name declared in src/ -- JsonAsset x4 (src/data/JsonAsset.gd, infrastructure) and AnimationNames x1/2 lines (src/data/AnimationNames.gd, content). Arm 5 cannot say it: class_name_homes() is built from the addon roots alone. WIDENING THE BUCKET FILTER IS THE OBVIOUS FIX AND IS WRONG -- measured, accepting every bucket gives 30 rows tree-wide of which 25 target addons/exmateria_platform/, which is what a portable addon is ALLOWED to reach (ADR-0139 dec. 12, ADR-0202 dec. 2). dec. 1: outbound_reaches returns Reach(rel,kind,target,dst,dst_path,lines) as a NamedTuple and the ARITY IS THE POINT -- score_goals' own `sum(len(h[4]))` would have counted CHARACTERS of a path and raised nothing. dec. 2: record() keeps every reach that LEAVES THE ADDON (only startswith(inside) excluded) and each of the exactly THREE consumers filters, through the named score_goals.cross_system(). dec. 3: shipped under a NOTHING-MOVES gate -- goal #5's rendered line and check_addon_portability's complete output byte-identical before and after, over all seven roots, because a scanner change that moves a burn-down is indistinguishable from debt being paid (ADR-0205 dec. 7). dec. 4: ARM 7 is arm 5's rule ONE LEVEL OUT -- a class_name under an addon root DECLARED outside every addon root, ENFORCING, for every subject, sitting before arm 1's `continue` for arm 6's reason, predicate arm 6's ^addons/[^/]+/ against dst_path. dec. 5: arm 7's universe is the class_name shape ALONE -- a scope over SHAPES, not #424's filter over ROWS, the same move _ARM6_REFERRERS makes when it subtracts the four shader suffixes because arm 3 owns them; the other five shapes are arms 2, 2b, 3 and 6's, each with the right stripper. dec. 6: the five rows ship NAMED in ARM7_BURN_DOWN with an owner and a reason each, both directions failing, stale scoped to the walk because --root NARROWS the subject. dec. 7: JsonAsset and AnimationNames are NOT one row twice -- AnimationNames reads res://assets/sprites/animation_names.json, so moving the class carries a host ASSET address under an addon root and lands the row on arm 6 instead. | | | | | | A GUARD THE SUITE DOES NOT LIST IS A GUARD NOBODY RUNS, AND THE LIST IS A REGISTER. tests/run_all_tests.sh names every static guard by hand with no discovery loop anywhere, so a guard is run because somebody remembered a line. MEASURED on origin/main de8ae7af4: 57 check_*.py under godot-learning/tools/, 48 invoked, 9 INVOKED BY NOTHING -- and four of those nine are RED (check_focus_anchor, check_residue, check_root_set, check_test_baseline), sitting on trunk unseen. COST IS NOT THE REASON: all nine together run in under ten seconds against a 342 s pre-flight. This is the FOURTH instance of the shape (#565 check_vault_anchors; root ADR-0003 dec. 7 check_addon_portability, which NAMED the file; ADR-0175 arms 3 and 4; ADR-0232's test_score_goals.py, filed as #876 for the test population) and the first measured across the whole guard population; each earlier one was paid by adding one line, which made the next instance no less likely. check_blueprint_walk's own red (#728, #770) is ALREADY PAID -- rc 0, zero unclassified -- and nobody noticed, because nothing was looking. A bare grep gets two things wrong: two guards are called as python3 "$PROJECT_DIR/tools/x.py" not uv run python, and the runner NAMES check_tune_owner_self_registration.py and check_test_list_coverage.py in prose, so a substring search scores a mention as a run. dec. 1: tools/check_guard_registry.py exists, is in the pre-flight, and grades itself. dec. 2: the register is NOT_IN_PREFLIGHT keyed by basename, each row a kind plus a dated owner plus a reason -- the reviewed-literal idiom of SCENE_BURN_DOWN and DECLARED_MOUNTS. dec. 3: exactly two kinds. EXEMPT is a permanent argument and needs no ticket; OWED is a promise, and an OWED row MUST name an issue because a promise with no address is one nobody will keep. Ships 0 exempt, 4 owed. dec. 4: arms 1-3 ENFORCE, arm 4 REPORTS. Arm 2 is the ratchet's other direction and has TWO halves -- a row naming a guard that no longer exists, and a row excusing a guard the pre-flight NOW INVOKES; without the second, paying a debt leaves its row behind forever and the excuse list can only grow. Arm 4 prints the owed count every run and says in the same breath that it is not a pass (ADR-0205 dec. 7). dec. 5: five guards WIRED IN, four DECLARED, and the line between them is exactly the line between green and red -- wiring a red guard into the gate aborts the full suite for everyone, which is what #871 had been doing. The four reds are #879 focus anchor, #880 residue, #881 root set, #882 test baseline; paying one deletes its row and arm 2 forces that. check_focus_anchor's 4 problems are TWO FILES AT TWO ADDRESSES (PlayerCamera.gd and TileCursor.gd moved into addons/exmateria_battlefield/ while ADR-0177 dec. 4's grandfather list still names src/scenes/) and check_test_baseline's MOVED TO NOWHERE traces to 5f8703961 renaming ScenarioEventPathfinderTest -- both moves, not new debt. dec. 6: the registry answers ONE question and its three blind spots are in its own docstring -- it does not know whether a guard's own TESTS run (#876, different population and runner), whether an invoked guard is ENFORCING, or anything outside godot-learning/tools/ (../exmateria-sound/tools/check_globals.py is named as outside). REJECTED: discovering and running every check_*.py, on ADR-0232 dec. 2's grounds -- a discovered population cannot express an exemption and this one demonstrably needs four, and it would have wired four red guards into the gate on day one. Also rejected: report instead of enforce (a report nobody reads is how all four earlier instances survived). 17 arms in tools/test_check_guard_registry.py, EVERY RED ONE CONSTRUCTED -- a seeded tools/check__registry_seed.py written into the real tree, a seeded row, a seeded runner -- so none stops firing when the owed set reaches zero; 3 exercise the invocation predicate (a comment mention is not a call, both real spellings are) and 6 read the shipped rows because claims about rows cannot be made by a constructed one. Recorded: the seed helper first REPLACED the register, which made the four real owed guards read as unregistered so arm 1 fired for the wrong reason -- seed rows are ADDED to the shipped register, never substituted for it. |
| [0234](0234-a-port-half-is-not-shipped-until-both-directions-of-the-value-are-on-it.md) A port half is not shipped until both directions of the value are on it | `method` | · | · | 7 | `0129`, `0150`, `0175`, `0202`, `0217`, `0223`, `0228`, `0229`, `0232` |
| | | | | | A PORT HALF IS NOT SHIPPED UNTIL BOTH DIRECTIONS OF THE VALUE ARE ON IT. #848 was the last row on the fifth stranger rig's known_failures.tsv and it offered two options -- (a) build a DisplayPort-shaped class_name in exmateria_platform beside TunePort, or (b) get_node_or_null(^"PSXDisplay") at the call site -- with the instruction 'measure that before choosing'. Measured: NEITHER WAS OPEN. Option (a) HAD ALREADY SHIPPED as addons/exmateria_platform/display_port/DisplayPort.gd since #590, and its own docstring already named #583's rename as reaching nothing in any addon. What had not shipped was its READ half: #590 moved set_camera_angle onto the port and left the mirror that call writes reachable only as a PROPERTY on the autoload, which serves the host and cannot serve an addon, so CameraRelativeRenderer.gd went on naming the bare PSXDisplay identifier at :35 and :43 and took four more files down with it including exmateria_sprite_rig.gd, the addon's ONE published global name. dec. 1: a push-only port half reads as complete for exactly as long as nobody outside the host wants the value back -- no instrument asks whether a port is SYMMETRIC, only whether an addon names something it should not; DisplayPort gains live_camera_angle() as a fourth member. dec. 2: a SYSTEM addon reaching a foreign autoload has ONE conformant shape and it is the port -- not the bare identifier (arm 2, and it does not parse) and NOT the node-path string, because arm 2b's free set is the kernel, the platform PORT and an addon reaching its OWN singleton, and ADR-0202 dec. 7's conformant ruling is scoped to TunePort.gd because TunePort IS the port; quoting it at a system's call site inverts it. The issue's '(b) is right if this is the only site' asked the wrong question -- WHICH ADDON IS NAMING decides it, not how many sites. dec. 3: the absent value is 0 and it is FOUND, not a chosen identity like NO_STRETCH=1.0 -- it is the autoload's own boot value for the mirror AND the fallback the one call site already spelled, so the port changed no behaviour on landing. dec. 4: the rig's twelve rig-owned goal #7 lines are renamed HERE and not deferred to #583. dec. 5: Sprite Rig goal #5 is met on BOTH halves, which is ADR-0232's join doing its job. dec. 6: goal #7 is NOT closeable from inside this addon and GOALS.tsv says so rather than implying a follow-up. THREE FALSE PREMISES, EACH RESTATED IN THREE PLACES. (1) 'no such port exists today' -- it has since #590. (2) '#848 is ordered behind #583' (ADR-0232's ticket line, known_failures.tsv's header and the session handoff) -- DisplayPort absorbs the spelling at its own node path INSIDE exmateria_platform, so paying #848 first DELETED the row rather than moving its signature text; if there was an ordering it ran the other way. (3) GOALS.tsv #7's 'it is #583's work' -- measured, the port does NOT spell the concept psx (live_camera_angle and set_camera_angle are prefix-free on both the autoload and the port), so the psx in those twelve lines was this addon's own local spelling echoing nothing. TRUE and load-bearing: 'no port is owed, it is an install step' -- plugin.cfg declares exmateria_platform a dep and rig.sh stages every declared dep with cp -rL, so the script is present and only the [autoload] binding was absent. MEASURED: jargon 34 -> 22 for this addon; score_goals has no partial credit and no provider exemption, so #7 needs all 22 gone, 20 of which name a psx_* symbol another package publishes and 2 are PSX_FOLD_GAIN which the same register books to goal #8 -- #583 as scoped clears 2 of the 22 (the psx_par includes; unit.gdshader:78 and unit_additive.gdshader:71 name psx_unit_stretch on the same line and survive), so #583 does not close #7 either and #899 carries the provider-side question. The rig went 72 -> 73 files on the LAST row, not proportionally, which is known_failures.tsv's own row-count observation measured in the second direction. The un-fireable 'PSXDisplay.live_camera_angle if PSXDisplay else 0' idiom now appears NOWHERE: its last copy was src/units/Unit.gd:975, in the host, where the name is bound and the guard was merely dead. Sprite Rig scores 6 met / 4 open and NONE of the four open rows is a Sprite Rig job -- #6/#10 are the authoring project, #7 is #899's, #8 is the epilogue's E1. |
| [0238](0238-a-global-uniform-is-validated-only-in-the-editor-and-the-debt-is-silent.md) A `global uniform` is validated only in the editor, and the debt it leaves is silent | `method` | · | · | 2 | `0129`, `0169`, `0190`, `0194`, `0203`, `0220`, `0229`, `0232`, `0234` |
| | | | | | A GLOBAL UNIFORM IS VALIDATED ONLY IN THE EDITOR AND THE DEBT IT LEAVES IS SILENT. The handoff named 'make check_addon_portability arm 2 and arm 4 ENFORCING for any addon that has a stranger rig' as the strongest next work, on the premise that the headings' 'there is no standalone project yet' had expired -- there are five rigs and all five pass. Measured: THE PREMISE IS EXPIRED AND THE PLAN STILL CANNOT WORK. ADR-0169's Measurement quotes servers/rendering/shader_language.cpp:9866 verbatim and accurately; the enclosing `if` FOUR LINES ABOVE IT is not in the quote and carries the engine's own comment -- `if (uniform_scope == SCOPE_GLOBAL && Engine::get_singleton()->is_editor_hint()) { // Type checking for global uniforms is not allowed outside the editor.` So a `global uniform` a project has not declared is a compile error ONLY under is_editor_hint(), and a stranger rig, an exported game and `godot --path X scene.tscn` are all outside it. MEASURED on fork 4.8 AND stock 4.7.1, with a live control (a deliberate syntax error DOES fail, witness ABSENT): an undeclared global uniform compiles as inline code, as a real .gdshader file, and through an #include -- the rig's exact arm shape -- exposes its uniforms, renders a frame, and emits only a DRAW-time WARNING from update_uniform_buffer, 'Shader uses global parameter X, but it was removed at some point. Material will not display correctly.' The value reads the type's zero. In the editor (is_editor_hint true) it DOES fail with the quoted error, which is the one true restatement in the corpus -- ADR-0203 dec. 6's 'the first enable in a bare project reports shader compile errors', accurate because the editor open IS the gated path. dec. 1: the mechanism is editor-only and every rule built on the old mechanism STANDS -- ADR-0220 dec. 1, arms 4b/4c, PROVIDED_GLOBALS and PlatformProvidesTest were reasoning to the right conclusion from a wrong premise. dec. 2: the debt is SILENT and that is a PROMOTION not a downgrade -- a compile error fails loudly at install, a zero-valued psx_par collapses every vertex's clip-space x and blanks the screen with nothing in the log; ADR-0203 dec. 7 and ADR-0220's OWN CONTEXT already said so, in the sentence immediately after the false one, and neither reached the thirteen other sites that kept saying COMPILE. dec. 3: arm 4 stays REPORTING and its heading loses a premise now false twice (there are five standalone projects, and the debt is not 'stricter when it lands'); an IOU naming the wrong blocker is worse than one naming none because it sends the next session to build something that cannot work. dec. 4: tests/stranger/exmateria_platform/global_uniform_unvalidated.tscn pins the engine fact and goes RED ON GOOD NEWS -- stranger_fork_absent.gd's shape; if a future engine validates outside the editor the arm fails and the response is to make arm 4 enforcing, not to fix the arm. dec. 5: RIG-OWNED not addon-owned, because godot-learning/project.godot declares seven [shader_globals] and would falsify the claim (ADR-0194 dec. 4 in the other direction). dec. 6: arm 2's standalone-parse DEBT register is ALREADY EMPTY (#848 paid the last row, the block is guarded by `if debt:` and does not print), so converting it is a ratchet not a discovery; the handoff counted TWO 'Not enforced' headings and there are THREE (cross-addon class_name DEBT is the third) and only arm 4's prints today. dec. 7: goal #7's majority owner is the KERNEL, recorded on #899. MEASURED against the second named hypothesis, 'the 20 goal-#7 symbols are largely the 12 shader globals' -- FALSE. Of exmateria_sprite_rig's 22 jargon lines, 2 BIND a shader global, 6 touch one of the seven names at all, and 14 are the kernel's (10 psx_ot_computed_depth/psx_ot_depth, 3 kernel #includes, 1 psx_color_apply); 2 are PSX_FOLD_GAIN, which the same register books to goal #8. In the other direction all 12 shader-global debt lines are exmateria_platform's own files and none is in the sprite rig, so THE TWO REGISTERS SHARE ZERO ROWS and one provider-side rename discharges neither the other; score_goals has no partial credit so goal #7 does not move. #899's title books the block to 'a PROVIDER-side psx_ retirement' and the majority provider is exmateria_schema, not exmateria_platform -- psx_ot_*/psx_color_* across 5 addons and 23 files. ADR-0129 dec. 10 ALREADY RULED that rename and its trigger fired at extraction #1; it never ran because the file landed in addons/exmateria_schema/ rather than the addons/render/ path dec. 10 wrote. #583's body names psx_ot_depth.gdshaderinc and its ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA do not, which is how the kernel half stayed nobody's. SEEDED BOTH CONTROLS: a [shader_globals] row added to the rig's project.godot reds the anti-vacuity arm and making the deliberately-broken shader valid reds the live control -- 15 passed 2 failed, rig rc=1, both restored. 14 sites carried the false mechanism and are corrected, 2 more said it and were RIGHT (the FIRST ENABLE blocks, where the editor open IS the gated path) and are annotated; the platform rig gains the FIRST rig-owned scene outside the sprite rig, exercising a rig.sh loop that has existed since ADR-0229 and run zero scenes. NOT CLAIMED: nothing reads the draw-time warning, so a missing entry is still undetected at runtime; arm 4c's static declarer/provider diff remains the only enforcement and ADR-0203 dec. 1's enable-time gap is unchanged. |
| [0240](0240-goal-7-scores-systems-so-a-consumer-exemption-counts-the-jargon-nowhere.md) Goal #7 scores systems, so exempting a consumer counts the provider's jargon nowhere | `method` | · | · | 0 | `0117`, `0129`, `0145`, `0149`, `0150`, `0171`, `0234`, `0238` |
| | | | | | GOAL #7 SCORES SYSTEMS, SO EXEMPTING A CONSUMER COUNTS THE PROVIDER'S JARGON NOWHERE. The handoff put three conjunctive decisions on Sprite Rig's goal #7 and named the second -- is a CONSUMER of an exempt system's published vocabulary itself exempt? -- 'the highest-leverage claim on the board', worth 14-20 of 22 with no rename at all. Measured: THE PREMISE IS FALSE. classify() books all four provider files (psx_ot_depth.gdshaderinc, psx_color_stack.gdshaderinc, psx_par.gdshaderinc, psx_unit_stretch.gdshaderinc) to `schema` and `platform`, which are BUCKETS and not members of SYSTEMS, so the publisher is not `Render` and there is no row-10 exemption to inherit; extraction #4's kernel split moved the depth model and the colour modes into exmateria_schema, where `Render` consumes them on the same terms Sprite Rig does (exmateria_render scored 3 lines of its own, not 20). And because the ten goals score systems -- the scorecard prints 'the classifier books no file here to a system ... so it is not scored here' above both provider addons -- a consumer exemption would have removed those 20 lines from every consumer's count while nothing counted them at their declaration: countable by NO row. ADR-0145 dec. 4 refuses exactly that shape ('This is reported and not adjusted for'; 'Netting it out would produce a flattering second instrument measuring a different thing'), which is the half of ADR-0150's map design score_goals.py's own comment cites it for; and an exemption travelling along an #include edge is the flag the map exists to prevent, acquired by an edge with nobody ruling on it. dec. 1 refuses the transitive route and keeps PLATFORM_EXEMPT at one entry (a system may still be ARGUED into the map). dec. 2: ADR-0129 dec. 10 executes now, re-addressed -- it never ran because it wrote the destination as res://addons/render/shaders/ot_depth.gdshaderinc while the files landed in exmateria_schema/ and exmateria_platform/, so no grep for the ruled path found the ruled file (ADR-0238 dec. 7 found the trigger had fired twice). THE CPU HALF HAD ALREADY RUN AND THAT WAS THE TELL: DepthMode.ot_depth() has carried the un-prefixed name all along, one file from a shader function called psx_ot_depth, and addons/exmateria_platform/README.md already booked the file rename to #583 by name. dec. 3: `par` becomes `pixel_aspect` per dec. 10's one exception, including the global uniform, both helpers, the file, the Tune slug and the psx-par-exempt marker; the UI half goes with it (render.psx_ui_par -> render.ui_pixel_aspect), because a world PAR called pixel_aspect beside a UI PAR called psx_ui_par is one concept spelled two ways. dec. 4: PSX_FOLD_GAIN -> FOLD_GAIN -- the identifier cannot record a PSX compromise inside crystal_fold.gdshader, a producer whose whole job is a PlayStation reproduction, so the prefix distinguishes nothing; the fact moves to the comment, which ADR-0149's rule scores at zero. dec. 5 names what is deliberately NOT renamed (psx_gamma, psx_dither_enabled, psx_camera_angle, the other three ADR-0044 stretches, PsxNum, PSXDisplay per ADR-0171 dec. 4, tests/TunePsxParTest) and why. dec. 6: a global uniform rename is verified by a RUNTIME read-back, never by a guard -- the guards are textual and ADR-0238 established the failure is silent outside the editor, a pixel_aspect of 0.0 collapsing every vertex's clip-space x with nothing in the log. dec. 7: historical records keep their spelling; only living documents move. MEASURED: sprite_rig 22 -> 0, schema 36 -> 0, render 3 -> 0, battlefield 118 -> 74, platform 68 -> 41; three Tune slugs and two [shader_globals] keys moved with the names. WITNESSES, all runtime: PlatformProvidesTest 54/54 over 7 arms, PSXDisplaySingleSourceTest 4/4, TunePsxParTest 5/5, AlignmentPanelTuneFieldTest 14/14, UnitMaterialVariantTest green, ColorStackGpuParityTest 11/11 (GPU compile+render through the renamed include), DepthModeTest 35/35, --import clean, nine check_*.py rc 0 before and after, score_goals.py rc 0. Sprite Rig goal #7 is `met` -- the first of its four open rows to close without an epilogue. OBSERVED, NOT FIXED: ADR-0117 row 10 names a subject `Render` now only partly owns, so a later pass reading row 10 as an address rather than a subject reaches the same false premise this one did. NOT CLAIMED: nothing detects a missing [shader_globals] entry at runtime (ADR-0238 dec. 3's gap is unchanged) and the full suite was not run -- two other sessions held the box and the claim rests on compile-and-render, which the named tests answer directly. |
| [0241](0241-unitprogression-is-the-catalogues-by-ownership-and-src-datas-by-address.md) `UnitProgression` is the catalogue's by ownership and `src/data/`'s by address, so extraction #5 is a tier and not a system | `method` | · | · | 4 | `0117`, `0126`, `0131`, `0139`, `0141`, `0153`, `0156`, `0157`, `0184`, `0202`, `0213`, … |
| | | | | | UNITPROGRESSION IS THE CATALOGUE'S BY OWNERSHIP AND src/data/'S BY ADDRESS, SO EXTRACTION #5 IS A TIER AND NOT A SYSTEM. ADR-0141 named Character Catalogue's blocking objection ('a store reaching the simulator is backwards') and ADR-0157 dec. 8 and ADR-0213 dec. 8 carried it unsettled through two more selections; ADR-0213 asked for it to be grilled BEFORE the #5 pass. Grilled on trunk d84fbdb0e and re-taken on 153e360bf nineteen commits later: THE MATRIX IS BYTE-IDENTICAL ACROSS BOTH (807 cross-system lines, every cell) and so is every arm-7 figure; only three SIZE cells moved (Battle 78->80 files / 24,609->25,516 lines, UI 141->142 / 41,863->42,198, Effects 58,103->58,112). dec. 1: OWNERSHIP IS THE CATALOGUE'S AND BATTLE'S OWN SOURCE SAYS SO -- src/units/Unit.gd types the field `Resource`, types bind_progression's parameter `Resource`, preloads the class by path, and its docstrings say the object 'outlives this node' and is 'shared with the roster entry'; Character.gd is the constructor and its ADR-0066 docstring already reads 'A Character = identity, the catalog record above the battle roster' and 'This is the thing a live Unit is spawned from'. Code-only lines naming the symbol: Character Catalogue 29, Battle 13, UI 9, assembler 9, Audio 3 -- and 12 of Battle's 13 are ENUM ACCESS, with exactly one line typing against it (GPUCombatPacker.gd:786). Unit.gd's 33 raw grep lines collapse to 3. dec. 2: AND THE RULING DOES NOT UNBLOCK -- APPLIED, IT DOUBLES THE DEBT. check_addon_portability arm 7's predicate over each proposed membership: 9 core files = 4 names / 45 lines; + UnitProgression = 9 names / 85 lines; all 14 classified files = 8 / 79; all 14 + UnitProgression = 13 / 119. StatCalculator (10) and AbilityType (3) surface only here -- both declared in src/data/ and both booked Battle, so UnitProgression naming them is INTRA-BUCKET and invisible to touch_matrix in both directions. dec. 3: the class does not split -- 41 of 53 functions touch a database, on level_up/change_job/add_jp/learn_ability and not only the derived-stat getters, and all 56 calls are STATIC class access with no injection point. dec. 4: OWNERSHIP AND ADDRESS ARE DIFFERENT QUESTIONS and fusing them is what made this look unanswerable for three passes -- the catalogue owns the record, but a rules object over five ROM databases belongs beside them, and five buckets consume it, not two. ADR-0213 dec. 10's shape for DisplayActivity, one tier further out. dec. 5: Character Catalogue is NOT #5, on measured ground and with no deferred question left -- 45 lines over 4 names at best-case membership against Battlefield's 9 and Sprite Rig's 6, and three of its four names are in one directory it does not own. dec. 6: EFFECTS' DEFERRAL IS RE-RATIFIED ON EVIDENCE, NOT ON ITS CITATION -- ADR-0157 dec. 9 and ADR-0213 both defer it as 'scheduled last by docs/agents/refactor-loop.md -> Extraction order' and THAT SECTION NO LONGER EXISTS in that file. The reason survives: map #262 is OPEN, scoped to src/effects/studio/, and that subtree is 116 of Effects' 191 files and 40,623 of its 58,103 lines (70%) with 355 commits since 2026-08-01. Effects has the cleanest boundary in the package (4 arm-7 names / 25 lines, 7 excluding TuneField and BaseDebugPanel) and stays deferred anyway. dec. 7: Campaign 8/59 (plus ADR-0157 dec. 11's live world_map collision), Cutscene 15/92, Battle 17/169, UI 29/322 -- all rejected. dec. 8: EXTRACTION #5 IS THE src/data/ TIER, A THIRD SHARED BUCKET AND NOT A SYSTEM. Across all six unextracted systems arm-7 debt sorts by DECLARING DIRECTORY: src/data/ 326 lines (43.7%), src/debug/ 202 (27.1%), src/scenarios/ 82, src/units/ 67 -- two directories are 70.8% of every remaining extraction's portability debt and neither is a member of SYSTEMS. TuneField alone is 170 lines, 22.8% of the total, one 491-line editor widget. Selecting a bucket is PRECEDENTED: exmateria_schema (ADR-0139) and exmateria_platform (ADR-0184, extraction #3 pass 6) were both built this way and neither was ever a system. THE METHOD'S OUT COLUMN IS BLIND TO THIS: OUT counts reach into other members of SYSTEMS, and content/generated/Debug are buckets, so three selection surveys ranked Character Catalogue first on a column that could not see what blocked it. The instrument that decides it is arm 7 -- ENFORCING for every subject, ARM7_BURN_DOWN deliberately EMPTY, and its only two historical rows were discharged by MOVING the class (JsonAsset to the port, AnimationNames into the rig). Measured: no extracted addon reaches content or generated on a single line; Battlefield/Sprite Rig/Render reach only platform and schema. TWO HANDOFF PREMISES FALSIFIED. (1) '37 of 41 outbound lines are one name' with per-file 28/6/3 -- the instrument reads 29 of 41 and 24/4/1, the gap being docstrings strip_noncode blanks. (2) '14 files, all in src/characters/ plus AllTemplatesSeeder' -- classify() books FIVE of the fourteen under src/debug/, 744 lines, 35% of the system, and they are why the OUT cell reads UI 2 and why 6 of the 38 Battle lines are `Unit`. Also corrected: Audio reads OUT 3, not 0 (ADR-0213's own table says so); the three lines are SfxRouter.gd naming UnitProgression. NOT DECIDED (ADR-0126, pass 1 selects and pass 3 designs): the tier's shape (extend schema, join platform, or a third addon -- src/data/ is 31 files / 23,069 lines across four buckets and does not move as one), where UnitProgression lands inside it, TuneField/BaseDebugPanel's 202 lines, the five src/debug/ files classify books to the catalogue, and whether Character Catalogue becomes #6. NOT CLAIMED: the arm-7 numbers are the PREDICATE applied by hand to a membership that does not exist -- outbound_reaches walks a real directory -- so the first run after the folder exists is the witness and it can only go up; arms 2 and 6 are not counted at all. Nothing was run in Godot. FILES #930 for the src/data/ tier's scope pass. |
