| | | | | | THE src/data/ TIER IS A THIRD ADDON, AND THE SPLIT THE SELECTION ASSUMED DOES NOT EXIST. ADR-0241 dec. 9 selected the tier and stated a premise while doing it -- 'Its four buckets (Battle 12 files, content 12, UI 5, generated 2) do not move as one and the split has to be ruled before anything moves' -- and #930 restates it verbatim. Measured across TEN candidate memberships with a new instrument: THE PREMISE IS FALSE. The whole directory is the cleanest membership available and every split is worse -- content alone opens at 25 arm-7 lines, UI alone at 27, all 31 at 8, all 31 + UnitProgression at 4, and the ruling membership at ONE. content-alone is 6x the whole directory because JobDatabase names UnitRole on 22 lines and classify() books one content and the other Battle, both in src/data/: A BUCKET IS WHO CONSUMES A FILE AND WAS NEVER A CLAIM ABOUT WHERE THE SEAM IS -- the general form of the error ADR-0241 dec. 2 found one level down with StatCalculator and AbilityType. dec. 1: NOT exmateria_schema, and the kernel's own gate closes twice -- ADR-0139 dec. 3's admission test is a row of ADR-0118 dec. 1's table and none of its ten rows is game content (the nearest, character records, is the row ADR-0139 dec. 4(a) already records failing on GambitList/UnitProgression/JobDatabase, two of them src/data/ files); and both mechanical vetoes fail on measurement -- the sink veto 5 edges / 20 lines, and SpriteRigContent is an autoload. dec. 2: NOT exmateria_platform -- ADR-0184 dec. 2's four files each encode a PSX platform fact and an FFT job table is not one; and the decisive evidence is the DIRECTION of the existing edge, the tier already CONSUMING the port on 11 lines of `const JsonAsset = ExMateriaPlatform.JsonAsset`, so folding a consumer into its provider deletes a working boundary rather than drawing one. dec. 4: the membership is 32 files / 24,111 lines and what crosses is ONE arm-7 line (BattleConditionalOpcode at BattleConditionalDatabase.gd:28), ONE arm-6 code path (StatusRegistry.gd:54 -> src/gpu/shaders/combat_common.glslinc), twelve assets/**.json payloads that travel, and ZERO host autoloads. NO EXTRACTED ADDON HAS EVER OPENED THIS CLEAN -- Battlefield was selected at nine outbound lines with an arm-1 burn-down, Sprite Rig at six. dec. 5: SpriteRigContent.gd is the ONLY file in the directory excluded -- it is the HOST ADAPTER answering exmateria_sprite_rig's ContentPort (ADR-0203 dec. 2), the addon's own file says so, nothing names it as a symbol, and moving it in would put the answer inside the question and ship one addon's host adapter inside another addon. dec. 6: UnitProgression moves in and ADR-0241 dec. 9's second question DISSOLVES rather than being answered -- it presupposed the databases staying behind, and all five plus StatCalculator and AbilityType are in the membership, so all 56 static calls become INTERNAL and no injection point has to be invented; measured, adding the class REDUCES the boundary 3 names/8 lines -> 2/4. dec. 7: AbilityLoadout moves in too (3 lines, no outbound of its own, Battle does not name it); BattleConditionalOpcode does NOT -- it is named 34 times by Campaign and once by the tier, so buying arm-7 zero by re-addressing a Campaign type on the strength of its smallest caller is the burn-down's own anti-pattern. Pass 3 picks an ADR-0211 dec. 4 alias, an inline, or one ARM7_BURN_DOWN row. dec. 8: the twelve asset payloads travel (ADR-0146 dec. 1's fold_layer.tres precedent); StatusRegistry stays in the tier THOUGH IT IS A CODEC, because its counterpart combat_common.glslinc is 1,511 lines of Battle's GPU kernel and cannot travel -- and removing it is MEASURABLY WORSE (arm 7 goes 0 -> 1 name / 3 lines because StatusEncoder then names it across). A codec whose counterpart cannot move is a case ADR-0139 dec. 7 and ADR-0146 dec. 7 do not cover; recorded, not resolved. dec. 9: THE PAYOFF IS 51% -- arm-7 debt across the six unextracted systems 758 -> 372 lines, membership held constant, with ADR-0241 dec. 7's table reproduced exactly as the control; by declaring directory src/data/ 326 -> 0 and src/units/ 67 -> 19. dec. 10: TuneField/BaseDebugPanel are #6 and S2 is RIGHT ABOUT THE MEASUREMENT AND WRONG ABOUT THE ORDERING -- zero arm-7 names between them, BaseDebugPanel zero outbound of any kind, TuneField's sole dependency the Tune autoload on 35 lines which is ARM 2's subject not arm 7's (which is why dec. 8's table could not see the cost) and whose port ADR-0234 already built. It stays #6 on share (src/debug/ becomes 202 of the remaining 372, 54.3%) and on DESTINATION, which is unsettled -- an editor widget is not a PSX platform fact and Debug is a system not a bucket, so it is a SELECTION question and ADR-0126 splits passes to keep selection out of them. dec. 11: S1 IS CLOSED BY tools/arm7_membership.py, an INSTRUMENT and not a guard (no pass/fail, no burn-down, so check_guard_registry correctly wants no row). CALIBRATED THREE WAYS because a fresh instrument agreeing with nothing is its own opinion: (1) against the real guard row-for-row INCLUDING LINE NUMBERS -- and the obvious form of that test is VACUOUS, every addon reading arm-7 zero so 0==0 passes whether it works or returns nothing, re-taken with the filter lifted where the sets are non-empty (battlefield 2/35, sprite_rig 2/19, render 1/1); (2) against ADR-0241 dec. 2's four hand-applied memberships 4/45, 9/85, 8/79, 13/119, all exact; (3) against dec. 7's six-system and dec. 8's by-directory tables, every row exact, both totals 746. tools/test_arm7_membership.py pins 1 and 2; seeded by removing strip_noncode, ALL EIGHT TESTS FAIL. ALSO MEASURED: no extracted addon names a single one of the 31 classes -- confirmed by two deliberately different instruments, the walk (zero) and a raw grep over addons/ including comments (14 hits in 8 files, EVERY ONE PROSE); and the one addon that did reach the tier was severed by ADR-0203 with a PORT, which points away from the kernel rather than towards it. CORRECTED: ADR-0241 dec. 9 says 'the five static database calls' where dec. 3's own measurement is 56 calls over five databases; #930's '56' is the same fact. REJECTED: ruling the split first as #930 asks (there is no split); extending schema; joining platform (the alternative that would have looked cheapest, since the codeOUT column would fall to near zero by fiat); a port per consumer (six adapters for a dependency one address change deletes, and the consumers are all still host systems); absorbing BattleConditionalOpcode to headline arm-7 zero; TuneField as #5 (rejected on share and destination, NOT on cost -- it is genuinely cheaper and this ADR does not dispute it); leaving UnitProgression behind (re-opens ADR-0241 dec. 4, a ruling). NOT DECIDED (ADR-0126 pass 3): the addon's name and subdirectory layout, the folder-named facade over 31 bare globals, the ~36 src/data/ rows in classify_blueprint.py that collapse to one directory rule, tools/generate_ability_database.py's hardcoded output paths, which severance BattleConditionalOpcode gets, the five src/debug/ files classify books to Character Catalogue, and whether Character Catalogue becomes #7. SOFT SPOTS: nothing moved so nothing was run in Godot and ADR-0241 S1's sentence carries forward UNCHANGED -- the first real guard run is the witness and the number can only go up, since dec. 11 closes the reproducibility gap and not the reality gap; 19,411 of the 24,111 lines are one generated file so the honest hand-written size is 4,434; AbilityCandidates reaches AbilityLoadout._SLOT_TYPE, a PRIVATE member, and dec. 7 makes that real coupling INVISIBLE rather than fixing it; the line count is a floor both ways (ADR-0131 dec. 6); and dec. 9's -386 is a PREDICTION that check_baseline.py --delta settles. |
| [0251](0251-the-almanac-is-thirty-two-names-behind-one-and-the-address-collapsed-while-the-buckets-did-not.md) The almanac is thirty-two names behind one, and the address collapsed while the buckets did not | `method` | · | · | 26 | `0126`, `0131`, `0139`, `0146`, `0147`, `0184`, `0194`, `0203`, `0211`, `0212`, `0223`, … |
| | | | | | THE ALMANAC IS THIRTY-TWO NAMES BEHIND ONE, AND THE ADDRESS COLLAPSED WHILE THE BUCKETS DID NOT. ADR-0243 dec. 12 left six design questions at the address and #945 asked for them; the move is BUILT as addons/exmateria_almanac/, 34 .gd + 13 JSON, 24,484 lines under the root. IT OPENS WITH AN EMPTY ARM-6 BURN-DOWN AND AN EMPTY ARM-7 BURN-DOWN, WHICH NO EXTRACTED ADDON HAS DONE BEFORE -- one better than ADR-0243 dec. 4's prediction on BOTH arms, and each was earned by a DELETION rather than declared away. dec. 1: the name is a COMPROMISE STATED OUTRIGHT -- 22 of 32 members are ROM tables or projections (exmateria_rom_tables would be exact) but the four gambits/ members are an ORIGINAL FFXII-style AI design this project invented, so a ROM-named package makes a false claim about a fifth of itself, which is the opposite of ADR-0184 dec. 2; an almanac is a book of tables you look things up in and claims nothing about provenance. Eight subdirectories named for the fact each holds, so ADR-0146 dec. 2's `ls` test is answerable. THE ONE DECISION IN THE PASS SETTLED BY TASTE AND THE CHEAPEST TO REVERSE (a git mv plus one substitution). dec. 2: THIRTEEN payloads travel, not twelve -- ItemDatabase carries items.json AND item_attributes.json -- each beside its reader rather than in a shared data/; 2.9 MB of the addon's 3.7 MB, and the STRANGER RIG is the only instrument that can prove they travelled (a payload left in assets/ is not a parse error, raises no arm-6 row because the path moved with the reader, and no static guard sees it). dec. 3: ONE published name, THIRTY-ONE published constants, members reach each other BY PATH. 31 published is the LARGEST FACADE SURFACE IN THE REPO (sprite_rig 20, sound 18, battlefield 17, render 1). Outward: 270 ADR-0211 dec. 4 alias lines across 134 host/test files, which makes `grep -rn ExMateriaAlmanac` a COMPLETE CENSUS of host->addon symbol coupling -- the measurement a bare class_name makes impossible. Inward: never through the facade (a member reaching its own package through its own exports depends on what it exports); const _Self = preload(own path) for self-typed statics; ONE preload cycle (JobDatabase <-> JobCandidates) broken by the package's only runtime load(), at jobs/JobDatabase.gd:148. progression/BaseStatsDatabase.gd is SHED BUT NOT PUBLISHED -- zero namers outside the addon, read only by its own siblings. check_addon_globals FACADES seeded with an EMPTY BURN_DOWN in the same commit that created the directory, a first. dec. 4: THE SHADER PARITY CHECK LEAVES and arm 6 opens EMPTY -- StatusRegistry loses SHADER_PATH, _shader_verified and _verify_against_shader() entirely; tests/StatusRegistryTest.gd::_test_shader_parity already re-implemented it IN FULL and is the STRONGER instrument (it _expects and FAILs; the registry's copy could only push_error from a static initializer firing at most once per boot with nothing asserting on it), and combat_common.glslinc is a HOST fact so the host's test owns it. ADR-0243 dec. 8 upheld on the CLASS and superseded on the PATH. dec. 5: OP_RUN_SCENARIO is DELETED -- THE FOURTH ANSWER TO A THREE-WAY QUESTION. ADR-0243 dec. 7 offered an alias, an inline, or the ARM7_BURN_DOWN's third row ever; the constant had TWO OCCURRENCES REPO-WIDE, its own declaration and the ADR's quotation of it, and no caller had ever read it (export_battle_conditionals.py consumes the opcode while decoding BTLEVT.BIN, so run_scenario arrives as a resolved integer). A THREE-WAY QUESTION WITH A LIVE PREMISE IS STILL A FALSE TRICHOTOMY IF NOBODY COUNTED THE OCCURRENCES: all three were answers to 'how does this class name a Campaign type', none to 'does it'. dec. 6: THREE OF ADR-0243'S COUNTS CORRECTED -- 32 class_names not 31 (the 31 coincidentally equals the PUBLISHED count so the two agree by accident), 13 payloads not 12, and the honest hand-written size is 4,490 lines, not the 24,111 quoted flat (19,689 of them are two generated files). dec. 7: #945 ITEM 5 IS REFUSED ON MEASUREMENT -- the ~36 src/data/ rows do NOT collapse to one directory rule, for three independent reasons: check_blueprint_walk check 4 runs a TWO-WAY content<->store rule over exactly this population and a blanket `content` booking needs a ~20-name exemption set its own comment refuses; booking it anything else re-books nineteen files whose consumers did not change (#744's rule one directory over); and a NEW bucket invalidates BASELINE.tsv's freeze (check_baseline check 2 requires other == classify_blueprint.OTHER exactly). So 32 exact rules were RE-ADDRESSED with buckets unchanged (2 generated, 11 content, 5 UI, 12 Battle) plus two Battle rows for files that held Battle through the src/units/ prefix they no longer match and two infrastructure rows. WHAT THE COLLAPSE WAS FOR IS STILL HAD: no catch-all under the root either, so a new file books UNCLASSIFIED and the classifier exits non-zero. dec. 8: generate_ability_database.py moves with its output (ADDON_DIR inside the addon, both templates lose class_name and gain the intra-addon preloads via one PRELOAD_BANNER so the paths move together); --check clean. dec. 9: the SIXTH stranger rig, engine="stock" (32 .gd, zero shaders/.gdshaderinc/.tres/scenes; every member extends RefCounted except UnitProgression which extends Resource) and deps="exmateria_platform" (the 11 ExMateriaPlatform.JsonAsset lines, the ADR-0223 dec. 8 PORT). THE FIRST ADDON TO ARRIVE AFTER THE JOIN THAT WOULD HAVE CAUGHT ITS ABSENCE -- _walk_roots.RIGS gained its row in the SAME COMMIT as the root, and rigs() arm 3's docstring had already named this case in the abstract as 'the sixth rig landing without a row'; tests/stranger/README.md's paragraph saying nothing detects a missing rig is REWRITTEN, not re-dated. dec. 10: THE PAYOFF IS 49.5%, and the prediction was a FLOOR the real number cleared FROM BELOW by 1.5 points -- arm-7 debt across the six unextracted systems 764 -> 386 on one tree before and after with one instrument (Battle 172->46, UI 322->127, Cutscene 107->103, Character Catalogue 79->32, Campaign 59->57, Effects 25->21); FOUR of six after-rows reproduce ADR-0243 dec. 9's prediction exactly and Cutscene +13 / Battle +1 are the gap. By declaring directory src/data/ 330->0, src/units/ 74->26, src/debug/ 202 unchanged. check_baseline.py --delta on the same pair: CROSS-SYSTEM REACHES 819 -> 694, -125. The BEFORE column is NOT ADR-0243's middle column and the difference is not drift -- ADR-0243 held membership constant by removing the tier from both sides; this takes classify()'s own booking on each tree. WITNESSES: the stranger rig PASSES 75/0 over 83 staged files; check_addon_portability rc 0 with the almanac listed, arms 2/6/7 all zero for it; check_addon_globals all three arms green (31 published, 51 citations, 0 in-addon); check_blueprint_walk green (723 files, 0 unclassified, 124 exact rules all live); check_unit_progression_resource green; generate_ability_database --check clean; RESIDUE.tsv regenerated. REJECTED: exmateria_rom_tables / exmateria_records; a shared data/ subdirectory for the 13 payloads; publishing all 32 including BaseStatsDatabase; the three ADR-0243 dec. 7 severances; keeping _verify_against_shader() and booking one ARM6_BURN_DOWN row; one directory rule in classify_blueprint. CAUGHT BEFORE LANDING: check_blueprint_walk check 4 would have gone SILENTLY BLIND -- scoped to src/data/*.gd it would have found one file left, reported OK and stopped asserting the store rule over 11 stores, and its is_store res://assets/**.json regex returns False for every moved database; both widened. SOFT SPOTS: the NAME is a taste call nothing measures and the reversal should happen now rather than after a second addon cites the path; StatusRegistry is still a codec whose counterpart cannot travel, a case ADR-0139 dec. 7 / ADR-0146 dec. 7 do not cover, recorded not resolved; dec. 10's before column is a SECOND READING of a table ADR-0243 took a different way and the ADR-0147/0148 warning applies to it; Cutscene's 103 vs the predicted 90 was not chased; and the 270 alias lines are a census ONLY WHILE THEY ARE THE ONLY ROUTE -- a host writing preload("res://addons/exmateria_almanac/...") directly is invisible to the grep dec. 3 claims as complete, and nothing enforces it today. Also corrected: check_addon_portability's shader-global heading said 'five stranger rigs'; ADR-0243's soft spot about AbilityCandidates reaching AbilityLoadout._SLOT_TYPE is no longer a BOUNDARY concern (both are members) though the coupling is unchanged. dec. 11: tools/test_arm7_membership.py's ADR-0241 calibration is RE-PINNED, NOT DELETED -- it was a claim about a tree THIS EXTRACTION CHANGED, the same shape as a guard's hardcoded directory surviving a move. A 4/45 -> 0/0, B 9/85 -> 0/0, C 8/79 -> 4/32, D 13/119 -> 4/32; UnitProgression.gd is inside an addon so arm 7's own predicate filters it and ADDING IT TO A MEMBERSHIP NOW COSTS NOTHING -- B == A and D == C is ADR-0243 dec. 6's 'dissolves rather than being answered' stated as an EQUALITY, and the test asserts the equality and not only the numbers. ROW A IS A LIVE INPUT TO THE NEXT SELECTION: ADR-0241 dec. 5 rejected Character Catalogue as #5 on '45 lines over 4 names at best-case membership' and that membership now reaches NOTHING outside an addon root. Row C reproduces dec. 10's Character Catalogue 79 -> 32 by a different route. Also fixed: test_check_addon_globals.MIN_GD_FILES needed the sixth row (the suite ABORTED in pre-flight on it, and the abort hid nothing else -- every tools/test_*.py was then run and the only other reds are missing numpy/pygltflib in a bare invocation). |
| [0254](0254-an-unattended-loop-may-delete-a-test-and-a-named-survivor-is-what-makes-that-safe.md) An unattended loop may delete a test, and a named survivor is what makes that safe | `method` | · | · | 0 | `0158`, `0160`, `0194` |
| | | | | | AN UNATTENDED LOOP MAY DELETE A TEST, AND A NAMED SURVIVOR IS WHAT MAKES THAT SAFE. The suite is 736 PROCESSES, not 736 functions: Godot boots with 28 autoloads in each and the cheapest test in the tree measures 2.31 s warm, so the COUNT is the cost. CLAUDE.md already carries the measurement that refutes per-test speed work -- 'Cutting slow tests barely helps (80% of the clock is 66% of the tests)' -- and names the only lever that pays: running FEWER. That makes delete and merge the two verdicts worth having, and both remove coverage. THE GATE: a green suite after a deletion proves nothing, because a deleted test cannot fail and the suite is greener the more it deletes. Before a test may be removed some OTHER test must be OBSERVED to red on the same seeded break; that test is the SURVIVOR and the pair (seeded_break, survivor) is what a morning review reads. dec. 1: seven audit verdicts -- KEEP, SPEED, DEMOTE, MERGE, DELETE, FLAKY, RED -- defined in CONTEXT.md 'Audit verdict' and recorded in docs/TEST-AUDIT-REGISTER.tsv. dec. 2: DELETE and MERGE require a named survivor OBSERVED to red, not argued to; a DELETE row with an empty survivor is an unfinished audit and the one thing to check in review. dec. 3: the loop does NOT fix the game -- a test already red on trunk is recorded RED and left alone. dec. 4: one commit per iteration, so a bad call is one git revert away. dec. 5: the loop works in its own worktree cut from origin/main, never from a working tree -- measured, the hub's staged config/tune_overrides.json had navigator.auto_advance_node true (the tunable NavigatorMain.gd documents as advancing past the world map) and NavigatorWorldMapFormationRenderTest failed in 243 s with 'the world map never mounted'. dec. 6: per-iteration verification is the test + its seeded break + scoped_tests.py; ONE full suite at the end of the night, not 736. dec. 7: the loop HOLDS when another session's Godot is running -- three suites on this box are RAM-gated to serial and a contended run produces verdicts neither session can trust. REJECTED: 're-run the suite, green means fine' (a deleted test cannot fail); 'never delete, only speed up' (59% of tests finish inside the boot cost, there is no speed left in them); 'never go below N tests' (a count floor protects a statistic -- it stops the loop at an arbitrary number whether or not the next deletion was justified, and permits every deletion before it whether or not those were); --fixed-fps suite-wide (A/B on two tests x 2 reps: 2.31-2.44 s in base, --disable-vsync and --fixed-fps 60 alike -- no measured payoff, so a 736-test delta-semantics change is unbought). SOFT SPOTS: this ADR does NOT claim the loop will shrink the suite -- whether 736 holds enough redundancy is UNMEASURED and the register's overlap column is a candidate list, not a finding; charter clauses 1 and 10 open at 736 violations each because they are new COLUMNS, not because 736 tests are broken (the real debt is 4 + 11 + 20 = 35 rows); and no baseline suite run has been taken, so the wall-clock target the loop stops on does not exist yet. |
| [0257](0257-a-debug-panel-is-not-a-member-and-the-order-that-counted-it-as-one-is-an-artefact.md) A debug panel is not a member, and the order that counted it as one is an artefact | `method` | · | · | 2 | `0035`, `0068`, `0126`, `0139`, `0175`, `0187`, `0223`, `0241`, `0243` |
| | | | | | A DEBUG PANEL IS NOT A MEMBER, AND THE ORDER THAT COUNTED IT AS ONE IS AN ARTEFACT. ADR-0243 dec. 10 ordered TuneField/BaseDebugPanel as extraction #6 on share -- '202 of the remaining 372 lines, 54.3%'. The measurement is right and the share is an artefact: every line it is computed over sits in a src/debug/*DebugPanel.gd file, a panel reaching its own base class, and classify() books a panel to the system it INSPECTS (ADR-0243 dec. 3), so the host debug window's debt is read as its subject's. dec. 1: a BaseDebugPanel subclass is not a member of any extraction and neither is the base class -- the rule is on the EXTENDS CHAIN, transitively, resolving both the class_name and the res:// spelling, NOT on the directory (src/debug/RosterDebugView.gd is a helper and stays; src/ui3/testing/CombatUIDebugPanel.gd is a panel outside src/debug/ and goes; 41 files match). Its evidence is the instantiation census -- NavigatorMain.gd and GPUArena.gd raise the panels, no system instantiates the panel that inspects it -- plus the fact that no shipped addon contains a panel, though addons DO ship debug code (MapGridOverlay.gd, depth_debug.gdshader, RigDebug.gd), so any rule phrased as 'debug files are not addon material' is FALSIFIED by the tree. dec. 2: EXTRACTION #6 IS CHARACTER CATALOGUE -- 10 files, 1,581 lines, arm 7 ZERO, arm 2 zero, arm 6 seven; the cleanest opening in the corpus, against Battlefield's 9 lines and Sprite Rig's 6, with Battle 17, Campaign 42, Cutscene 51, UI 64 behind it. dec. 3: THE RULE IS NOT A DISCOUNT -- dropping a panel can convert an internal name into an outbound one, so UI rises 17 names to 20 while its lines fall 131 to 64, and that arm is pinned by a test. dec. 4: #946 IS NOT AN EXTRACTION and is re-scoped, not closed -- outside src/debug/ the two files are named on TWO lines of code (AudioHostAdapter.gd:52, CombatUIDebugPanel.gd:2), and like-for-like the rule alone takes arm-7 debt 686->480 where #946 takes it 686->484, so the extraction buys EIGHT LINES beyond the rule. dec. 5: ADR-0243 dec. 10's measurement stands and its inference is expired; ALSO ITS TunePort CITATION DOES NOT RESOLVE -- ADR-0234 added a verb to a port ADR-0175 dec. 2 shaped and ADR-0187 built, which is the docs/adr/-excluded half of the blind spot ADR-0243 dec. 13 left open. dec. 6: assembler is excluded from candidacy on the ground that an entry point is the host (94 names/291 lines, 42% of the total, so the exclusion is stated rather than silent). dec. 7: AN AUTOLOAD INSIDE A MEMBERSHIP IS DESIGN WORK, NOT DISQUALIFICATION -- CharacterCatalog is reached on 35 host lines and ADR-0139 dec. 4b read as a candidacy veto would disqualify every remaining system but two. dec. 8: AllTemplatesSeeder.gd travels, on classify_blueprint.py:289's hand pin, since the instrument is silent. dec. 9: the rule ships as --exclude-panels on arm7_membership.py with six seed-red tests and NOT as a check_*.py, which would be vacuous -- no addon ships a panel to fail on. dec. 10: pass 1 selects, pass 3 designs. SUPERSEDES NOTHING; ADR-0243 decs. 1-9 and 11-13 stand. Soft spot: the rule's evidence is an ABSENCE across three addons built before anyone asked, and the arm-7 zero is ONE ARM OF SEVEN -- the first real check_addon_portability run after the folder exists is the witness and the number can only go UP. |
| [0262](0262-the-alias-route-hid-forty-nine-lines-and-the-almanac-reads-as-a-system-only-because-classify-books-by-consumer.md) The alias route hid forty-nine lines, and the almanac reads as a system only because classify books by consumer | `method` | · | · | 18 | `0126`, `0148`, `0157`, `0175`, `0184`, `0187`, `0202`, `0211`, `0212`, `0241`, `0243`, … |
| | | | | | THE ALIAS ROUTE HID FORTY-NINE LINES, AND THE ALMANAC READS AS A SYSTEM ONLY BECAUSE CLASSIFY BOOKS BY CONSUMER. ADR-0126 pass 2 -- the AUDIT -- for extraction #6, Character Catalogue (#1025). It audits and moves no files. ADR-0257 selected the system on ONE arm, arm 7, zero over ten files; this measures the other eight and the sentence is not zero. dec. 1: the membership is 10 files / 1,581 LINES on 153baa0d8 and #1025's 1,530 is stale; arm 7 is still 0/0, 41 commits after the selection; --exclude-panels drops zero files and that is a no-op, not the rule failing. dec. 2: ALL NINE ARMS -- arm 1 ONE line, arm 2 zero foreign, arm 2b zero, arm 3 zero (the membership ships no shader), arm 4 zero, arm 4b zero, arm 5 SIXTY-ONE, arm 6 seven over six paths, arm 7 0/0. Arm 1 is CharacterTemplateResolver.gd:38 naming ExMateriaSpriteRig, and arm 7 is structurally blind to it because the declaring file is already under an addon root -- the selection instrument could not see the only cross-system reach the membership has. dec. 3: ARM 5 IS 61 LINES, NOT 12, AND A SHAPE SCAN CANNOT SEE THE DIFFERENCE -- `const JobDatabase = ExMateriaAlmanac.JobDatabase` is one line to a class_name token scan and the head of a chain to arm 5, which binds the alias (ADR-0211 dec. 4) and scores every bare use below it; resolved: exmateria_almanac 53 (debt), exmateria_platform 6 (FREE, the port), exmateria_sprite_rig 2 (debt). The 53 are carried by UnitProgression 32, JobDatabase 15, GambitList 4, SpriteDatabase 2 -- the same four names ADR-0241 dec. 5 called this system's blockers; they did not go away, they became addon-resident, which is what took arm 7 to zero. Every selection ADR that quoted a sibling count quoted the FLOOR. dec. 4: exmateria_almanac IS NOT A SYSTEM AND _system_of SAYS IT IS -- arm 5's free set is `system_of[home] is None`, _system_of majority-votes over classify() buckets, classify() books by CONSUMER (ADR-0243 dec. 3), so the almanac's 34 files vote Battle 14 / content 11 / UI 5 and the guard prices every reach into a book of tables (ADR-0251 dec. 1) as a reach into the Battle system, whose 66 files and 24,783 lines are all in src/. Recorded, NOT fixed: widening the free set re-prices three shipped extractions and an audit cannot re-price its own subject mid-pass. dec. 5: EXTRACTION #6 LANDS AS ITS OWN ADDON, not folded into exmateria_almanac, and 55 arm-5 debt lines are NAMED AND ACCEPTED -- folding severs no edge, it stops the guard PRINTING one (ADR-0157 dec. 2 enumerated Battlefield's outbound debt and chose the system anyway); the almanac ships no [autoload] and should not start; the dependency is one-way (nothing in the almanac names any of the ten members) and folding makes every table consumer ship a 480-line mutable Character; and folded, the almanac still votes Battle 14 to 10, so dec. 4's artefact would misattribute the extraction's own report card. These will be the corpus's FIRST arm-5 rows targeting neither the kernel nor the port -- all 337 free rows today target exmateria_schema or exmateria_platform -- and they are DEBT not RED because package_project returns None inside the host project. dec. 6: THE AUTOLOAD MOVES WITH THE ADDON and the [autoload] line stays the host's -- the corpus already autoloads three addon-resident scripts (PSXDisplay, ExMateriaAudioEngine, ExMateriaEffectSfx) and autoload_route_reaches exempts an own singleton by design. The '35 host lines' ADR-0257 dec. 7 priced were NEVER ANY ARM'S SUBJECT (34 here, plus 161 test lines): every arm scans files UNDER an addon root. The member-side exposure is TWO lines in two spellings -- Character.gd:209 `CharacterCatalog.get_character(slug)` (arm 2's dotted predicate) and AllTemplatesSeeder.gd:184 `catalog = CharacterCatalog`, BARE, which NO arm can see (arm 2 requires the dot, arm 2b requires a get_node string) -- a fourth spelling after #648's third. A node-path soft-bind reads free where a bare dot reads as debt, so the TunePort shape (ADR-0175 dec. 2, BUILT BY ADR-0187 -- ADR-0243 dec. 10's attribution to ADR-0234 is wrong) needs three verbs here against TunePort's six; pass 3 picks between the port and injection, since seed(catalog = null) already injects. dec. 7: THE FACADE PUBLISHES NINE NAMES AND tests/ IS WHY IT IS NOT SEVEN -- UnitBirthdays and UnitNames are named only from five test files, ADR-0211 dec. 5 rules a test-only host use a real host use, and tests/ is not in classify_blueprint.WALK_ROOTS, so the selecting instrument cannot see two of the names its facade must publish; 9 constants and 59 alias lines against #945's 32 names; CharacterCatalog is reached in 63 files and needs no alias because it is the [autoload] line. dec. 8: THE PANELS ARE FOUR AND 643 LINES, NOT FIVE AND 750, AND ONE IS DEAD -- ProgressionDebugPanel 420, BattleBindingDebugPanel 92, RosterUniverseDebugPanel 70, RosterViewDebugPanel 61; StoryTimelineDebugPanel (175) books to Campaign, which is where the fifth came from; raisers are NavigatorMain.gd:483,498 and GPUArena.gd:851 (#1025's GPUArena.gd:1201 is past the end of a 959-line file); RosterViewDebugPanel is instantiated by NOTHING and GPUArena.gd:896 says so in a comment. dec. 9: ARM 6 IS SEVEN LINES OVER SIX PATHS AND ONE CANNOT TRAVEL -- five payloads' only other readers are tests/ and the tools/ scripts that GENERATE them, but assets/sprites/textures/ (AllTemplatesSeeder.gd:50) is shared with exmateria_sprite_rig and four host files, so it stays a host content root and the row SURVIVES the move, paid by ADR-0202's host-injected content_root already in project.godot twice. This refutes #1025's 'zero code coupling / cheapest arm-6 set' for the sixth row. All seven rows are const-shaped so this arm is EXACT rather than a floor, proved by a control test, not excused by name. dec. 10: TWO INSTRUMENTS SHIP AND NEITHER IS A GUARD -- tools/membership_arms.py (all nine arms over a proposed membership, three of them the guard's OWN functions handed a _FileSet rather than a second implementation, per ADR-0148) and tools/selection_sweep.py (the per-system sweep that had survived twice only as a /tmp file); no pass/fail, so check_guard_registry wants no row for either. The one-implementation rule was not stylistic: the first cut hand-rolled arm 4's push regex and read ZERO on exmateria_platform, whose pushes are spelled with a StringName literal. 26 tests, every agreement test paired with a liveness witness on a non-empty set, because goal #5 is enforcing and a naive comparison against a green guard is 0 == 0 and passes for a stub. dec. 11: NOT DECIDED -- the addon name and layout, port versus injection, the ARM1_BURN_DOWN wording, the dead panel's retirement, and whether arm 5 should stop asking _system_of. Soft spots: S1 the 61 is measured in src/ and the guard will run it in addons/ (it can only go up); S2 dec. 4's defect is left live and will price extraction #7 the same way -- filed as #1059, which found the vote is ONE line (check_addon_portability.py:983) whose own comment at :1073 already states the tier-membership semantics it stands in for, and that plugin.cfg already carries declared measured keys (engine=, ADR-0194 dec. 7; deps=) so a declared tier needs no new mechanism; S3 the inbound reading is a \b<name>\b scan that over-reads ordinary words -- the first cut counted five shader // comments strip_noncode does not strip; S4 nothing here has met a stranger rig, and five payloads travel. S5 NEITHER INSTRUMENT IS INVOKED BY run_all_tests.sh -- ADR-0257 shipped test_arm7_membership.py with no invocation and its dec. 3 pin has drifted 17 to 18 unobserved; test_membership_arms.py inherits the gap. Wiring them in cannot precede ruling the pin (#770; check_guard_registry's three OWED notes). Filed as #1055. |
| [0267](0267-the-catalogue-lands-and-the-ninth-published-name-was-a-symbol-census-that-could-not-see-a-path.md) The catalogue lands, and the ninth published name was a symbol census that could not see a path | `method` | · | · | 1 | `0126`, `0135`, `0146`, `0167`, `0184`, `0194`, `0202`, `0203`, `0211`, `0212`, `0241`, … |
| | | | | | THE CATALOGUE LANDS, AND THE NINTH PUBLISHED NAME WAS A SYMBOL CENSUS THAT COULD NOT SEE A PATH (#1025 pass 3, extraction #6's git mv). The ten ADR-0257 dec. 2 members leave src/characters/, src/scenarios/ and src/debug/ for addons/exmateria_catalogue/ -- 1,658 lines, six member directories, class_name ExMateriaCatalogue -- shed every class_name onto the folder-named facade (ADR-0212 dec. 1) and are aliased back into 46 host files on 71 `const X = ExMateriaCatalogue.X` lines (ADR-0211 dec. 4). THREE OF ADR-0262'S FIVE PRICED NUMBERS WERE WRONG AND EACH FOR A KEEPABLE REASON. dec. 7's NINE published names is TEN: the nine came from a census of class_name declarations, which is exactly the set a shed converts one-for-one, and `CharacterCatalog` reaches the facade by a preload PATH that no symbol census can see -- ten shed and ten published is two different routes agreeing, which is the thing to distrust. 59 alias lines is 71 over 46 files; the 59 came from a string-stripping regex that ran across newlines and blanked live code, and two offsetting errors landed it on a number matching the audit's. dec. 9's ONE unmovable content root is TWO -- assets/characters/templates/ is git check-ignore-matched, untracked and a symlink, exactly like assets/sprites/textures/. dec. 2: the autoload TRAVELS, plugin.gd registers it, and both host reach sites are paid by NODE PATH, so arms 2 AND 2b read ZERO where dec. 6 priced two lines. dec. 3: ONE ARM1_BURN_DOWN row (templates/CharacterTemplateResolver.gd:44 -> ExMateriaSpriteRig, #1071) which is SEVERABLE AND NOT SEVERED HERE -- inlining it forks a rule the rig centralises, and ADR-0167 says a behavioural change does not ride inside an address move. dec. 5: four payloads travel (ADR-0251 dec. 2), three lines over two trees are injected through install/CatalogueContent.gd whose default is EMPTY on purpose, arm 6 goes 6 paths / 7 lines -> 0. dec. 6: classify() RE-BOOKS NOTHING -- origin/main's classify() over the old paths and this tree's over the new agree row for row (#744). dec. 8: soft spot S1 discharged EXACTLY -- arm 5 resolved is 61 (almanac 53, platform 6, sprite_rig 2), row for row and line number for line number with the src/ reading, which says the arm-5 number is a property of what the files REACH and not of where they sit. 🔴 dec. 9: THE SEVENTH STRANGER RIG FAILED ON ITS FIRST RUN AND FOUND THREE DEFECTS NO INSTRUMENT IN tools/ CAN REACH -- ADR-0262 S4 arriving in the exact shape it was written in. (1) Shedding `class_name Character` left nine lines inside Character.gd naming a type that no longer exists; the HOST tree does not report it because .godot/global_script_class_cache.cfg:227 still registers `Character` against res://src/characters/Character.gd, a path this commit deletes, so every host suite parses off a stale entry. Paid with a self-preload, the corpus idiom. (2) rig.sh staged `deps=` as a LINE, not a CLOSURE -- indistinguishable from correct for six rigs because every declared dep in the corpus was a LEAF, and the catalogue is the first addon whose deps have deps (it names exmateria_sprite_rig, which names exmateria_schema). (3) `engine=` was read the same way: the catalogue truthfully declares stock and its closure contains two fork addons, so the DECLARATION stays the subject's and the RUN follows the closure. Widening the catalogue's own deps= or engine= was REJECTED -- it would make plugin.cfg, the one document the rig trusts, claim a reach the addon does not have. Final: 33 passed / 0 failed / 32 files / PASS, fork-absence arm green; almanac, sprite_rig and schema rigs re-run to say nothing moved rather than assume it. dec. 10: BOTH PINS RE-TAKEN, and THREE tests had their PREMISE expire rather than their value -- TwoSpellingsOfOneAutoload was seeded on the very reach dec. 2 paid and would now assert [] == [] (re-based on PerfMonitor, live at 5 dotted / 4 bare); TestsAreNotOutsideTheFacade read the surface off ma.inbound(), which after the shed is structurally unable to see this addon and returns {CharacterCatalog} for every caller (re-expressed against the alias lines, finding unchanged at SEVEN from src/ vs TEN tree-wide); test_the_green_sentence_carries_arm_1_unqualified was written the day ARM1_BURN_DOWN went empty and is re-pinned as an IF AND ONLY IF in both directions. test_a_debug_helper_that_is_not_a_panel_stays was passing VACUOUSLY -- assertNotIn on a path the move deleted is true for every possible detector -- and is re-pointed with an is_file() guard. SOFT SPOTS: S1 plugin.gd books infrastructure and the facade does not, and the stated reason (no behaviour) is false of the facades too -- majority followed, disagreement written down rather than settled. S2 the rig proves the four payloads TRAVELLED and does NOT exercise the branch where a host DOES declare a content_root. S3 neither pin is in run_all_tests.sh (#1055); both are green now, which removes the obstacle without ruling the pin. S4 the 71 was counted by a fixed version of the scanner that produced the wrong 59, and agreement between a scanner and its own output is weaker than it looks -- the check that settles it is a parse, not a grep. NOT DECIDED: #1059 (arm 5's free set), #1070 (RosterViewDebugPanel), #1071 (the palette edge), #1055 (wiring the pins). |
| [0271](0271-the-tier-is-declared-in-plugin-cfg-because-a-vote-over-consumers-cannot-see-a-fourth-tier.md) The tier is declared in plugin.cfg, because a vote over consumers cannot see a fourth tier | `method` | · | · | 11 | `0115`, `0131`, `0139`, `0169`, `0194`, `0202`, `0205`, `0223`, `0232`, `0243`, `0262`, … |
| | | | | | THE TIER IS DECLARED IN plugin.cfg, BECAUSE A VOTE OVER CONSUMERS CANNOT SEE A FOURTH TIER (#1059 phase 1; ADR-0262 soft spot S2 discharged). check_addon_portability's arms 1, 2b, 4b and 5 all read `system_of[addon] is None` as a TIER MEMBERSHIP TEST, but the value was a majority vote over classify() buckets, and classify() books a file to the system that CONSUMES it (ADR-0243 dec. 3) -- so a package of tables inherits its readers' names and exmateria_almanac scored as Battle's addon. THE VOTE IS WRONG IN TWO DIRECTIONS AND A COUNT OF THE ALMANAC'S BUCKETS SEES ONLY ONE: confident-and-wrong for the almanac (Battle 14 / content 11 / UI 5 / infrastructure 2 / generated 2), and BLIND for both EXTRACTED roots -- classify() returns None for all 163 exmateria_sound + exmateria_spu source files, so the modal vote returns None, which IS the free set; only _walk_roots.EXTRACTED naming two paths by hand stands between that and a free verdict. dec. 1: tier="kernel"|"port"|"system"|"rules" is DECLARED in the package's own plugin.cfg beside engine= (ADR-0194 dec. 7) and deps=, for engine='s reason -- a rig that STAGES the package can read it and a Python table in the host does not travel; it is ADR-0202 dec. 4's shape (plugin.cfg could not express a dependency, so the README and the register carried the claim) arriving at the one key that had nowhere to live. Nine roots, nine declarations; reader is _walk_roots.declared_tier(). dec. 2: #1059's OWN NUMBERS ARE CORRECTED AND THE CORRECTIONS WEAKEN ITS ARGUMENT -- 'nine of eleven systems reach it' is SEVEN (nine was a count of BUCKETS; assembler and content are not systems), and 'top two within 1%' is 3.4%. Re-measured through the facade on this tree: 698 lines / 140 rows -- Battle 274, UI 265, assembler 69, Character Catalogue 56, content 11, Cutscene 8, Audio 6, Effects 6, Campaign 3 (Catalogue's 56 exists because ADR-0267 moved src/characters/). This is ADR-0115 dec. 4's content shadow, except it is the union of seven shadows plus two non-system buckets, which is nobody's shadow in particular. dec. 3: an undeclared tier RAISES (extracted_roots()'s rule) and a `; tier=` COMMENT is not a declaration -- there is NO fallback to the vote, because a fallback fires exactly on the day a new addon joins the walk, which is the case this exists to catch. dec. 4: `rules` is a fourth tier, almanac its only member -- the corpus named all four already; what it could not say was that a package is NONE of a system, the kernel or the port. dec. 5: `rules` IS NOT FREE, PRICED NOT ASSERTED -- freeing it takes arm 5 from 354 free / 53 debt to 407 free / ZERO -- the block stops existing, heading and all; it was drafted as 55 -> 2 and BOTH halves moved when #1071 landed first (ADR-0272), because the 2 sprite_rig lines WERE arm 1's row, the alias and the call it fed, so they were never an independent remainder, and that is what #1059 phase 2 is worth, held as a test rather than a paragraph. Not taken today because 581 of the 698 consumed lines (83%) sit on members TWO OR MORE of the eleven reach and only 40% is a *Database at all (GambitCondition 74 = UI 44 + Battle 30; TargetSelector 81 = Battle 43 + UI 34 + assembler 4) -- Battle and UI sharing BEHAVIOUR through a package of tables is ADR-0115 dec. 6's warning, whose own example is gambits. A wrong bucket is falsifiable; silence is not. dec. 6: two questions, two maps -- tier_of is declared, system_of keeps the vote and returns None for every non-system tier, free[] is spelled ONCE; the free set having been spelled four times in four arms is why widening it looked like a one-line fix. 🔴 dec. 7: ARM 1'S GATE MOVES TO THE TIER, AND LEAVING IT ON THE NAME WOULD HAVE SILENCED THE ARM OVER A WHOLE ADDON -- system_of[almanac] goes Battle -> None, and arm 1's `if system is None: continue` would have taken it out of arm 1's SUBJECT, which is this guard's own founding defect (green because it stopped looking). Only the two FREE tiers are outside arm 1; guarded by a seeded test that writes a file naming Unit into the real addon and requires Battle.Unit to still print. dec. 8: THE VERDICT DOES NOT MOVE and that is the claim -- output BYTE-IDENTICAL before and after (354 free / 53 debt / rc=0), RE-TAKEN on the rebased tree against a fresh origin/main worktree and identical but for the absolute path in a SyntaxWarning line, diffed not eyeballed, under ADR-0223 dec. 3's NOTHING-MOVES gate. dec. 9: the declaration also covers the two EXTRACTED roots, where the vote was blind rather than wrong. dec. 10: --root/--system declares tier="system" by hand. 🔴 dec. 10 (finding): `ADR-0139 dec. 9, dec. 12` DOES NOT CONTAIN THE FREE-SET RULE that four comments in check_addon_portability.py attribute to it -- dec. 9 is 'Name and place: addons/exmateria_schema/' and dec. 12 is about Tune having no kernel member; the rule is STATED in ADR-0202 dec. 2, which is where the guard borrowed the attribution, and ADR-0139 dec. 8 is 'the kernel is not a system'. A SECOND INDEPENDENT INSTANCE was found in the same pass: ADR-0223 dec. 3 AND ADR-0232 item 1 both cite 'ADR-0205 dec. 7' for the NOTHING-MOVES rule, and ADR-0205 dec. 7 is 'This pass rules and builds the register. It does NOT collapse MapComposer.' -- none of its eight decisions states the rule. Two instances from opening two cited files is a RATE, not an anecdote, and it is precisely what check_adr_anchors cannot see: it checks that a cited ADR NAMES A FILE, not that the decision number exists or says what is claimed, and docs/adr/ is excluded from its citation scan besides. Corrected only in the five lines this diff already writes; the ACCEPTED ADRs are not edited. REJECTED: a TIERS table in tools/_walk_roots.py (engine='s own argument -- a host table does not travel with the addon it describes); declaring the system NAME too (a second unchecked home for a fact EXTRACTED already declares, and the vote is unambiguous for all four in-walk system addons: Battlefield 55/16/1, Sprite Rig 34/1/1, Catalogue 12/1, Render 5/1); freeing `rules` now (dec. 5's 83%); splitting the almanac (ADR-0167 -- a behavioural change does not ride inside an address move; it is #1060); special-casing the almanac by name (a name list IS the inference wearing a list); falling back to the vote when undeclared (dec. 3). CONSEQUENCES: adding an addon root now requires a tier or the guard raises; plugin.cfg grows a third non-standard key, inert to tests/stranger/shared/rig.sh's two anchored seds (read, not run); tools/score_goals.py:668 STILL HOLDS A SECOND COPY OF THE VOTE, unreached for the almanac only because docs/GOALS.tsv carries Render/Battlefield/Sprite Rig/Audio -- currently correct and structurally able to be wrong; register 69 -> 80 arms. SOFT SPOTS: S1 exmateria_spu is declared `system` as measured and its own description reads like a port -- today's free rows out of exmateria_sound are free on arm 5's SAME-PACKAGE ground (package_project(home) == own), not a tier ground, so re-declaring would change no count and would change what the count MEANS; a tier declaration must not smuggle a re-pricing. S2 the system NAME is still a vote and the agreement test asserts one bit, not the margin. S3 the 698 is a FLOOR (ADR-0131 dec. 6) -- facade-resolved, .gd only, duck-typed reaches invisible. S4 the tier is declared but its MEMBERSHIP is unchecked (#1059 phase 3), and the ticket already names three members that pass the almanac README's predicate's letter and fail its intent (UnitProgression, GambitList, AbilityLoadout hold per-playthrough state). S5 dec. 10's sweep is not swept and the instrument is HALF-BUILT, not absent -- tools/check_adr_quotes.py substring-matches every *"..."* span against the ADR cited ON THE SAME LINE and is live on this file (a seeded fabrication at 0271:312 was flagged, so its zero here is measured), but it is blind to BOTH findings because neither carries a quoted span: a bare (ADR-0139 dec. 9, dec. 12) is an attribution with nothing to match. 1 of this ADR's 38 ADR-NNNN mentions is on a line with a quotable span; all 48 of its `dec. N` references are unchecked. The missing half is a resolver from ADR-NNNN dec. K to a heading in the cited file, cheap because every decision here is a `**K. ...**` line. Not filed. NOT DECIDED: #1059 phases 2 and 3, #1060, #1071, and the encounters/EntdPositionDatabase un-publish. |
| [0272](0272-the-palette-row-edge-is-paid-by-deleting-the-key-because-no-consumer-read-it.md) The palette-row edge is paid by deleting the key, because no consumer read it | `method` | · | · | 6 | `0022`, `0053`, `0072`, `0167`, `0175`, `0183`, `0184`, `0196`, `0211`, `0262`, `0267` |
| | | | | | THE PALETTE-ROW EDGE IS PAID BY DELETING THE KEY, BECAUSE NO CONSUMER READ IT (#1071). ARM1_BURN_DOWN's last row -- exmateria_catalogue/templates/CharacterTemplateResolver.gd -> ExMateriaSpriteRig for one call, job_body_palette_row(job), put back by #1025 pass 3 after ADR-0184 dec. 4's six were paid -- is paid by DELETING THE KEY, not by the injected port the ticket proposed, and ARM1_BURN_DOWN is EMPTY for the second time. dec. 2 IS THE MEASUREMENT THE TICKET ONLY HALF-STATED: body_palette_row was a PASS-THROUGH no consumer read -- a three-row consumer table, UnitSpawn.build and FormationScene.resolve_body_render both hand the value straight to a sprite-layer setter and both already hold the Character the row is a function of, and the third caller (CharacterRosterParityTest) only compared it to itself. So the two callers make the one call the resolver was making on their behalf. dec. 3: it COSTS THE HOST NOTHING because a host->addon reach is not any arm's subject -- ADR-0262's Context measures it, 'Every arm of check_addon_portability.py scans files UNDER AN ADDON ROOT'. dec. 4: the rule still lives in ONE place, SpritePaletteResolver.job_body_palette_row, which is what ADR-0022's job axis is for; two call sites is not two rules. dec. 5: THE PORT WAS REJECTED ON A FACT, NOT TASTE -- resolve() is static, so injection means either a static registry (global mutable state plus an init-order hazard) or a threaded parameter, which is MORE coupling than the caller making the call. 🔴 dec. 6: the ticket's own DoD item could not be met as written. dec. 7: FormationScene.resolve_body_render's palette_row had NO test at all -- found while writing the severance, covered now by CombatBodyPaletteRowTest 8 -> 12. dec. 8: the caveat test is RE-SHAPED as an if-and-only-if with a seeded reach for the positive direction, because a SIZE pin on ARM1_BURN_DOWN has now been wrong on BOTH sides (empty when it was not, non-empty when it was) -- mutation-proved in both directions by if burn: -> if True: / if False:. 🔴 dec. 9: ADR-0167 DOES NOT CONTAIN THE SENTENCE FOUR DOCUMENTS ATTRIBUTE TO IT -- #1071's body, ADR-0267 dec. 3, the burn-down row's own comment and the catalogue README all render it as 'a behavioural change does not ride inside an address move', whose only source is ADR-0267 (:54, :131, :271); ADR-0167 says 'riding inside a BOOKING CORRECTION makes the booking's own numbers unattributable', and ADR-0175:316 and ADR-0183:188 both quote that correctly. Accepted ADRs NOT edited. 🔴 dec. 10: addons/exmateria_battlefield/README.md:118 asserted ARM1_BURN_DOWN is EMPTY for 68 commits in which it was not. ALTERNATIVES: the injected port (dec. 5); a static registry; threading the resolver as a parameter; inlining the row rule into the catalogue (forks a rule the rig centralises); widening arm 1's free set (that is #1059, not this). SOFT SPOTS: S3 records an AllTemplatesSeederTest A/B -- 2 failures (39/37, 18/16) proved PRE-EXISTING by restoring origin/main's three runtime files and re-running to a byte-identical 477 passed / 2 failed. dec. 11: THE SEVERANCE MADE TWO REGISTERS STALE AND BOTH WERE FOUND BY THE REGISTER, NEITHER OF THEM check_addon_portability -- exmateria_sprite_rig.gd:231 cited tests/CharacterRosterParityTest.gd as a host use of SpritePaletteResolver and this pass deleted that file's alias, which check_addon_globals' citation-rot arm caught and which reddened THIRTEEN of its tests at once because each seeds on top of the shipped tree; and test_membership_arms' Arm1HasALiveSubject had the catalogue's one reach as its subject. dec. 12: ARM 1 NOW HAS NO LIVE SUBJECT ANYWHERE, so its witness is SEEDED -- the first control in the corpus that could NOT be re-based (TwoSpellingsOfOneAutoload moved to PerfMonitor; this one has nowhere to move, because goal #5 fully met is a state the tree is meant to STAY in), written under the catalogue root and deleted again, both directions asserted, mutation-proved by stubbing arm 1 to {} (3 of 5 arms red; the two that stay green are the paid-direction arm and the leak arm). CONSEQUENCE: #1059's inherited debt is 53 lines, NOT 55 -- sprite_rig's two arm-5 resolved lines WERE arm 1's row, the alias and the call it fed; ADR-0262 dec. 5 and its :126 table, ADR-0267's soft-spot note and the handoff all quote 55 and are NOT edited here. Catalogue shape/resolved 12/61 -> 11/59, membership 1,673 -> 1,676 lines. |
| [0273](0273-free-ness-inside-the-rules-tier-is-declared-per-member-because-a-package-of-tables-is-not-all-tables.md) Free-ness inside the `rules` tier is declared per member, because a package of tables is not all tables | `method` | · | · | 6 | `0115`, `0131`, `0167`, `0175`, `0212`, `0241`, `0262`, `0267`, `0271`, `0272` |
| | | | | | FREE-NESS INSIDE THE `rules` TIER IS DECLARED PER MEMBER, BECAUSE A PACKAGE OF TABLES IS NOT ALL TABLES (#1059 phase 2; ADR-0271's "Not decided" entry for phase 2 discharged). ADR-0271 gave every addon a declared tier= and left `rules` outside _walk_roots.PORTABLE_TIERS, so check_addon_portability arm 5 printed EVERY sibling reach into exmateria_almanac -- 53 lines, all exmateria_catalogue's. dec. 5 priced the wholesale fix and rejected it: 83% of the almanac's ~700 consumed lines sit on members two or more of the eleven reach, and "a wrong bucket is a claim someone can falsify, and silence is not". THIS ADR SPLITS FREE-NESS BY MEMBER instead, on the line ADR-0115 dec. 7 draws -- "the driver ships, the banks are content". dec. 1: every member a `rules` facade publishes declares one of three kinds in `const MEMBER_KINDS` -- table (a bank; every answer is STORED; reading it is ADR-0115 dec. 4's content shadow; FREE), rule (a driver; it COMPUTES what the ROM computes rather than stores; PRINTED), state (a unit's own numbers, not a fact about the game; PRINTED). _walk_roots.MEMBER_FREE_KINDS is {table}. The almanac reads 17 table / 11 rule / 3 state. Arm 5 goes 354 free / 53 debt -> 371 / 36. dec. 2: it goes in the FACADE, not plugin.cfg -- ADR-0271 dec. 1's two arguments (a rig stages the package; a host table does not travel) both still hold and neither selects plugin.cfg here, and the facade is where each member is already named exactly once, so 31 names in an ini value would be the second unreconciled register ADR-0271 refused to create for the system NAME. dec. 3: arm 5 asks the free question of the MEMBER (member_free(home, name)); arms 1, 2b and 4b keep reading free[] unchanged, which is what keeps Arm1HasALiveSubject's seeded witness live -- verified by an A/B revert that reddens exactly four arms. Arm 5 is the only one keyed on the reach's TARGET rather than its SUBJECT and the only one holding a member name, which is why the seam is there and nowhere else. dec. 4: state IS DECLARED AND DECLARING IT DOES NOT SETTLE IT -- UnitProgression, GambitList and AbilityLoadout are ADR-0271 S4's three, they are 36 of the 53 lines, and ADR-0241 dec. 1 already ruled UnitProgression the Character Catalogue's BY OWNERSHIP. Where they live is #1059 phase 3 and #1060; a behavioural change does not ride inside a declaration (ADR-0167). dec. 5: TIES GO TO `rule` -- a member wrongly called rule costs a printed line somebody can falsify, one wrongly called table costs SILENCE. StatusEncoder, ElementEncoder and UnitRole are the three close calls and all three are rule; none has an arm-5 row, so the tie-break costs nothing on this tree and is recorded before its first use. dec. 6: FOUND WHILE VERIFYING THE CITATIONS -- ADR-0115 dec. 6 DOES NOT SAY "TWO". Four sites paraphrase it as "a feature threads two systems"; dec. 6 reads "A feature is not a system. Gambits thread through three systems; so does equipment." Third instance of ADR-0271 dec. 10's finding and the first that is a NUMBER rather than a decision reference, which is why check_adr_quotes could not see it (a bare paraphrase carries no *"..."* span to match). Corrected in the two live-source sites this diff reads; ADR-0271's own is left. dec. 7: THE REGISTER IS PROVED BY MUTATION, not by a green guard -- declaring UnitProgression a table must take the block 36 -> 4 and the free block 371 -> 403; declaring it a rule must move NOTHING, because rule and state are different claims and the same verdict. dec. 8: both directions reconciled BEFORE the first row is scored and a mismatch RAISES; a published member with no row and a row naming an unpublished member both raise (#424). Never a .get(member, "table"). dec. 9: the UNRESOLVED FACADE is not free -- sibling_class_reaches resolves an unpublished member to the bare ExMateriaAlmanac, which names an INTERNAL and is a worse dependency than a published table. CONTEXT FINDING: THE ARGUMENT FOR PRINTING THE 53 PRICES A DIFFERENT REGISTER FROM THE ONE ARM 5 READS. ADR-0271 dec. 5's 83% is measured over src/ PLUS every sibling addon root; arm 5 scans addon->addon only, so the four members dec. 5 names (GambitCondition, TargetSelector, Gambit, UnitRole -- 239 lines here) contribute ZERO arm-5 rows today. That does not make dec. 5 wrong -- the eleven are destined to become addons -- but its cost is a FORWARD cost. Re-measured: 706 lines over 30 members against dec. 2's 698 (a floor either way, ADR-0131 dec. 6). REJECTED: adding rules to PORTABLE_TIERS (ADR-0271 dec. 5's rejected alternative, rejected again with a second reason -- it is not even a smaller number, the total is 407 rows either way because arm 5 prints its free block, so the whole decision is which HEADING a row appears under; a handoff into this ticket read its pricing TEST as phase 2's specification, and a tripwire and a target are indistinguishable from the assertion alone); inferring the kind from a *Database name (nine members are spelled that and all nine are table, but there are SEVENTEEN tables, so the proxy under-counts by eight with a perfect record on the nine it sees) or from the subdirectory (six of the eight hold more than one kind; abilities/ holds all three); two kinds with no state; splitting the almanac (#1060's subject); declaring the kind in plugin.cfg; freeing a member by COUNTING ITS CONSUMERS (the majority-vote inference ADR-0271 dec. 1 removed, rebuilt at member level -- and it inverts on contact with success, booking a new consumer as a new defect). SOFT SPOTS: S1 the 17 freed lines are still PRINTED and free is a heading rather than a discharge -- the catalogue still does not parse without the almanac. S2 `rule` reads ZERO on arm 5 and the arm has no positive control for that branch on this tree, because the members that would produce those rows are consumed by src/, which arm 5 does not scan. S3 the kind is declared and its TRUTH is not checked -- ADR-0271 S4 one level in. S4 dec. 5's tie-break has no live subject either. |
| [0280](0280-gambits-stays-in-the-almanac-and-the-tests-that-said-otherwise-were-measuring-a-kernel-vocabulary-in-the-wrong-package.md) `gambits/` stays in the almanac, and the tests that said otherwise were measuring a kernel vocabulary in the wrong package | `method` | · | · | 12 | `0115`, `0118`, `0139`, `0164`, `0167`, `0241`, `0251`, `0262`, `0271`, `0272`, `0273` |
| | | | | | GAMBITS/ STAYS IN THE ALMANAC, AND THE TESTS THAT SAID OTHERWISE WERE MEASURING A KERNEL VOCABULARY IN THE WRONG PACKAGE (#1060, wayfinder:grilling, closed). ADR-0251 dec. 1 flagged the almanac's name as its pass's one taste call; #1060 asked whether the four gambits/ members should leave instead. dec. 1: THEY STAY, AND REP DECIDES IT -- gambits/ passes ADR-0115 dec. 3's asymmetry test (zero back-edges: nothing in the almanac's other 28 files names a gambits member) and dec. 6's one-import test, but FAILS dec. 1, because Gambit.gd names AbilityDatabase on three lines so the extracted addon would declare deps="exmateria_almanac" and could never install without the package it left. THE NUMBERS NEVER BLOCKED IT: post-dec. 3 the extraction creates ZERO new debt lines. What blocks it is that splitting CREATES no edge, the mirror of ADR-0262 dec. 5's 'folding severs no edge; it stops the guard printing one' -- the 252 lines are HOST reaches, bucketed identically either way, and arm 5 has never seen one. dec. 2: GambitList does NOT move to the Character Catalogue alone -- catalogue->GambitList is 4 lines (Character.gd:53,108,191,461) and GambitList->Gambit is 9 (:12,15,18,22,45,51,89,97,126), so the move is +5 under every destination; it travels with Gambit or not at all, and ADR-0241 dec. 1's ownership argument does not reach it. dec. 3: UnitRole IS ADMITTED TO THE SHARED KERNEL AS ADR-0118 dec. 1's ELEVENTH ROW -- 47 lines, an enum plus an enum->string table plus a member list plus a two-line predicate, NO derivation (the 'derived from job type' in its docstring is JobDatabase.get_job_role, a different file), and FFT has no roles at all. A FILE MOVE CANNOT DO THIS: ADR-0139 dec. 3 makes an ADR the gate and ADR-0164 dec. 2 says 'the type cannot be written until the schema is named here', so the row comes first. Both of ADR-0139 dec. 4's vetoes pass by inspection -- sink veto with outbound edge count 0 (no preload, no load, names nothing it does not declare), autoload veto by construction. First schema row whose producer is a TIER rather than a system. dec. 4: Gambit's ~150-line English-rendering surface (_get_noun_string, _get_team_string, _build_when_line, _action_display_name, _build_action_line, _resolution_to_friendly_string, _to_string, GambitCondition.get_sentence_text) is UI's and moves -- ONE external consumer, src/ui3/detail/GambitOptions.gd, on two lines, and both AbilityDatabase call sites live inside it. Booked as its own change so it is not a fix booked into the bucket it drains (ADR-0167). dec. 5: the name exmateria_almanac is RATIFIED and stops being taste -- dec. 3 moves the composition TOWARD 'a book of tables you look things up in' (the one vocabulary member leaves; 31 remain, 18 table / 10 rule / 3 state). exmateria_rules rejected: ~700 call sites, zero measurement change, and it collides with the tier the package declares. dec. 6: the catalogue's 36 arm-5 lines are ADR-0262 dec. 5's ACCEPTED PRICE, not a countdown -- 55 -> 53 (ADR-0272) -> 36 (ADR-0273) by three rulings and none set a target of zero; two consecutive handoffs read it as one. dec. 7: census re-taken at f60bb3290 -- 252 lines, UI 125 / Battle 119 (Gambit 35/26, GambitCondition 35/46, TargetSelector 44/41 + assembler 4, GambitList 5/12 + catalogue 4). #1060's table says 240 with Battle ahead; the ordering is REVERSED. Instrument is a re-implementation, not the guard -- sibling_class_reaches answers addon->addon and this is host->addon (soft spot S1). dec. 8: THREE OF #1060's PREMISES ARE REFUTED -- the title's 'two systems' (ADR-0115 dec. 6 says three; same defect as ADR-0273 dec. 6, fourth instance), the 'JobDatabase drag' (not reached at all), and GambitList's independence (+5). Register proved by MUTATION per ADR-0273 dec. 7: baseline 373 free / 36 debt / 12 shader-global rc 0; UnitRole as table moves NOTHING and reds exactly one test; positive control UnitProgression as table takes it to 405 / 4, the predicted numbers to the line. ANOTHER PARAPHRASE FOUND, NOT #1060's: #1059's phase-1 table cites 'ADR-0139 -- a shared vocabulary is not coupling' and ADR-0139 contains no such sentence; drafting dec. 3 from it would have skipped the admission gate entirely. dec. 9: DOES NOT DECIDE (i) whether UnitProgression/AbilityLoadout move to the catalogue (#1059 phase 3; ADR-0241 dec. 2's doubling still unanswered; #1123's EquipSlot/AbilitySlot lift is preparation and NOT approval), (ii) whether the 36 should ever reach zero, (iii) whether a second rules-tier addon may exist -- dec. 1's third option (sever the rendering, then extract) is DECLINED on today's evidence rather than forbidden, and dec. 4 is what would make it cheap. |
| [0281](0281-a-test-process-has-no-tune-staging-file-and-the-runner-brackets-the-run.md) A test process has no Tune staging file, and the runner brackets the run | `method` | · | · | 3 | `0051`, `0068`, `0158`, `0254` |
| | | | | | A TEST PROCESS HAS NO TUNE STAGING FILE, AND THE RUNNER BRACKETS THE RUN (#1149). config/tune_overrides.json is MACHINE state -- one human's F3 pins, on one box, untracked since 90e593900 -- and it has poisoned this repo three times (#614 ten pins to one; 90e593900 three blocked pulls; #1149 nine false reds mid-suite, attributed to the branch under test). THE LEAK WAS ONE EARLY RETURN: TileOverlayConfigTuneTest's own snapshot/restore opened with `if not _overrides_existed: return`, so the ONE transition it never repaired was absent->present -- harmless while the file was tracked and always present, and the only reachable case once untracking made absence normal. The defence and the defect shipped in the same function and it passed every run it ever made. dec. 1: a test process has NO staging file -- Tune._staging_path is empty, and empty means there is no file, not a different one. dec. 2: a disabled commit SUCCEEDS, so the in-memory baseline still advances and is_dirty/Pin/Reset are unchanged; the single difference is that no bytes reach disk. dec. 3: ONE SEAM CUTS BOTH DIRECTIONS -- the boot load_overrides() read (four Navigator tests carry a hand-written pin against one slug each) and the AUTOSAVE TuneField write resolve through the same member, which is why the remedy is not a per-test snapshot/restore. dec. 4: 'am I a test?' is read off OS.get_cmdline_args() for a res://tests/ prefix -- the one thing every invocation form shares; NOT an env var (ADR-0051). dec. 5: an explicit path always wins, so TuneTest/TuneFieldTest/TuneColorTest/ScenarioActiveIdAutosaveTest keep round-tripping real temp files. dec. 6: the runner COMPARES bytes-or-absence across the run rather than asserting the file is absent, which is what #1149 proposed -- absence is false for a developer with legitimate pins and would make ADR-0068 R6's scrub-then-drain workflow unrunnable beside the suite; what a run may never do is CHANGE it. dec. 7: tools/machine_state_sentinel.py is NOT a check_*.py and carries no guard-registry row, because a pre-flight guard asks about the TREE and this asks what the RUN did -- but BOTH arms invoke it. dec. 8: only the parallel runner scores it red; the sequential arm has never had an exit code and inventing one here would change what --sequential means as a side effect. dec. 9: the test that leaked is now the DIRECTION TEST for the seam -- the repair is deleted and a verdict stands in its place, asserting staging_path() is empty FIRST because 'no file appeared' from a test whose seam never fired is a zero from a blind instrument. REJECTED: fixing the early return (repairs one test, leaves nine AUTOSAVE panels and the whole read direction); asserting absence in the pre-flight (#1149's own proposal -- false for a dev with pins, and a static check cannot see a write that has not happened); redirecting to user:// (shared by all 8 parallel workers, so it recreates the contamination one level down; per-PID names litter ~736 files a run); making TuneField's path mandatory at every call site (a gate per call site, same failure mode). VERIFIED four arms in one worktree on one warm cache: defect (absent -> present, {"tile.selected_type": 2}, 11 passed/0 failed); fixed-absent (absent -> absent, 13/0); fixed-present (md5 d20e0b97 -> d20e0b97, 14/0); SEEDED BREAK `if false and _is_test_process()` reds BOTH new assertions together (11 passed/2 failed, file reappears). The sentinel is direction-tested on its own 2x2 in tools/test_machine_state_sentinel.py (11 arms). SOFT SPOTS: S1 the four Navigator pins are now redundant and are left standing deliberately, named here so a later session retires them in one commit. S2 detection is a string prefix, so an addon-owned test run by path is undetected -- the sentinel is what covers that. S3 one watched path, with the criteria written at the list. S4 the sequential arm reports and does not fail. |
| [0294](0294-the-catalogues-progression-debt-is-a-vocabulary-and-the-kernel-is-where-a-value-set-lives.md) The catalogue's progression debt is a vocabulary, and the kernel is where a value set lives | `method` | · | · | 12 | `0115`, `0118`, `0139`, `0164`, `0196`, `0202`, `0211`, `0213`, `0215`, `0217`, `0241`, … |
| | | | | | THE CATALOGUE'S PROGRESSION DEBT IS A VOCABULARY, AND THE KERNEL IS WHERE A VALUE SET LIVES. ADR-0280 dec. 6 ruled the Character Catalogue's 36 arm-5 lines 'the accepted price, not a countdown' and dec. 9(ii) left the other half open in the same breath -- it 'does not rule that a future pass may not pay them.' This is that pass and it pays 23 of the 36 WITHOUT MOVING A CLASS INTO THE CATALOGUE. dec. 1: THE 36 WERE NEVER 36 REACHES INTO UnitProgression -- read line by line they are 23 lines naming an enum declared inside it (EquipSlot 16, BaseStatType 4, Zodiac 2, plus UnitBirthdays' now-dead alias 1) and 13 holding or constructing the object (UnitProgression 9, GambitList 4). ADR-0241 dec. 1 had already said so -- 'the residual simulator dependency is a vocabulary, not a coupling' -- and nobody had built on it. dec. 2: EquipSlot, BaseStatType and Zodiac are admitted to the shared kernel as ADR-0118 dec. 1's TWELFTH row, the second whose producer is the rules tier rather than a system. ADR-0139 dec. 3 is the gate and an ADR is the only route -- 'a file move cannot do it' -- and two of the three did not exist as files at all, they were enums nested in a ~1,000-line Resource. Both dec. 4 vetoes pass per member by inspection: outbound edge count 0 for all three, extends RefCounted, no autoload. BaseStatType's TABLE DOES NOT COME -- BaseStatsDatabase and StatCalculator name a JsonAsset and would fail the sink veto; what crosses is the three-member label, not the numbers it indexes. ONE ROW, THREE MEMBERS, on ADR-0215 dec. 2's precedent and not ADR-0196's. dec. 3: UnitProgression RE-EXPORTS all three through ADR-0211 dec. 4's alias idiom, so NOT ONE CALL SITE IS RESPELLED -- src/ is not touched by this change at all, where ADR-0217 dec. 7 paid 61 alias declarations for four enums this pays SIX. dec. 4: zodiac_from_birthday travels with the enum because it is ADR-0273 table and not rule -- delete the function and the answer is still sitting in _ZODIAC_MONTH. Boundaries validated against Ramza 12/30 Capricorn, Agrias 6/22 Cancer, Delita 3/18 Pisces, Algus 5/11 Taurus BEFORE the move. dec. 5: AbilitySlot does NOT travel -- ZERO namers outside the almanac and tests/, so it crosses no boundary, realises no row, and dec. 3's gate refuses it on its own terms. #1123 lifted the pair together and only one is admissible. dec. 6: THE MOVE IS DOWN INTO THE KERNEL AND THAT IS WHAT MAKES IT NOT #1059 PHASE 3 -- phase 3 moves the CLASS UP into a system, gated by ADR-0241 dec. 2 and still unanswered; this moves a member's VOCABULARY DOWN, gated by ADR-0139 dec. 3 and answered here. EquipSlot.gd's old docstring objection ('the move would have inverted a declared dependency') prices a move UP and is answered-for-this-destination rather than deleted. The three routes TierDeclarationTests already rejects -- widening PORTABLE_TIERS, a rules exemption, moving UnitProgression -- are none of them taken. dec. 7: the authority to pay is ADR-0280 dec. 9(ii) and dec. 6 SURVIVES INTACT, confirmed by 13 lines left standing with no plan and no ticket. dec. 8: census re-pinned 406/36 -> 424/13, and THE TWO SIDES DO NOT MATCH ON PURPOSE -- free +18 against debt -23, attributed three ways: the 23 re-home to 7 (an alias is ONE line however many use sites it serves), UnitProgression's re-export block is +6 lines that did not exist, and EquipCandidates.gd becomes VISIBLE at +5 (it reached EquipSlot through a same-addon preload, so it was in NEITHER block). UnitBirthdays.gd leaves the debt block ENTIRELY, which is why the residue is 13 and not 14. Mutation control re-taken per ADR-0273 dec. 7: declaring UnitProgression a table was 32 and is now 9 (433/4, GambitList the positive control); declaring it a rule still moves NOTHING. SOFT SPOT: BaseStatType and Zodiac ordinals are @export-ed STORED DATA, so both are moved with explicit integer values and their docstrings say so. |
| [0300](0300-1059-phase-3-is-rejected-because-the-move-retires-nine-arm-5-lines-and-pays-ten-and-one-reach-has-no-published-name.md) #1059 phase 3 is rejected: the move retires nine arm-5 lines and pays ten, and one of the seven reaches has no published name | `method` | · | · | 5 | `0115`, `0118`, `0139`, `0175`, `0241`, `0251`, `0262`, `0271`, `0272`, `0273`, `0280`, … |
| | | | | | #1059 PHASE 3 IS REJECTED: THE MOVE RETIRES NINE ARM-5 LINES AND PAYS TEN, AND ONE OF THE SEVEN REACHES HAS NO PUBLISHED NAME (#1059 phase 3; not built). Phase 2 declared MEMBER_KINDS and named UnitProgression, GambitList and AbilityLoadout `state`, saying explicitly that the kind does not settle where they live; phase 3 was the pass that would move them to exmateria_catalogue. Four blocks stand in front of it and three were already on the record -- ADR-0241 dec. 1 (the catalogue's BY OWNERSHIP) with dec. 2 (applying the ruling doubles the debt) and dec. 3 (the class does not split: re-measured today 41 of 52 functions touch a database, 72 static reaches, no injection point), ADR-0280 dec. 2 (GambitList costs 4 and pays 9) and dec. 6 (the catalogue's arm-5 lines are a price, not a countdown), and exmateria_schema.gd's twelfth-row block (UnitProgression fails ADR-0139 dec. 4(a)'s sink veto on all five database edges). THE FOURTH WAS NEVER PRICED: UnitProgression reaches five databases and StatCalculator by seven raw preload consts, intra-package and free today, cross-addon after a move. dec. 2 prices them against MEMBER_KINDS -- ItemDatabase 21, JobDatabase 13, AbilityDatabase 9, JobLevelsDatabase 8, AbilityType 3 are `table` and free; StatCalculator 10 is `rule` and PRINTED; BaseStatsDatabase 5 has no row in MEMBER_KINDS and no const on the facade at all. The move retires the 9 catalogue lines naming ExMateriaAlmanac.UnitProgression and creates 10, so arm-5 debt goes 13 -> 14 at best. dec. 3: BaseStatsDatabase's publication trigger is 'the day a host file names it' (ADR-0251 dec. 3) and a sibling addon is not that trigger. dec. 4: keeping the raw res:// preloads is the WRONG escape -- arm 6 emits a row only when the target leaves EVERY addon root, so a cross-addon res://addons/ path is invisible to it and to arm 5, and the census would read 13 -> 4 with the coupling unchanged. dec. 5: a move creates NO CYCLE (each of the three is named on exactly two almanac code lines outside its own file -- the facade preload and the MEMBER_KINDS row -- and zero members reach them), but only if the facade DROPS the const; re-exporting it creates a real cycle and costs ADR-0115 dec. 1's granule rule, and dropping it re-points 56 code lines in 55 files. dec. 6: THIRTEEN IS THE FLOOR under this architecture (Character.gd 10 + AllTemplatesSeeder.gd 3) because move, re-kind and re-spell are each closed by a ruling. dec. 7: arm 8 (#1241) checks deps= CORRESPONDENCE, not acyclicity, so the cycle in dec. 5 would pass it. |
